namespace DevExpress.XtraTreeMap
{
	public abstract class BorderBase : TreeMapElement
	{
		private const int DefaultThickness = 1;

		private readonly Color defaultColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultTreeMapBorderColor;

		private readonly bool defaultVisible;

		private Color color;

		private Color skinColor = Color.Empty;

		private bool visible;

		private bool isColorDefault = true;

		private int thickness = 1;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public int ActualThickness => GetActualThickness(Visible);

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public bool IsColorDefault => isColorDefault;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public Color ActualColor
		{
			get
			{
				if (!isColorDefault || skinColor.IsEmpty)
				{
					return color;
				}
				return skinColor;
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[Description("Gets or sets the border color.")]
		public Color Color
		{
			get
			{
				return color;
			}
			set
			{
				if (color != value)
				{
					color = value;
					isColorDefault = color == defaultColor;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(1)]
		[Description("Gets or sets the border’s thickness.")]
		public int Thickness
		{
			get
			{
				return thickness;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value <= 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Incorrect Border Thickness");
				}
				if (thickness != value)
				{
					thickness = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[Description("Specifies whether the border is visible.")]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (visible != value)
				{
					visible = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		protected BorderBase(bool visible, Color color, HierarchicalElement treeMapElement)
			: base(treeMapElement)
		{
			defaultVisible = visible;
			this.visible = visible;
			this.color = color;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeColor()
		{
			return color != defaultColor;
		}

		private void ResetColor()
		{
			Color = defaultColor;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeVisible()
		{
			return visible != defaultVisible;
		}

		private bool ResetVisible()
		{
			return visible = defaultVisible;
		}

		protected internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeColor())
			{
				return ShouldSerializeVisible();
			}
			return true;
		}

		private int GetActualThickness(bool visible)
		{
			if (!visible)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return Thickness;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public void UpdateSkinColor(Color color)
		{
			skinColor = color;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is BorderBase borderBase && borderBase.GetType().Equals(GetType()) && color == borderBase.color && thickness == borderBase.thickness)
			{
				return visible == borderBase.visible;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return "(Border)";
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is BorderBase borderBase)
			{
				color = borderBase.color;
				thickness = borderBase.thickness;
				visible = borderBase.visible;
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public abstract class RectangularBorder : BorderBase
	{
		protected internal enum RectangularBorderItem
		{
			TopItem,
			BottomItem,
			LeftItem,
			RightItem
		}

		internal static void Render(IRenderer renderer, RectangularBorderLayout layout, Color color)
		{
			using DXBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(color);
			renderer.FillRectangle(layout.TopRect, brush);
			renderer.FillRectangle(layout.BottomRect, brush);
			renderer.FillRectangle(layout.LeftRect, brush);
			renderer.FillRectangle(layout.RightRect, brush);
		}

		protected RectangularBorder(bool visible, Color color, HierarchicalElement treeMapElement)
			: base(visible, color, treeMapElement)
		{
		}

		protected internal abstract Rectangle ConstructBorderedRect(RectangularBorderLayout borderLayout);

		protected internal abstract Rectangle CalcBorderItem(Rectangle rect, RectangularBorderItem item, int actualThickness);

		internal void Render(IRenderer renderer, Rectangle rect, int actualThickness, Color actualColor)
		{
			if (base.Visible)
			{
				RectangularBorderLayout layout = new RectangularBorderLayout(this, rect, actualThickness);
				Render(renderer, layout, actualColor);
			}
		}

		internal Rectangle CalcBorderedRect(Rectangle rect)
		{
			RectangularBorderLayout borderLayout = new RectangularBorderLayout(this, rect, base.ActualThickness);
			return ConstructBorderedRect(borderLayout);
		}
	}
	[DesignerSerializer("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.TreeMapItemSerializer,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.ComponentModel.Design.Serialization.CodeDomSerializer, System.Design, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
	public sealed class InsideRectangularBorder : RectangularBorder
	{
		internal InsideRectangularBorder(bool visible, Color color, HierarchicalElement treeMapElement)
			: base(visible, color, treeMapElement)
		{
		}

		protected internal override Rectangle ConstructBorderedRect(RectangularBorderLayout borderLayout)
		{
			Rectangle topRect = borderLayout.TopRect;
			Rectangle bottomRect = borderLayout.BottomRect;
			Rectangle leftRect = borderLayout.LeftRect;
			Rectangle rightRect = borderLayout.RightRect;
			return new Rectangle(leftRect.Location.X + leftRect.Width, topRect.Location.Y + topRect.Height, rightRect.Location.X - leftRect.Location.X - leftRect.Width, bottomRect.Location.Y - topRect.Location.Y - topRect.Height);
		}

		protected internal override Rectangle CalcBorderItem(Rectangle rect, RectangularBorderItem item, int actualThickness)
		{
			return item switch
			{
				RectangularBorderItem.TopItem => new Rectangle(rect.Location.X, rect.Location.Y, rect.Width, actualThickness), 
				RectangularBorderItem.BottomItem => new Rectangle(rect.Location.X, rect.Location.Y + rect.Height - actualThickness, rect.Width, actualThickness), 
				RectangularBorderItem.LeftItem => new Rectangle(rect.Location.X, rect.Location.Y + actualThickness, actualThickness, rect.Height - 2 * actualThickness), 
				RectangularBorderItem.RightItem => new Rectangle(rect.Location.X + rect.Width - actualThickness, rect.Location.Y + actualThickness, actualThickness, rect.Height - 2 * actualThickness), 
				_ => Rectangle.Empty, 
			};
		}
	}
	public enum PaletteScaleMode
	{
		Repeat,
		Extrapolate
	}
	[TypeConverter(typeof(PaletteTypeConverter))]
	public class Palette : NotificationCollection<Color>
	{
		private class ColorIndexCache : ColorIndexCacheCore
		{
			private readonly Dictionary<int, Color> convertedColorCache = new Dictionary<int, Color>();

			public Color ConvertColor(Color color, int index, int actualIndex)
			{
				if (convertedColorCache.TryGetValue(index, out var value))
				{
					return value;
				}
				int actualIndexRepeatCount = GetActualIndexRepeatCount(index, actualIndex);
				value = ((actualIndexRepeatCount == 0) ? color : ConvertColor(color.ToColorARGB(), actualIndexRepeatCount).ToColor());
				convertedColorCache[index] = value;
				return value;
			}
		}

		private const string CustomPaletteName = "Custom Palette";

		private const PaletteScaleMode DefaultScaleMode = PaletteScaleMode.Repeat;

		private readonly ColorIndexCache indexCache = new ColorIndexCache();

		private PaletteScaleMode scaleMode;

		private static Palette officePalette;

		private static Palette office2013Palette;

		private static Palette office2016Palette;

		private static Palette greenYellowPalette;

		private static Palette inAFogPalette;

		private static Palette dXTreeMapPalette;

		private static Palette chameleonPalette;

		private static Palette natureColorsPalette;

		private static Palette marqueePalette;

		private static Palette slipstreamPalette;

		private static Palette office2019Palette;

		protected internal bool IsEmpty => ((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)this).Count == 0;

		public bool IsPredefined { get; internal set; }

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		public bool IsFromSkin { get; set; }

		[Description("Specifies how to distribute colors when their number in the palette is less than the number of chart items.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public PaletteScaleMode ScaleMode
		{
			get
			{
				return scaleMode;
			}
			set
			{
				scaleMode = value;
			}
		}

		public Color this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return ((DXCollection<Color>)(object)this)[index];
			}
			set
			{
				((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)this).InnerList[index] = value;
			}
		}

					yellowPalette = CreatePredefinedPalette(Color.FromArgb(255, 255, 202, 8), Color.FromArgb(255, 248, 147, 29), Color.FromArgb(255, 206, 141, 62), Color.FromArgb(255, 236, 112, 22), Color.FromArgb(255, 230, 72, 35), Color.FromArgb(255, 156, 106, 106));
				}
				return yellowPalette;
			}
		}

		internal static Palette CreatePredefinedPalette(params Color[] colors)
		{
			Palette palette = new Palette();
			palette.AddRange(colors);
			palette.IsPredefined = true;
			return palette;
		}

		public static Palette CreatePalette(params Color[] colors)
		{
			Palette palette = new Palette();
			palette.AddRange(colors);
			return palette;
		}

		protected internal Color GetColor(int index)
		{
			if (IsEmpty)
			{
				return Color.Empty;
			}
			int num = index % ((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)this).Count;
			if (ScaleMode == PaletteScaleMode.Repeat)
			{
				return this[num];
			}
			return indexCache.ConvertColor(this[num], index, num);
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeScaleMode()
		{
			return scaleMode != PaletteScaleMode.Repeat;
		}

		private void ResetScaleMode()
		{
			scaleMode = PaletteScaleMode.Repeat;
		}

		public Palette()
		{
		}

		public Palette(Color[] colors)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < colors.Length; i++)
			{
				Add(colors[i]);
			}
		}

		private void TestIsPredefined()
		{
			if (IsPredefined)
			{
				throw new Exception("Modify Default Palette Error");
			}
		}

		protected override bool OnInsert(int index, Color value)
		{
			TestIsPredefined();
			return base.OnInsert(index, value);
		}

		protected override bool OnRemove(int index, Color value)
		{
			TestIsPredefined();
			return base.OnRemove(index, value);
		}

		internal bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)this).Count > 0)
			{
				return !IsFromSkin;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void Add(Color color)
		{
			TestIsPredefined();
			((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)this).InnerList.Add(color);
		}

		public void AddRange(Color[] colors)
		{
			base.AddRange((ICollection)colors);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			if (!Palettes.ListPalettes.ContainsKey(this))
			{
				return "Custom Palette";
			}
			return Palettes.ListPalettes[this];
		}
	}

		internal static Dictionary<Palette, string> ListPalettes => listPalettes;

		public static List<string> GetNames()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (string value in ListPalettes.Values)
			{
				list.Add(value);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static Palette GetPalette(string name)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Palette, string> listPalette in ListPalettes)
			{
				if (listPalette.Value == name)
				{
					return listPalette.Key;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public struct TreeMapItemGroupInfo : IItemGroupInfo
	{
		public int GroupLevel { get; private set; }

		public int GroupIndex { get; private set; }

		public int ItemIndex { get; private set; }

		public double MinValue { get; private set; }

		public double MaxValue { get; private set; }

		int IItemGroupInfo.MaxGroupLevel => -1;

		int IItemGroupInfo.ItemCount => -1;

		public TreeMapItemGroupInfo(int groupLevel, int groupIndex, int itemIndex, double minValue, double maxValue)
		{
			this = default(TreeMapItemGroupInfo);
			GroupLevel = groupLevel;
			GroupIndex = groupIndex;
			ItemIndex = itemIndex;
			MinValue = minValue;
			MaxValue = maxValue;
		}
	}
	public struct SunburstItemGroupInfo : IItemGroupInfo
	{
		public int GroupLevel { get; private set; }

		public int MaxGroupLevel { get; private set; }

		public int GroupIndex { get; private set; }

		public int ItemIndex { get; private set; }

		public int ItemCount { get; private set; }

		public double MinValue { get; private set; }

		public double MaxValue { get; private set; }

		public SunburstItemGroupInfo(int groupLevel, int maxGroupLevel, int groupIndex, int itemIndex, int itemCount, double minValue, double maxValue)
		{
			this = default(SunburstItemGroupInfo);
			GroupLevel = groupLevel;
			MaxGroupLevel = maxGroupLevel;
			GroupIndex = groupIndex;
			ItemIndex = itemIndex;
			ItemCount = itemCount;
			MinValue = minValue;
			MaxValue = maxValue;
		}
	}
	public delegate void ColorizerChangedEventHandler(object sender, ColorizerChangedEventArgs e);
	public class ColorizerChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapColorizerBase : TreeMapElement, ITreeMapColorizer, ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer, ISupportPropertyChanged, ILegendItemProvider
	{
		protected ColorizerBehavior Behavior { get; }

		[Description("Gets or sets a provider of values the colorizer uses to determine colors for treemap items.")]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public IColorizerValueProvider ValueProvider
		{
			get
			{
				return Behavior.ValueProvider;
			}
			set
			{
				Behavior.ValueProvider = value;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TypeNameSerializable => Behavior.TypeNameSerializable;

		Palette ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer.DefaultPalette => ((InnerHierarchicalControlBase)base.Owner.Owner).DefaultPalette;

		protected internal bool CanColorizeItems => Behavior.CanColorizeItems;

		public event ColorizerChangedEventHandler ColorizerChanged
		{
			add
			{
				Behavior.ColorizerChanged += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				Behavior.ColorizerChanged -= value;
			}
		}

		public TreeMapColorizerBase()
		{
			Behavior = CreateColorizerBehavior();
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged(UpdateType updateType)
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanged();
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanging()
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanging();
		}

		Color ITreeMapColorizer.GetItemColor(ITreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return GetItemColor(item, group);
		}

		List<ILegendItem> ILegendItemProvider.CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return CreateLegendItems(sourceItems);
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeValueProvider()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected abstract TreeMapElement CreateColorizer();

		protected virtual ColorizerBehavior CreateColorizerBehavior()
		{
			return DefaultColorizerBehavior.Instance;
		}

		protected virtual Color GetItemColor(ITreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return Behavior.GetItemColor(item, group);
		}

		protected virtual List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return CreateColorizer();
		}

		protected internal void UpdateDefaults(Palette paletteFromSkin)
		{
			Behavior.UpdateDefaults(paletteFromSkin);
		}
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase : TreeMapColorizerBase
	{
		protected PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase => (PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase)base.Behavior;

		[Description("Gets or sets the palette the colorizer uses to paint items.")]
		[Editor("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.ColorizerPaletteTypeEditor,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.Drawing.Design.UITypeEditor, System.Drawing, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Palette Palette
		{
			get
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.Palette;
			}
			set
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.Palette = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the format pattern that is applied to a treemap legend item’s text.")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string LegendItemPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.LegendItemPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.LegendItemPattern = value;
			}
		}

		private void ResetPalette()
		{
			PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ResetPalette();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializePalette()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ShouldSerializePalette();
		}

		private void ResetLegendItemPattern()
		{
			PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ResetPalette();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeLegendItemPattern()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ShouldSerializePalette();
		}

		protected override bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == "Palette" || propertyName == "LegendItemPattern")
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.XtraShouldSerialize();
			}
			return base.XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName);
		}

		protected internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase treeMapPaletteColorizerBase)
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.Assign(treeMapPaletteColorizerBase.PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase);
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapGradientColorizer : TreeMapColorizerBase
	{
		private GradientColorizerBehavior GradientColorizerBehavior => (GradientColorizerBehavior)base.Behavior;

		[Description("Gets or sets the start color of the gradient used to color tree map items.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color StartColor
		{
			get
			{
				return GradientColorizerBehavior.StartColor;
			}
			set
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.StartColor = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the end color of the gradient used to color tree map items.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color EndColor
		{
			get
			{
				return GradientColorizerBehavior.EndColor;
			}
			set
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.EndColor = value;
			}
		}

		protected override ColorizerBehavior CreateColorizerBehavior()
		{
			return new GradientColorizerBehavior(this);
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateColorizer()
		{
			return new TreeMapGradientColorizer();
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapGradientColorizer treeMapGradientColorizer)
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.Assign(treeMapGradientColorizer.GradientColorizerBehavior);
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeStartColor()
		{
			return GradientColorizerBehavior.ShouldSerializeStartColor();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeEndColor()
		{
			return GradientColorizerBehavior.ShouldSerializeEndColor();
		}

		private void ResetStartColor()
		{
			GradientColorizerBehavior.ResetStartColor();
		}

		private void ResetEndColor()
		{
			GradientColorizerBehavior.ResetEndColor();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		private const double defaultMax = 0.9;

		private GroupGradientColorizerBehavior GroupGradientColorizerBehavior => (GroupGradientColorizerBehavior)base.Behavior;

		[Description("Gets or sets the value indicating the minimum portion of the group color in the color mixed for a tree map item.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(0.5)]
		public double Min
		{
			get
			{
				return GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.Min;
			}
			set
			{
				GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.Min = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the value indicating the maximum portion of the group color in the color mixed for a tree map item.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(0.9)]
		public double Max
		{
			get
			{
				return GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.Max;
			}
			set
			{
				GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.Max = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the color mixed with the group color for a tree map item.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color GradientColor
		{
			get
			{
				return GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.GradientColor;
			}
			set
			{
				GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.GradientColor = value;
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeGradientColor()
		{
			return GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.ShouldSerializeGradientColor();
		}

		private void ResetGradientColor()
		{
			GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.ResetGradientColor();
		}

		protected override ColorizerBehavior CreateColorizerBehavior()
		{
			return new GroupGradientColorizerBehavior(this, 0.9);
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateColorizer()
		{
			return new TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer();
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return TreeMapPaletteColorizer.CreatePaletteColorizerLegendItems(GroupGradientColorizerBehavior, varyColorInGroup: false, sourceItems, GradientColor);
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapGroupGradientColorizer treeMapGroupGradientColorizer)
			{
				GroupGradientColorizerBehavior.Assign(treeMapGroupGradientColorizer.GroupGradientColorizerBehavior);
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapPaletteColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		private PaletteColorizerBehavior PaletteColorizerBehavior => (PaletteColorizerBehavior)base.Behavior;

		[Description("Gets or sets a value indicating whether or not tree map items should be colorized by groups.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool ColorizeGroups
		{
			get
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehavior.VaryColorInGroup;
			}
			set
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehavior.VaryColorInGroup = value;
			}
		}

		internal static List<ILegendItem> CreatePaletteColorizerLegendItems(ColorizerBehavior colorizerBehavior, bool varyColorInGroup, IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems, Color gradientColor)
		{
			return colorizerBehavior.CreateLegendItems(sourceItems, varyColorInGroup, (ILegendSourceItem item) => WinLegendItemCreator.CreateLegendItemBrushSource(item, gradientColor));
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeColorizeGroups()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehavior.ShouldSerializeVaryColorInGroup();
		}

		private void ResetColorizeGroups()
		{
			PaletteColorizerBehavior.ResetVaryColorInGroup();
		}

		protected internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehavior.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		protected override ColorizerBehavior CreateColorizerBehavior()
		{
			return new PaletteColorizerBehavior(this, defaultVaryColorInGroup: false);
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateColorizer()
		{
			return new TreeMapPaletteColorizer();
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return CreatePaletteColorizerLegendItems(PaletteColorizerBehavior, !ColorizeGroups, sourceItems, Color.Empty);
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapPaletteColorizer treeMapPaletteColorizer)
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehavior.Assign(treeMapPaletteColorizer.PaletteColorizerBehavior);
			}
		}
	}

	public class DoubleCollection : TreeMapNotificationCollection<double>
	{
		internal DoubleCollection(string str)
			: base(str)
		{
		}

		public DoubleCollection()
		{
		}

		protected override void CreateCollection(string str, string separator)
		{
			string[] array = str.Split(separator[0]);
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (double.TryParse(array[i].Trim(), out var result))
				{
					((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)this).InnerList.Add(result);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public delegate void DataAdapterChangedEventHandler(object sender, DataAdapterChangedEventArgs e);
	public class DataAdapterChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapDataAdapterBase : TreeMapElement, IItemsHolder<TreeMapItem>, ISupportPropertyChanged, ITreeMapDataAdapter
	{
		private readonly TreeMapItemCollection items;

		protected internal IList<IHierarchicalItem> InnerItems => items;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TypeNameSerializable => GetType().Name;

		IEnumerable<ITreeMapItem> ITreeMapDataAdapter.Items => (IEnumerable<ITreeMapItem>)items;

		HierarchicalItemCollectionBase<TreeMapItem> IItemsHolder<TreeMapItem>.Items => items;

		public event DataAdapterChangedEventHandler DataAdapterChanged;

		public TreeMapDataAdapterBase()
		{
			items = new TreeMapItemCollection();
			((NotificationCollection<TreeMapItem>)items).CollectionChanged += delegate
			{
				RaiseDataAdapterChanged();
			};
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged(UpdateType updateType)
		{
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Data);
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanging()
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanging();
		}

		private void RaiseDataAdapterChanged()
		{
			if (this.DataAdapterChanged != null)
			{
				this.DataAdapterChanged(this, new DataAdapterChangedEventArgs());
			}
		}

		protected abstract TreeMapElement CreateObject();

		protected override void OnOwnerChanged(HierarchicalElement oldOwner, HierarchicalElement newOwner)
		{
			((IOwnedElement)items).Owner = newOwner;
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return CreateObject();
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (!(obj is TreeMapDataAdapterBase treeMapDataAdapterBase))
			{
				return;
			}
			((DXCollectionBase<TreeMapItem>)(object)items).Clear();
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in (DXCollectionBase<TreeMapItem>)(object)treeMapDataAdapterBase.items)
			{
				((DXCollectionBase<TreeMapItem>)(object)items).Add(item);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class SunburstDataAdapterBase : SunburstElement, IItemsHolder<SunburstItem>, ISupportPropertyChanged, ISunburstDataAdapter
	{
		private readonly SunburstItemCollection items;

		protected internal IList<IHierarchicalItem> InnerItems => items;

		[Browsable(false)]

			{

			ProcessChanged(updateType);
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanging()
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanging();
		}

		private void RaiseDataAdapterChanged()
		{
			if (this.DataAdapterChanged != null)
			{
				this.DataAdapterChanged(this, new DataAdapterChangedEventArgs());
			}
		}

		protected abstract SunburstElement CreateObject();

		}

		protected override SunburstElement CreateObjectForClone()

		}

		public override void Assign(SunburstElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (!(obj is SunburstDataAdapterBase sunburstDataAdapterBase))
			{
				return;
			}
			((DXCollectionBase<SunburstItem>)(object)items).Clear();
			foreach (SunburstItem item in (DXCollectionBase<SunburstItem>)(object)sunburstDataAdapterBase.items)

			}
		}

		}

	[DesignerSerializer("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.TreeMapItemSerializer,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.ComponentModel.Design.Serialization.CodeDomSerializer, System.Design, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
	public class HierarchicalDataMapping : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private Type type;

		private string valueDataMember;

		private string labelDataMember;

		private string childrenDataMember;

		internal string ColorDataMember { get; set; } = "Color";

		[Description("Gets or sets the data object type on the current nested level.")]
		[Category("Data")]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Type Type
		{
			get
			{
				return type;
			}
			set
			{
				if (type != value)
				{
					type = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("Type");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the name of the data member whose data are used to specify tree map or sunburst items’ values.")]
		[Category("Data")]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string ValueDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return valueDataMember;
			}
			set
			{
				if (valueDataMember != value)
				{
					valueDataMember = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("ValueDataMember");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the name of the data field whose values are used to specify the tree map or sunburst items’ labels.")]
		[Category("Data")]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string LabelDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return labelDataMember;
			}
			set
			{
				if (labelDataMember != value)
				{
					labelDataMember = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("LabelDataMember");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the name of the data field whose values are used to specify child items of current level tree map or sunburst items.")]
		[Category("Data")]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string ChildrenDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return childrenDataMember;
			}
			set
			{
				if (childrenDataMember != value)
				{
					childrenDataMember = value;
					NotifyPropertyChanged("ChildrenDataMember");
				}
			}
		}

		private event PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChanged;

		event PropertyChangedEventHandler INotifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertyChanged += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				propertyChanged -= value;
			}
		}

		private void NotifyPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.propertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.propertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"{GetType().Name}";
		}
	}

			{
				if (!(destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor)))
				{
					return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
				}
				return new InstanceDescriptor(typeof(TreeMapHierarchicalDataMapping).GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes), null, isComplete: false);
			}
		}
	}

		{

					return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
				}
				return true;
			}

			{
				ConstructorInfo constructor = typeof(SunburstItem).GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes);
				if (!(destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor)))

					return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
				}
				return new InstanceDescriptor(constructor, null, isComplete: false);
			}
		}

		private static TreeMapCustomItemStyleMerger merger = new TreeMapCustomItemStyleMerger();

		private SunburstItemStyle style;

		private HierarchicalItemBehavior behavior;

		internal object Color => behavior.Color;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TypeNameSerializable => behavior.TypeNameSerializable;

		[Description("Gets or sets the object that contains data related to a sunburst item.\n\nSecurity note: Deserializing layout settings from untrusted resources may create security issues. Serializable System.Object properties that contain custom type values are not (de)serialized automatically. Review the following help topic for information on how to (de)serialize custom type values: Safe Deserialization.")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(StringConverter))]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return behavior.Tag;
			}
			set
			{
				behavior.Tag = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the sunburst item’s label.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue("")]
		public string Label
		{
			get
			{
				if (behavior.Label == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return behavior.Label.ToString();
			}
			set

				behavior.Label = value;
			}
		}

		{

			}

			}
		}

		{

					style = ElementHelper.CreateTreeMapElement<SunburstItemStyle>(this);

			}

		public bool IsGroup => behavior.Children.Count > 0;

		internal SunburstItem(double value, object label, object tag, object groupValue, object color = null)
		{
			SunburstItemCollection sunburstItemCollection = new SunburstItemCollection();

			{
				ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Data);
			};
			behavior = new HierarchicalItemBehavior(this, sunburstItemCollection, value, label, tag, groupValue, color);
		}

			: this(value, label, null, null)

		{
			((IOwnedElement)Children).Owner = newOwner;
			base.OnOwnerChanged(oldOwner, newOwner);
		}

		internal void ApplyStyle(SunburstItemStyle defaultStyle, SunburstItemStyle sunburstStyle)
		{
			if (style != null)
			{
				merger.Merge(defaultStyle, sunburstStyle, style);
			}
			else
			{
				merger.Merge(defaultStyle, sunburstStyle);
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapCustomItem : TreeMapItem, IStyleProvider, ITreeMapStyleOwner

		{

					return GroupStyle;

				return LeafStyle;
			}
		}

		private class TreeMapItemTypeConverter : TypeConverter
		{
			public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (!(destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor)))
				{
					return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
				}
				return true;
			}

			public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
			{
				ConstructorInfo member = ((value is TreeMapCustomItem) ? typeof(TreeMapCustomItem).GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes) : typeof(TreeMapItem).GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes));
				if (!(destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor)))
				{
					return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
				}
				return new InstanceDescriptor(member, null, isComplete: false);
			}
		}

	public delegate void SelectionChangedEventHandler(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e);
	public class SelectionChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private readonly IEnumerable selectedItems;

		private readonly IEnumerable selectedGroups;

		public IEnumerable SelectedItems => selectedItems;

		public IEnumerable SelectedGroups => selectedGroups;

	public class GroupInfo
	{
		private IInteractiveElement item;

		private object groupValue;

		private GroupInfo parentGroup;

		private List<object> sourceItems;

		private List<GroupInfo> childGroups;

		private List<object> groupPath = new List<object>();

		internal IInteractiveElement Item => item;

		internal List<object> GroupPath
		{
			get
			{
				if (groupPath.Count == 0)
				{
					FillGroupPath(this, groupPath);
				}
				return groupPath;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<object> SourceItems => sourceItems;

		public object GroupValue => groupValue;

		public IEnumerable<GroupInfo> ChildGroups => childGroups;

		internal GroupInfo(IInteractiveElement item, object groupValue, GroupInfo parentGroup)
		{
			this.item = item;
			this.groupValue = groupValue;
			this.parentGroup = parentGroup;
			sourceItems = new List<object>();
			childGroups = new List<GroupInfo>();
		}

		private void FillGroupPath(GroupInfo group, List<object> result)
		{
			result.Insert(0, group.GroupValue);
			if (group.parentGroup != null)
			{
				FillGroupPath(group.parentGroup, result);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class HitInfoBase
	{
		private readonly IHitTestableElement[] hitElements;

		internal HitInfoBase(IHitTestableElement[] hitElements)
		{
			this.hitElements = hitElements;
		}

		protected T GetHitObject<T>() where T : class
		{
			for (int num = hitElements.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				if (hitElements[num].Object is T result)
				{
					return result;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapHitInfo : HitInfoBase
	{
		public TreeMapItem TreeMapItem => GetHitObject<TreeMapItem>();

		public bool InTreeMapItem => TreeMapItem != null;

		internal TreeMapHitInfo(IHitTestableElement[] hitElements)
			: base(hitElements)
		{
		}
	}
	public class SunburstHitInfo : HitInfoBase
	{
		public SunburstItem SunburstItem => GetHitObject<SunburstItem>();

		public bool InSunburstItem => SunburstItem != null;

		public SunburstCenterLabel CenterLabel => GetHitObject<SunburstCenterLabel>();

		public bool InCenterLabel => CenterLabel != null;

		internal SunburstHitInfo(IHitTestableElement[] hitElements)
			: base(hitElements)
		{
		}
	}
	public class SelectedGroupCollection : IList<IEnumerable<object>>, ICollection<IEnumerable<object>>, IEnumerable<IEnumerable<object>>, IEnumerable
	{
		private class GroupPath : List<object>
		{
			public GroupPath(IEnumerable values)
			{
				foreach (object value in values)
				{
					IEnumerable<object> enumerable = value as IEnumerable<object>;
					GroupInfo groupInfo = value as GroupInfo;
					if (enumerable != null)
					{
						AddRange(enumerable);
					}
					else if (groupInfo != null)
					{
						AddRange(groupInfo.GroupPath);
					}
					else
					{
						Add(value);
					}
				}
			}

			public override bool Equals(object obj)
			{
				if (!(obj is GroupPath groupPath))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (base.Count != groupPath.Count)
				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (object item in groupPath)
				{
					if (!Contains(item))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}

			public override int GetHashCode()
			{
				return base.GetHashCode();
			}
		}

		private SelectionController selectionController;

		private List<GroupPath> innerList = new List<GroupPath>();

		public int Count => innerList.Count;

		public IEnumerable<object> this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return innerList[index];
			}
			set
			{
				innerList[index] = new GroupPath(value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsReadOnly => false;

		internal SelectedGroupCollection(SelectionController selectionController)
		{
			this.selectionController = selectionController;
		}

		private void AddCore(IEnumerable values)
		{
			GroupPath groupPath = AddInternalCore(values);
			if (groupPath != null)
			{
				selectionController.OnSelectedGroupChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>>((CollectionChangedAction)0, (IList<object>)groupPath));
			}
		}

		private bool RemoveCore(IEnumerable values)
		{
			GroupPath groupPath = new GroupPath(values);
			bool flag = innerList.Remove(groupPath);
			if (flag)
			{
				selectionController.OnSelectedGroupChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>>((CollectionChangedAction)1, (IList<object>)groupPath));
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private GroupPath AddInternalCore(IEnumerable values)
		{
			GroupPath groupPath = new GroupPath(values);
			if (innerList.Contains(groupPath))
			{
				return null;
			}
			innerList.Add(groupPath);
			return groupPath;
		}

		internal void ClearInternal()
		{
			innerList.Clear();
		}

		internal void AddInternal(IEnumerable values)
		{
			AddInternalCore(values);
		}

		public void Add(params object[] groupPath)
		{
			AddCore(groupPath);
		}

		public void Add(IEnumerable<object> groupPath)
		{
			AddCore(groupPath);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			ClearInternal();
			selectionController.OnSelectedGroupChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>>((CollectionChangedAction)3, (IList<object>)null));
		}

		public int IndexOf(IEnumerable<object> groupPath)
		{
			return innerList.IndexOf(new GroupPath(groupPath));
		}

		public int IndexOf(params object[] groupPath)
		{
			return innerList.IndexOf(new GroupPath(groupPath));
		}

		public void Insert(int index, IEnumerable<object> groupPath)
		{
			GroupPath groupPath2 = new GroupPath(groupPath);
			innerList.Insert(index, groupPath2);
			selectionController.OnSelectedGroupChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>>((CollectionChangedAction)0, (IList<object>)groupPath2));
		}

		public void Insert(int index, params object[] groupPath)
		{
			GroupPath groupPath2 = new GroupPath(groupPath);
			innerList.Insert(index, groupPath2);
			selectionController.OnSelectedGroupChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>>((CollectionChangedAction)0, (IList<object>)groupPath2));
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			RemoveCore(this[index]);
		}

		public bool Contains(IEnumerable<object> groupPath)
		{
			return innerList.Contains(new GroupPath(groupPath));
		}

		public bool Contains(params object[] groupPath)
		{
			return innerList.Contains(new GroupPath(groupPath));
		}

		public void CopyTo(IEnumerable<object>[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			Array.Copy(innerList.ToArray(), 0, array, arrayIndex, innerList.Count);
		}

		public bool Remove(IEnumerable<object> groupPath)
		{
			return RemoveCore(groupPath);
		}

		public bool Remove(params object[] groupPath)
		{
			return RemoveCore(groupPath);
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return innerList.GetEnumerator();
		}

		IEnumerator<IEnumerable<object>> IEnumerable<IEnumerable<object>>.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return innerList.GetEnumerator();
		}
	}

	public class SunburstLabel : SunburstElement, ILabelAppearanceProvider
	{
		private bool visible = true;

		private bool autoLayout;

		private SunburstLabelDisplayMode displayMode = SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Radial;

		private string textPattern = "{L}";

		[Description("Specifies the visibility of sunburst item labels.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(false)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (visible != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					visible = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Indicates whether the adaptive layout is enabled for sunburst item labels.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(true)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool AutoLayout
		{
			get
			{
				return autoLayout;
			}
			set
			{
				if (autoLayout != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					autoLayout = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Specifies how to align a label within a sunburst item.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Radial)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public SunburstLabelDisplayMode DisplayMode
		{
			get
			{
				return displayMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (displayMode != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					displayMode = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the pattern that formats the Sunburst’s label text.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue("{L}")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TextPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return textPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				if (textPattern != value)
				{
					textPattern = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		internal SunburstLabel(HierarchicalElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override SunburstElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return new SunburstLabel(null);
		}

		public override void Assign(SunburstElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is SunburstLabel sunburstLabel)
			{
				autoLayout = sunburstLabel.autoLayout;
				displayMode = sunburstLabel.displayMode;
				textPattern = sunburstLabel.textPattern;
				visible = sunburstLabel.visible;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase
	{
		private TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode layoutMode;

		private TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode actualLayoutMode;

		private Dictionary<int, TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode> groupLevelsDirections = new Dictionary<int, TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode>();

		[Description("Gets or sets the layout mode used to arrange items using this algorithm.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Auto)]
		public TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode LayoutMode
		{
			get
			{
				return layoutMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (layoutMode != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					layoutMode = value;
					RaiseLayoutAlgorithmChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode GetActualLayoutMode(double width, double height, int groupLevel)
		{
			if (groupLevel == 0)
			{
				CalculateMode(width, height);
				return layoutMode;
			}
			return groupLevelsDirections[groupLevel % 2];
		}

		private void CalculateMode(double width, double height)
		{
			if (layoutMode == TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Auto)
			{
				groupLevelsDirections[0] = ((width > height) ? TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Vertical : TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Horizontal);
			}
			else
			{
				groupLevelsDirections[0] = layoutMode;
			}
			groupLevelsDirections[1] = ((groupLevelsDirections[0] == TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Horizontal) ? TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Vertical : TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode.Horizontal);
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateLayoutAlgorithm()
		{
			return new TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		protected override LayoutAlgorithmBase GetLayoutAlgorithm()
		{
			return new SliceAndDiceAlgorithm((LayoutAlgorithmDirection)base.Direction, (SliceAndDiceAlgorithmLayoutMode)actualLayoutMode);
		}

		protected override void Calculate(IEnumerable<ITreeMapItemLayout> items, double width, double height, int groupLevel)
		{
			List<ITreeMapItemLayout> list = new List<ITreeMapItemLayout>(items);
			list.Sort(new TreeMapLayoutItemComparer());
			actualLayoutMode = GetActualLayoutMode(width, height, groupLevel);
			GetLayoutAlgorithm().Calculate(list, width, height);
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm treeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm)
			{
				LayoutMode = treeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm.LayoutMode;
			}
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm treeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm && treeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm.LayoutMode == LayoutMode)
			{
				return base.Equals((object)treeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutAlgorithm);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapSquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase
	{
		protected override TreeMapElement CreateLayoutAlgorithm()
		{
			return new TreeMapSquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		protected override LayoutAlgorithmBase GetLayoutAlgorithm()
		{
			return new SquarifiedAlgorithm((LayoutAlgorithmDirection)base.Direction);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm : TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase
	{
		private double lastStripeMinThickness = 0.025;

		[Description("Gets or sets the minimum thickness of the last strip.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(0.025)]
		public double LastStripeMinThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return lastStripeMinThickness;
			}
			set
			{
				double num = CoerceValue(value);
				if (lastStripeMinThickness != num)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					lastStripeMinThickness = num;
					RaiseLayoutAlgorithmChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private double CoerceValue(double value)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			if (value > num2)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			if (value < num)
			{
				return num;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateLayoutAlgorithm()
		{
			return new TreeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		protected override LayoutAlgorithmBase GetLayoutAlgorithm()
		{
			return new StripedAlgorithm((LayoutAlgorithmDirection)base.Direction, LastStripeMinThickness);
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm treeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm)
			{
				LastStripeMinThickness = treeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm.LastStripeMinThickness;
			}
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is TreeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm treeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm && treeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm.LastStripeMinThickness == LastStripeMinThickness)
			{
				return base.Equals((object)treeMapStripedLayoutAlgorithm);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public enum TreeMapLayoutDirection
	{
		TopLeftToBottomRight,
		BottomLeftToTopRight,
		TopRightToBottomLeft,
		BottomRightToTopLeft
	}
	public enum TreeMapSliceAndDiceLayoutMode
	{
		Auto,
		Vertical,
		Horizontal
	}
	public delegate void LayoutAlgorithmChangedEventHandler(object sender, LayoutAlgorithmChangedEventArgs e);
	public class LayoutAlgorithmChangedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase : TreeMapElement, ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm
	{
		private TreeMapLayoutDirection direction;

		[Description("Gets or sets the layout fill direction used by the algorithm.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(TreeMapLayoutDirection.TopLeftToBottomRight)]
		public TreeMapLayoutDirection Direction
		{
			get
			{
				return direction;
			}
			set
			{
				if (direction != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					direction = value;
					RaiseLayoutAlgorithmChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TypeNameSerializable => GetType().Name;

		public event LayoutAlgorithmChangedEventHandler LayoutAlgorithmChanged;

		protected void RaiseLayoutAlgorithmChanged()
		{
			if (this.LayoutAlgorithmChanged != null)
			{
				this.LayoutAlgorithmChanged(this, new LayoutAlgorithmChangedEventArgs());
			}
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
		}

		protected abstract LayoutAlgorithmBase GetLayoutAlgorithm();

		protected abstract TreeMapElement CreateLayoutAlgorithm();

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return CreateLayoutAlgorithm();
		}

		void ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm.Calculate(IEnumerable<ITreeMapItemLayout> items, double width, double height, int groupLevel)
		{
			Calculate(items, width, height, groupLevel);
		}

		protected virtual void Calculate(IEnumerable<ITreeMapItemLayout> items, double width, double height, int groupLevel)
		{
			List<ITreeMapItemLayout> list = new List<ITreeMapItemLayout>(items);
			list.Sort(new TreeMapLayoutItemComparer());
			LayoutAlgorithmBase layoutAlgorithm = GetLayoutAlgorithm();
			layoutAlgorithm.Calculate(list, width, height);
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase treeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase)
			{
				Direction = treeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase.Direction;
			}
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is TreeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase treeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase && treeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase.GetType().Equals(GetType()))
			{
				return Direction == treeMapLayoutAlgorithmBase.Direction;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public enum LegendHorizontalAlignment
	{
		LeftOutside,
		Left,
		Center,
		Right,
		RightOutside
	}

	{
		TopOutside,
		Top,
		Center,
		Bottom,

	}

		TopToBottom,
		BottomToTop,
		LeftToRight,
		RightToLeft
	}
	public class LegendCollection : ElementNotificationCollection<LegendBase>
	{
		public void Assign(LegendCollection collection)
		{
			if (collection == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("collection");
			}
			((DXCollectionBase<LegendBase>)(object)this).InnerList.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < ((DXCollectionBase<LegendBase>)(object)collection).Count; i++)
			{
				LegendBase legendBase = ((DXCollection<LegendBase>)(object)collection)[i].CreateObjectForClone();
				legendBase.Assign(((DXCollection<LegendBase>)(object)collection)[i]);
				((DXCollectionBase<LegendBase>)(object)this).InnerList.Add(legendBase);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class LegendBase : INotifyPropertyChanged, IXtraPartlyDeserializable
	{
		private const LegendHorizontalAlignment DefaultHorizontalAlignment = LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside;

		private const LegendVerticalAlignment DefaultVerticalAlignment = LegendVerticalAlignment.Top;

		private const LegendDirection DefaultDirection = LegendDirection.TopToBottom;

		private const bool DefaultVisible = true;

		private const int DefaultBorderWidth = 1;

		private const int DefaultHorizontalIndent = 4;

		private const int DefaultVerticalIndent = 2;

		private const int DefaultTextOffset = 4;

		private const bool DefaultEquallySpacedItems = true;

		private static TreeMapPadding DefaultPadding = new TreeMapPadding(6);

		private static TreeMapPadding DefaultMargins = new TreeMapPadding(5);

		private readonly LegendTitle title;

		private BackCompatibilityFontController fontController;

		private LegendHorizontalAlignment horizontalAlignment = LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside;

		private LegendVerticalAlignment verticalAlignment = LegendVerticalAlignment.Top;

		private LegendDirection direction;

		private bool visible = true;

		private Color backColor;

		private int borderWidth = 1;

		private Color borderColor;

		private Color textColor;

		private TreeMapPadding margins = DefaultMargins;

		private TreeMapPadding padding = DefaultPadding;

		private int horizontalIndent = 4;

		private int verticalIndent = 2;

		private bool equallySpacedItems = true;

		private int textOffset = 4;

		internal bool IsOutside
		{
			get
			{
				if (horizontalAlignment != LegendHorizontalAlignment.LeftOutside && horizontalAlignment != LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside && verticalAlignment != LegendVerticalAlignment.TopOutside)
				{
					return verticalAlignment == LegendVerticalAlignment.BottomOutside;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		[Description("Returns the legend’s title options.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		public LegendTitle Title => title;

		[Description("Gets or sets the legend’s horizontal alignment in the control.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public LegendHorizontalAlignment HorizontalAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return horizontalAlignment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != horizontalAlignment)
				{
					horizontalAlignment = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("HorizontalAlignment");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the legend’s vertical alignment in the control.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public LegendVerticalAlignment VerticalAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return verticalAlignment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != verticalAlignment)
				{
					verticalAlignment = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("VerticalAlignment");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the direction in which items are positioned in the legend.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public LegendDirection Direction
		{
			get
			{
				return direction;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != direction)
				{
					direction = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Direction");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Specifies whether the legend is visible.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != visible)
				{
					visible = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Visible");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the legend border width in pixels.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public int BorderWidth
		{
			get
			{
				return borderWidth;
			}
			set
			{
				if (borderWidth != value)
				{
					borderWidth = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("BorderWidth");
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the legend border color.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color BorderColor
		{
			get
			{
				return borderColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(borderColor == value))
				{
					borderColor = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("BorderColor");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the legend background color.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color BackColor
		{
			get
			{
				return backColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(backColor == value))
				{
					backColor = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("BackColor");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the color for legend item text.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color TextColor
		{
			get
			{
				return textColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != textColor)
				{
					textColor = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("TextColor");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the legend text font.")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public Font Font
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.Font;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.Font = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public DXFont DXFont
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.DXFont;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.DXFont = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the indents between the legend edges and other chart elements in pixels.")]
		[Category("Layout")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public TreeMapPadding Margins
		{
			get
			{
				return margins;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != margins)
				{
					margins = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Margins");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the indents between the legend’s edges and its content.")]
		[Category("Layout")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public TreeMapPadding Padding
		{
			get
			{
				return padding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != padding)
				{
					padding = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Padding");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the horizontal indent between legend items in pixels.")]
		[Category("Layout")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public int HorizontalIndent
		{
			get
			{
				return horizontalIndent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != horizontalIndent)
				{
					if (value < 0 || value >= 1000)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException();
					}
					horizontalIndent = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("HorizontalIndent");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the vertical indent between legend items in pixels.")]
		[Category("Layout")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public int VerticalIndent
		{
			get
			{
				return verticalIndent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != verticalIndent)
				{
					if (value < 0 || value >= 1000)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException();
					}
					verticalIndent = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("VerticalIndent");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets whether to arrange legend items in left-aligned columns.")]
		[Category("Layout")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool EquallySpacedItems
		{
			get
			{
				return equallySpacedItems;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != equallySpacedItems)
				{
					equallySpacedItems = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("EquallySpacedItems");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the distance between a legend item’s marker and description in pixels.")]
		[Category("Layout")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public int TextOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return textOffset;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != textOffset)
				{
					if (value < 0 || value >= 1000)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("The legend text offset should be greater than or equal to 0 and less than 1000.");
					}
					textOffset = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("TextOffset");
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		private bool ShouldSerializeTitle()
		{
			return title.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeBorderWidth()
		{
			return borderWidth != 1;
		}

		private void ResetBorderWidth()
		{
			borderWidth = 1;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeBackColor()
		{
			return backColor != Color.Empty;
		}

		private void ResetBackColor()
		{
			backColor = Color.Empty;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeBorderColor()
		{
			return borderColor != Color.Empty;
		}

		private void ResetBorderColor()
		{
			BorderColor = Color.Empty;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeVisible()
		{
			return !visible;
		}

		private void ResetVisible()
		{
			visible = true;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeHorizontalAlignment()
		{
			return horizontalAlignment != LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside;
		}

		private void ResetHorizontalAlignment()
		{
			HorizontalAlignment = LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeVerticalAlignment()
		{
			return verticalAlignment != LegendVerticalAlignment.Top;
		}

		private void ResetVerticalAlignment()
		{
			VerticalAlignment = LegendVerticalAlignment.Top;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeDirection()
		{
			return direction != LegendDirection.TopToBottom;
		}

		private void ResetDirection()
		{
			Direction = LegendDirection.TopToBottom;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextColor()
		{
			return textColor != Color.Empty;
		}

		private void ResetTextColor()
		{
			textColor = Color.Empty;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeFont()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void ResetFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeDXFont()
		{
			return fontController.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		private void ResetDXFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeMargins()
		{
			return margins != DefaultMargins;
		}

		private void ResetMargins()
		{
			margins = DefaultMargins;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializePadding()
		{
			return padding != DefaultPadding;
		}

		private void ResetPadding()
		{
			padding = DefaultPadding;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeHorizontalIndent()
		{
			return horizontalIndent != 4;
		}

		private void ResetHorizontalIndent()
		{
			HorizontalIndent = 4;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeVerticalIndent()
		{
			return verticalIndent != 2;
		}

		private void ResetVerticalIndent()
		{
			VerticalIndent = 2;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeEquallySpacedItems()
		{
			return !equallySpacedItems;
		}

		private void ResetEquallySpacedItems()
		{
			EquallySpacedItems = true;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextOffset()
		{
			return textOffset != 4;
		}

		private void ResetTextOffset()
		{
			TextOffset = 4;
		}

		protected LegendBase()
		{
			title = new LegendTitle();
			title.PropertyChanged += delegate
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("Title");
			};
			fontController = new BackCompatibilityFontController(DefaultFonts.Tahoma8, null, delegate
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("DXFont");
			});
		}

		protected void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		protected internal abstract LegendViewDataBase CreateViewData(IList<ILegendItem> legendItems, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Rectangle bounds);

		protected internal abstract LegendBase CreateObjectForClone();

		internal virtual void Assign(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is LegendBase legendBase)
			{
				title.Assign(legendBase.title);
				visible = legendBase.visible;
				horizontalAlignment = legendBase.HorizontalAlignment;
				verticalAlignment = legendBase.VerticalAlignment;
				direction = legendBase.Direction;
				textColor = legendBase.TextColor;
				backColor = legendBase.BackColor;
				borderColor = legendBase.BorderColor;
				borderWidth = legendBase.BorderWidth;
				margins = legendBase.margins;
				padding = legendBase.padding;
				horizontalIndent = legendBase.horizontalIndent;
				verticalIndent = legendBase.verticalIndent;
				equallySpacedItems = legendBase.equallySpacedItems;
				textOffset = legendBase.textOffset;
				fontController.Assign(legendBase.fontController);
			}
		}

		void IXtraPartlyDeserializable.Deserialize(object rootObject, IXtraPropertyCollection properties)
		{
			SerializationUtils.ConvertFontToDXFont(properties, "Font");
		}
	}
