	public class HatchFillStyle : FillStyleBase
	{
		private readonly IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider;

		private readonly Func<IAppearanceProvider, HierarchicalChartHatchBrush> getDefaultHatch;

		private DXHatchStyle hatchStyle = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchStyle;

		private Color color2 = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchColor2;

		private DXHatchStyle ActualHatchStyle
		{
			get
			{
				if (appearanceProvider != null && hatchStyle == TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchStyle)
				{
					return getDefaultHatch(appearanceProvider).HatchStyle;
				}
				return hatchStyle;
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the pattern used to hatch treemap items.")]
		[Editor("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.HatchStyleTypeEditor,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.Drawing.Design.UITypeEditor, System.Drawing, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public HatchStyle HatchStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return (HatchStyle)hatchStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != HatchStyle)
				{
					hatchStyle = (DXHatchStyle)value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("HatchStyle");
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the second color used to hatch the treemap item background.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color Color2
		{
			get
			{
				return color2;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == color2))
				{
					color2 = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Color2");
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeHatchStyle()
		{
			return hatchStyle != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchStyle;
		}

		private void ResetHatchStyle()
		{
			hatchStyle = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchStyle;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeColor2()
		{
			return color2 != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchColor2;
		}

		private void ResetColor2()
		{
			color2 = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHatchColor2;
		}

		internal HatchFillStyle(IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Func<IAppearanceProvider, HierarchicalChartHatchBrush> getDefaultHatch)
		{
			this.appearanceProvider = appearanceProvider;
			this.getDefaultHatch = getDefaultHatch;
		}

		public HatchFillStyle()
			: this(null, null)
		{
		}

		protected internal override void Assign(FillStyleBase styleBase)
		{
			base.Assign(styleBase);
			if (styleBase is HatchFillStyle hatchFillStyle)
			{
				color2 = hatchFillStyle.Color2;
				hatchStyle = (DXHatchStyle)hatchFillStyle.HatchStyle;
			}
		}

		protected internal override void Render(IRenderer renderer, Rectangle bounds, Color color)
		{
			renderer.FillRectangle(bounds, ActualHatchStyle, color, color2);
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	[TypeConverter("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.TreeMapPaddingConverter,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a")]
	public struct TreeMapPadding : IEquatable<TreeMapPadding>
	{
		private bool all;

		private int top;

		private int left;

		private int right;

		private int bottom;

		public static readonly TreeMapPadding Empty = new TreeMapPadding(0);

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[Description("Specifies the padding value that is set for all element sides.")]
		[DefaultValue(0)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public int All
		{
			get
			{
				if (!all)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				return top;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!all || top != value)
				{
					all = true;
					top = (left = (right = (bottom = value)));
				}
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[Description("Gets or sets the value that specifies the bottom padding in pixels.")]
		[DefaultValue(0)]
		public int Bottom
		{
			get
			{
				if (all)
				{
					return top;
				}
				return bottom;
			}
			set
			{
				if (all || bottom != value)
				{
					all = false;
					bottom = value;
				}
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[Description("Gets or sets the value that specifies the left padding in pixels.")]
		[DefaultValue(0)]
		public int Left
		{
			get
			{
				if (all)
				{
					return top;
				}
				return left;
			}
			set
			{
				if (all || left != value)
				{
					all = false;
					left = value;
				}
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[Description("Gets or sets the value that specifies the right padding in pixels.")]
		[DefaultValue(0)]
		public int Right
		{
			get
			{
				if (all)
				{
					return top;
				}
				return right;
			}
			set
			{
				if (all || right != value)
				{
					all = false;
					right = value;
				}
			}
		}

		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[Description("Gets or sets the value that specifies the top padding in pixels.")]
		[DefaultValue(0)]
		public int Top
		{
			get
			{
				return top;
			}
			set
			{
				if (all || top != value)
				{
					all = false;
					top = value;
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public int Horizontal => Left + Right;

		[Browsable(false)]
		public int Vertical => Top + Bottom;

		[Browsable(false)]
		public Size Size => new Size(Horizontal, Vertical);

		public static TreeMapPadding Add(TreeMapPadding p1, TreeMapPadding p2)
		{
			return p1 + p2;
		}

		public static TreeMapPadding Subtract(TreeMapPadding p1, TreeMapPadding p2)
		{
			return p1 - p2;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object other)
		{
			if (other is TreeMapPadding)
			{
				return (TreeMapPadding)other == this;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static TreeMapPadding operator +(TreeMapPadding p1, TreeMapPadding p2)
		{
			return new TreeMapPadding(p1.Left + p2.Left, p1.Top + p2.Top, p1.Right + p2.Right, p1.Bottom + p2.Bottom);
		}

		public static TreeMapPadding operator -(TreeMapPadding p1, TreeMapPadding p2)
		{
			return new TreeMapPadding(p1.Left - p2.Left, p1.Top - p2.Top, p1.Right - p2.Right, p1.Bottom - p2.Bottom);
		}

		public static bool operator ==(TreeMapPadding p1, TreeMapPadding p2)
		{
			if (p1.Left == p2.Left && p1.Top == p2.Top && p1.Right == p2.Right)
			{
				return p1.Bottom == p2.Bottom;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool operator !=(TreeMapPadding p1, TreeMapPadding p2)
		{
			return !(p1 == p2);
		}

		public TreeMapPadding(int all)
		{
			this.all = true;
			top = (left = (right = (bottom = all)));
		}

		public TreeMapPadding(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
		{
			this.top = top;
			this.left = left;
			this.right = right;
			this.bottom = bottom;
			all = this.top == this.left && this.top == this.right && this.top == this.bottom;
		}

		private void ResetAll()
		{
			All = 0;
		}

		private void ResetBottom()
		{
			Bottom = 0;
		}

		private void ResetLeft()
		{
			Left = 0;
		}

		private void ResetRight()
		{
			Right = 0;
		}

		private void ResetTop()
		{
			Top = 0;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return HashCodeHelper.CalculateGeneric<int, int, int, int>(left, top, right, bottom);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return "{Left=" + Left.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + ",Top=" + Top.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + ",Right=" + Right.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + ",Bottom=" + Bottom.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + "}";
		}

		public bool Equals(TreeMapPadding other)
		{
			return other == this;
		}

		internal bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (Left == 0 && Top == 0 && Right == 0)
			{
				return Bottom != 0;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapElementStyleBase : IOwnedElement
	{
		private HierarchicalElement owner;

		HierarchicalElement IOwnedElement.Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return owner;
			}
			set
			{
				if (owner != value)
				{
					owner = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public static void MergeStyles(TreeMapElementStyleBase destinationStyle, TreeMapElementStyleBase style)
		{
			destinationStyle.Merge(style);
		}

		protected void StyleChanged()
		{
			if (owner is ITreeMapStyleOwner treeMapStyleOwner)
			{
				treeMapStyleOwner.OnStyleChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void Merge(TreeMapElementStyleBase style)
		{
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"({GetType().Name})";
		}
	}
	public class HierarchicalItemStyle : TreeMapElementStyleBase, IXtraPartlyDeserializable
	{
		private Color fill;

		private Color textColor;

		private Color borderColor;

		protected BackCompatibilityFontController fontController;

		private bool showLabel = true;

		private float borderBrightnessCoeff = 1f;

		internal bool ShowLabel
		{
			get
			{
				return showLabel;
			}
			set
			{
				if (showLabel != value)
				{
					showLabel = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		internal float BorderBrightnessCoeff
		{
			get
			{
				return borderBrightnessCoeff;
			}
			set
			{
				if (borderBrightnessCoeff != value)
				{
					borderBrightnessCoeff = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the color that is used to fill a treemap / sunburst item.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color Fill
		{
			get
			{
				return fill;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(fill == value))
				{
					fill = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets a treemap / sunburst item text color.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color TextColor
		{
			get
			{
				return textColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(textColor == value))
				{
					textColor = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets a treemap / sunburst item text font.")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public Font Font
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.Font;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.Font = value;
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public DXFont DXFont
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.DXFont;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.DXFont = value;
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets a sunburst item’s border color.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color BorderColor
		{
			get
			{
				return borderColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(borderColor == value))
				{
					borderColor = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool ShouldSerializeFill()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void ResetFill()
		{
		}

		protected virtual bool ShouldSerializeTextColor()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void ResetTextColor()
		{
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeFont()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void ResetFont()
		{
		}

		protected virtual bool ShouldSerializeDXFont()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void ResetDXFont()
		{
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeBorderColor()
		{
			return borderColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor;
		}

		private void ResetBorderColor()
		{
			BorderColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor;
		}

		public HierarchicalItemStyle()
		{
		}

		public HierarchicalItemStyle(Color fill, Color textColor, DXFont font, Color borderColor)
		{
			this.fill = fill;
			this.textColor = textColor;
			this.borderColor = borderColor;
			fontController = new BackCompatibilityFontController(font, null, base.StyleChanged);
		}

		internal virtual bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return ShouldSerializeBorderColor();
		}

		protected void Assign(HierarchicalItemStyle style)
		{
			Fill = style.Fill;
			TextColor = style.TextColor;
			BorderColor = style.BorderColor;
			fontController.Assign(style.fontController);
		}

		void IXtraPartlyDeserializable.Deserialize(object rootObject, IXtraPropertyCollection properties)
		{
			PartlyDeserialize(rootObject, properties);
		}

		protected virtual void PartlyDeserialize(object rootObject, IXtraPropertyCollection properties)
		{
			SerializationUtils.ConvertFontToDXFont(properties, "Font");
		}
	}
	public class SunburstItemStyle : HierarchicalItemStyle
	{
		private Color textGlowColor;

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the glow color of the sunburst item’s text.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color TextGlowColor
		{
			get
			{
				return textGlowColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(textGlowColor == value))
				{
					textGlowColor = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextGlowColor()
		{
			return TextGlowColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor;
		}

		private void ResetTextGlowColor()
		{
			TextGlowColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor;
		}

		public SunburstItemStyle()
			: base(TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFill, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextColor, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFont, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor)
		{
			textGlowColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor;
		}

		protected override void Merge(TreeMapElementStyleBase style)
		{
			if (style is SunburstItemStyle sunburstItemStyle)
			{
				if (sunburstItemStyle.Fill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFill)
				{
					base.Fill = sunburstItemStyle.Fill;
				}
				if (sunburstItemStyle.TextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextColor)
				{
					base.TextColor = sunburstItemStyle.TextColor;
				}
				if (sunburstItemStyle.TextGlowColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor)
				{
					TextGlowColor = sunburstItemStyle.TextGlowColor;
				}
				if (!sunburstItemStyle.fontController.IsDefaultFont)
				{
					fontController.Assign(sunburstItemStyle.fontController);
				}
				if (sunburstItemStyle.BorderColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor)
				{
					base.BorderColor = sunburstItemStyle.BorderColor;
				}
			}
		}

		internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeFill() && !ShouldSerializeDXFont() && !ShouldSerializeTextColor() && !ShouldSerializeTextGlowColor())
			{
				return base.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Assign(TreeMapItemLeafStyle style)
		{
			TextGlowColor = style.TextGlowColor;
			Assign((HierarchicalItemStyle)style);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemLeafStyle : HierarchicalItemStyle
	{
		private const bool DefaultBorderVisible = true;

		private System.Drawing.StringAlignment labelAlignment;

		private TreeMapPadding padding;

		private bool borderVisible = true;

		private Color textGlowColor;

		private System.Drawing.StringAlignment labelVerticalAlignment;

		private FillStyleBase fillStyle = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultFillStyle;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[DefaultValue(true)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		public bool BorderVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return borderVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				borderVisible = value;
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the treemap leaf padding.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public TreeMapPadding Padding
		{
			get
			{
				return padding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(padding == value))
				{
					padding = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the alignment for treemap item labels.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public System.Drawing.StringAlignment LabelAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return labelAlignment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (labelAlignment != value)
				{
					labelAlignment = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the glow color of a text for tree map leaf items.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color TextGlowColor
		{
			get
			{
				return textGlowColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(textGlowColor == value))
				{
					textGlowColor = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the treemap leaf label’s vertical alignment.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public System.Drawing.StringAlignment LabelVerticalAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return labelVerticalAlignment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (labelVerticalAlignment != value)
				{
					labelVerticalAlignment = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the fill style for treemap leaves.")]
		[Editor("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.FillStylePickerEditor,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.Drawing.Design.UITypeEditor, System.Drawing, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public FillStyleBase FillStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return fillStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (fillStyle != value)
				{
					if (fillStyle != null)
					{
						fillStyle.PropertyChanged -= OnFillStylePropertyChanged;
					}
					fillStyle = value;
					if (fillStyle != null)
					{
						fillStyle.PropertyChanged += OnFillStylePropertyChanged;
					}
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeLabelAlignment()
		{
			return labelAlignment != System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near;
		}

		private void ResetLabelAlignment()
		{
			labelAlignment = System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextGlowColor()
		{
			return TextGlowColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor;
		}

		private void ResetTextGlowColor()
		{
			TextGlowColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor;
		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeLabelVerticalAlignment()
		{
			return labelVerticalAlignment != System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near;
		}

		protected void ResetLabelVerticalAlignment()
		{
			labelVerticalAlignment = System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near;
		}

		private void OnFillStylePropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			StyleChanged();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeFillStyle()
		{
			return fillStyle != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultFillStyle;
		}

		private void ResetFillStyle()
		{
			fillStyle = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultFillStyle;
		}

		internal TreeMapItemLeafStyle(Color defaultFill, Color defaultTextColor, DXFont defaultFont, Color defaultItemBorderColor, TreeMapPadding defaultPadding, Color defaultTextGlowColor)
			: base(defaultFill, defaultTextColor, defaultFont, defaultItemBorderColor)
		{
			padding = defaultPadding;
			textGlowColor = defaultTextGlowColor;
		}

		public TreeMapItemLeafStyle()
			: this(TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFill, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextColor, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFont, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafPadding, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor)
		{
		}

		protected virtual bool ShouldSerializePadding()
		{
			return Padding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafPadding;
		}

		protected virtual void ResetPadding()
		{
			Padding = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafPadding;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeFill()
		{
			return base.Fill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFill;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeTextColor()
		{
			return base.TextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextColor;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeDXFont()
		{
			return fontController.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		protected override void ResetFill()
		{
			base.Fill = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFill;
		}

		protected override void ResetTextColor()
		{
			base.TextColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextColor;
		}

		protected override void ResetDXFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		protected override void Merge(TreeMapElementStyleBase style)
		{
			if (style is TreeMapItemLeafStyle treeMapItemLeafStyle)
			{
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.Fill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafFill)
				{
					base.Fill = treeMapItemLeafStyle.Fill;
				}
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.TextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextColor)
				{
					base.TextColor = treeMapItemLeafStyle.TextColor;
				}
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.TextGlowColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafTextGlowColor)
				{
					TextGlowColor = treeMapItemLeafStyle.TextGlowColor;
				}
				if (!treeMapItemLeafStyle.fontController.IsDefaultFont)
				{
					fontController.Assign(treeMapItemLeafStyle.fontController);
				}
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.Padding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultLeafPadding)
				{
					Padding = treeMapItemLeafStyle.Padding;
				}
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.LabelAlignment != System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near)
				{
					LabelAlignment = treeMapItemLeafStyle.LabelAlignment;
				}
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.LabelVerticalAlignment != System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near)
				{
					LabelVerticalAlignment = treeMapItemLeafStyle.LabelVerticalAlignment;
				}
				if (treeMapItemLeafStyle.BorderColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor)
				{
					base.BorderColor = treeMapItemLeafStyle.BorderColor;
				}
				FillStyle = treeMapItemLeafStyle.FillStyle;
				base.ShowLabel = treeMapItemLeafStyle.ShowLabel;
				base.BorderBrightnessCoeff = treeMapItemLeafStyle.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
			}
		}

		internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeFill() && !ShouldSerializeDXFont() && !ShouldSerializeTextColor() && !ShouldSerializeTextGlowColor() && !ShouldSerializePadding() && !ShouldSerializeLabelVerticalAlignment() && !ShouldSerializeLabelAlignment() && !ShouldSerializeFillStyle())
			{
				return base.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal virtual TreeMapItemLeafStyle CreateInstance()
		{
			return new TreeMapItemLeafStyle();
		}

		public virtual void Assign(TreeMapItemLeafStyle style)
		{
			Assign((HierarchicalItemStyle)style);
			LabelAlignment = style.LabelAlignment;
			base.Fill = style.Fill;
			base.TextColor = style.TextColor;
			Padding = style.Padding;
			LabelAlignment = style.LabelAlignment;
			base.BorderColor = style.BorderColor;
			TextGlowColor = style.TextGlowColor;
			LabelVerticalAlignment = style.LabelVerticalAlignment;
			FillStyle = style.FillStyle;
			BorderVisible = style.BorderVisible;
			fontController.Assign(style.fontController);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemGroupStyle : TreeMapItemLeafStyle
	{
		private TreeMapPadding headerPadding;

		private TreeMapPadding subGroupHeaderPadding;

		private Color subGroupTextColor;

		private Color subGroupFill;

		private Color subGroupBorderColor;

		private BackCompatibilityFontController subGroupFontController;

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the padding of the tree map group’s header")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public TreeMapPadding HeaderPadding
		{
			get
			{
				return headerPadding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(headerPadding == value))
				{
					headerPadding = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the header padding of sub groups of the styled group.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public TreeMapPadding SubGroupHeaderPadding
		{
			get
			{
				return subGroupHeaderPadding;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(subGroupHeaderPadding == value))
				{
					subGroupHeaderPadding = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the subgroup fill color.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color SubGroupFill
		{
			get
			{
				return subGroupFill;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(subGroupFill == value))
				{
					subGroupFill = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the subgroup text color.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color SubGroupTextColor
		{
			get
			{
				return subGroupTextColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(subGroupTextColor == value))
				{
					subGroupTextColor = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the header font of sub groups of the styled group.")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public Font SubGroupFont
		{
			get
			{
				return subGroupFontController.Font;
			}
			set
			{
				subGroupFontController.Font = value;
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public DXFont DXSubGroupFont
		{
			get
			{
				return subGroupFontController.DXFont;
			}
			set
			{
				subGroupFontController.DXFont = value;
			}
		}

		[Category("Appearance")]
		[Description("Gets or sets the border color of sub groups of the styled group.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color SubGroupBorderColor
		{
			get
			{
				return subGroupBorderColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(subGroupBorderColor == value))
				{
					subGroupBorderColor = value;
					StyleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeHeaderPadding()
		{
			return headerPadding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding;
		}

		private void ResetHeaderPadding()
		{
			HeaderPadding = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeSubGroupHeaderPadding()
		{
			return headerPadding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding;
		}

		private void ResetSubGroupHeaderPadding()
		{
			SubGroupHeaderPadding = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeSubGroupFill()
		{
			return SubGroupFill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupFill;
		}

		private void ResetSubGroupFill()
		{
			SubGroupFill = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupFill;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeSubGroupTextColor()
		{
			return SubGroupTextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupTextColor;
		}

		private void ResetSubGroupTextColor()
		{
			SubGroupTextColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupTextColor;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeSubGroupFont()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void ResetSubGroupFont()
		{
			subGroupFontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeDXSubGroupFont()
		{
			return subGroupFontController.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		private void ResetDXSubGroupFont()
		{
			subGroupFontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeSubGroupBorderColor()
		{
			return subGroupBorderColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor;
		}

		private void ResetSubGroupBorderColor()
		{
			SubGroupBorderColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor;
		}

		public TreeMapItemGroupStyle()
			: base(TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupFont, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupPadding, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupTextGlowColor)
		{
			subGroupFill = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupFill;
			subGroupTextColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupTextColor;
			headerPadding = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding;
			subGroupHeaderPadding = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding;
			subGroupBorderColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor;
			subGroupFontController = new BackCompatibilityFontController(TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupFont, null, base.StyleChanged);
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeFill()
		{
			return base.Fill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeTextColor()
		{
			return base.TextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor;
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeDXFont()
		{
			return fontController.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializePadding()
		{
			return base.Padding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupPadding;
		}

		protected override void ResetFill()
		{
			base.Fill = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill;
		}

		protected override void ResetTextColor()
		{
			base.TextColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor;
		}

		protected override void ResetDXFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		protected override void ResetPadding()
		{
			base.Padding = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupPadding;
		}

		internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeHeaderPadding() && !ShouldSerializeSubGroupHeaderPadding() && !ShouldSerializeFill() && !ShouldSerializeDXFont() && !ShouldSerializeTextColor() && !ShouldSerializeSubGroupFill() && !ShouldSerializeSubGroupTextColor() && !ShouldSerializeDXSubGroupFont() && !ShouldSerializePadding() && !ShouldSerializeSubGroupBorderColor())
			{
				return base.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void Merge(TreeMapElementStyleBase style)
		{
			if (style is TreeMapItemGroupStyle treeMapItemGroupStyle)
			{
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.HeaderPadding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding)
				{
					HeaderPadding = treeMapItemGroupStyle.HeaderPadding;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupHeaderPadding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultHeaderPadding)
				{
					SubGroupHeaderPadding = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupHeaderPadding;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.Fill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill)
				{
					base.Fill = treeMapItemGroupStyle.Fill;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupFill != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupFill)
				{
					SubGroupFill = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupFill;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.TextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor)
				{
					base.TextColor = treeMapItemGroupStyle.TextColor;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupTextColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSubGroupTextColor)
				{
					SubGroupTextColor = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupTextColor;
				}
				if (!treeMapItemGroupStyle.fontController.IsDefaultFont)
				{
					fontController.Assign(treeMapItemGroupStyle.fontController);
				}
				if (!treeMapItemGroupStyle.subGroupFontController.IsDefaultFont)
				{
					subGroupFontController.Assign(treeMapItemGroupStyle.subGroupFontController);
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.Padding != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultGroupPadding)
				{
					base.Padding = treeMapItemGroupStyle.Padding;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.LabelAlignment != System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Near)
				{
					base.LabelAlignment = treeMapItemGroupStyle.LabelAlignment;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.BorderColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor)
				{
					base.BorderColor = treeMapItemGroupStyle.BorderColor;
				}
				if (treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupBorderColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultItemBorderColor)
				{
					SubGroupBorderColor = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupBorderColor;
				}
				base.ShowLabel = treeMapItemGroupStyle.ShowLabel;
			}
		}

		internal override TreeMapItemLeafStyle CreateInstance()
		{
			return new TreeMapItemGroupStyle();
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapItemLeafStyle style)
		{
			base.Assign(style);
			if (style is TreeMapItemGroupStyle treeMapItemGroupStyle)
			{
				SubGroupFill = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupFill;
				SubGroupTextColor = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupTextColor;
				HeaderPadding = treeMapItemGroupStyle.HeaderPadding;
				SubGroupHeaderPadding = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupHeaderPadding;
				subGroupBorderColor = treeMapItemGroupStyle.SubGroupBorderColor;
				subGroupFontController.Assign(treeMapItemGroupStyle.subGroupFontController);
			}
		}

		protected override void PartlyDeserialize(object rootObject, IXtraPropertyCollection properties)
		{
			base.PartlyDeserialize(rootObject, properties);
			SerializationUtils.ConvertFontToDXFont(properties, "SubGroupFont");
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapAppearance : TreeMapElement
	{
		private readonly TreeMapItemLeafStyle leafStyle;

		private readonly TreeMapItemGroupStyle groupStyle;

		private readonly TreeMapItemLeafStyle highlightedLeafStyle;

		private readonly TreeMapItemLeafStyle selectedLeafStyle;

		[Description("Returns the style that applies to treemap leaves.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public TreeMapItemLeafStyle LeafStyle => leafStyle;

		[Description("Returns the style of treemap groups.")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public TreeMapItemGroupStyle GroupStyle => groupStyle;

		[Description("Returns a style used to specify the appearance of highlighted treemap leaves.")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public TreeMapItemLeafStyle HighlightedLeafStyle => highlightedLeafStyle;

		[Description("Returns the style that applies to selected treemap leaves.")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public TreeMapItemLeafStyle SelectedLeafStyle => selectedLeafStyle;

		private TreeMapAppearance()
		{
		}

		internal TreeMapAppearance(HierarchicalElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			leafStyle = ElementHelper.CreateTreeMapElement<TreeMapItemLeafStyle>(owner);
			groupStyle = ElementHelper.CreateTreeMapElement<TreeMapItemGroupStyle>(owner);
			highlightedLeafStyle = ElementHelper.CreateTreeMapElement<TreeMapItemLeafStyle>(owner);
			highlightedLeafStyle.FillStyle = new HatchFillStyle((IAppearanceProvider)owner, (IAppearanceProvider provider) => provider.HighlightedItemHatch);
			selectedLeafStyle = ElementHelper.CreateTreeMapElement<TreeMapItemLeafStyle>(owner);
			selectedLeafStyle.FillStyle = new HatchFillStyle((IAppearanceProvider)owner, (IAppearanceProvider provider) => provider.SelectedItemHatch);
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return new TreeMapAppearance();
		}

		protected internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!leafStyle.ShouldSerialize() && !groupStyle.ShouldSerialize() && !highlightedLeafStyle.ShouldSerialize() && !selectedLeafStyle.ShouldSerialize())
			{
				return base.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is TreeMapAppearance treeMapAppearance)
			{
				leafStyle.Assign(treeMapAppearance.leafStyle);
				groupStyle.Assign(treeMapAppearance.groupStyle);
				highlightedLeafStyle.Assign(treeMapAppearance.highlightedLeafStyle);
				selectedLeafStyle.Assign(treeMapAppearance.selectedLeafStyle);
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstCenterLabel : SunburstElement, ICenterLabelAppearanceProvider
	{
		private string textPattern;

		private Color backgroundColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill;

		private Color foregroundColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor;

		private BackCompatibilityFontController fontController;

		private bool visible = true;

		[Description("Gets or sets the pattern that formats the sunburst’s center label text.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TextPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return textPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(textPattern == value))
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					textPattern = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the center label’s foreground color.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color BackgroundColor
		{
			get
			{
				return backgroundColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(backgroundColor == value))
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					backgroundColor = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the center label’s foreground color.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color ForegroundColor
		{
			get
			{
				return foregroundColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(foregroundColor == value))
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					foregroundColor = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the center label text’s font.")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public Font TextFont
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.Font;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.Font = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public DXFont DXTextFont
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.DXFont;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.DXFont = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Specifies the sunburst center label visibility.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(true)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (visible != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					visible = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		internal SunburstCenterLabel(HierarchicalElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
			fontController = new BackCompatibilityFontController(TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSunburstCenterLabelFont, ApplyPropertyChanging, delegate
			{
				ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
			});
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextPattern()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(textPattern);
		}

		private void ResetTextPattern()
		{
			textPattern = string.Empty;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeBackgroundColor()
		{
			return backgroundColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill;
		}

		private void ResetBackgroundColor()
		{
			backgroundColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeForegroundColor()
		{
			return foregroundColor != TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor;
		}

		private void ResetForegroundColor()
		{
			foregroundColor = TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextFont()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void ResetTextFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeDXTextFont()
		{
			return fontController.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		private void ResetDXTextFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		protected override SunburstElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return new SunburstCenterLabel(null);
		}

		public override void Assign(SunburstElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is SunburstCenterLabel sunburstCenterLabel)
			{
				backgroundColor = sunburstCenterLabel.backgroundColor;
				foregroundColor = sunburstCenterLabel.foregroundColor;
				textPattern = sunburstCenterLabel.textPattern;
				visible = sunburstCenterLabel.visible;
				fontController.Assign(sunburstCenterLabel.fontController);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class SunburstColorizerBase : SunburstElement, ISunburstColorizer, ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer, ISupportPropertyChanged, ILegendItemProvider
	{
		protected ColorizerBehavior Behavior { get; }

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		[NonTestableProperty]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string TypeNameSerializable => Behavior.TypeNameSerializable;

		Palette ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer.DefaultPalette => ((InnerHierarchicalControlBase)base.Owner.Owner).DefaultPalette;

		protected internal bool CanColorizeItems => Behavior.CanColorizeItems;

		public event ColorizerChangedEventHandler ColorizerChanged
		{
			add
			{
				Behavior.ColorizerChanged += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				Behavior.ColorizerChanged -= value;
			}
		}

		public SunburstColorizerBase()
		{
			Behavior = CreateColorizerBehavior();
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged(UpdateType updateType)
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanged();
		}

		void ISupportPropertyChanged.PropertyChanging()
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanging();
		}

		List<ILegendItem> ILegendItemProvider.CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return CreateLegendItems(sourceItems);
		}

		Color ISunburstColorizer.GetItemColor(ISunburstItem item, SunburstItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return GetItemColor(item, group);
		}

		protected abstract ColorizerBehavior CreateColorizerBehavior();

		protected virtual Color GetItemColor(ISunburstItem item, SunburstItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return Behavior.GetItemColor(item, group);
		}

		protected internal void UpdateDefaults(Palette paletteFromSkin)
		{
			Behavior.UpdateDefaults(paletteFromSkin);
		}

		protected virtual List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public abstract class SunburstPaletteColorizerBase : SunburstColorizerBase
	{
		protected PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase => (PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase)base.Behavior;

		[Description("Gets or sets the palette the colorizer uses to paint items.")]
		[Editor("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.ColorizerPaletteTypeEditor,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.Drawing.Design.UITypeEditor, System.Drawing, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Palette Palette
		{
			get
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.Palette;
			}
			set
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.Palette = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the format pattern that is applied to a sunburst legend item’s text.")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Visible)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string LegendItemPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.LegendItemPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.LegendItemPattern = value;
			}
		}

		private void ResetPalette()
		{
			PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ResetPalette();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializePalette()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ShouldSerializePalette();
		}

		private void ResetLegendItemPattern()
		{
			PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ResetPalette();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeLegendItemPattern()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ShouldSerializePalette();
		}

		protected override bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == "Palette")
			{
				return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.XtraShouldSerialize();
			}
			return base.XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName);
		}

		protected internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		public override void Assign(SunburstElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is SunburstPaletteColorizerBase sunburstPaletteColorizerBase)
			{
				PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase.Assign(sunburstPaletteColorizerBase.PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase);
			}
		}
	}
	public enum GradientColorizerMode
	{
		ByGroupLevel,
		ByItemIndex
	}
	public class SunburstGradientColorizer : SunburstPaletteColorizerBase
	{
		private const double defaultMax = 1.0;

		private RootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior GradientColorizerBehavior => (RootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior)base.Behavior;

		[Description("The color intensity of the sunburst lowest level items.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(0.5)]
		public double Min
		{
			get
			{
				return GradientColorizerBehavior.Min;
			}
			set
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.Min = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the mode that defines how to distribute a gradient across items.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(GradientColorizerMode.ByGroupLevel)]
		public GradientColorizerMode Mode
		{
			get
			{
				return GradientColorizerBehavior.Mode;
			}
			set
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.Mode = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("The color intensity of the sunburst top level items.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		[DefaultValue(1.0)]
		public double Max
		{
			get
			{
				return GradientColorizerBehavior.Max;
			}
			set
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.Max = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the color that should be blended with the sunburst item color.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color GradientColor
		{
			get
			{
				return GradientColorizerBehavior.GradientColor;
			}
			set
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.GradientColor = value;
			}
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeGradientColor()
		{
			return GradientColorizerBehavior.ShouldSerializeGradientColor();
		}

		private void ResetGradientColor()
		{
			GradientColorizerBehavior.ResetGradientColor();
		}

		protected override ColorizerBehavior CreateColorizerBehavior()
		{
			return new RootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior(this, 1.0);
		}

		protected override SunburstElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			return new SunburstGradientColorizer();
		}

		protected internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected override List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems(IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> sourceItems)
		{
			return TreeMapPaletteColorizer.CreatePaletteColorizerLegendItems(GradientColorizerBehavior, Mode == GradientColorizerMode.ByItemIndex, sourceItems, GradientColor);
		}

		public override void Assign(SunburstElement obj)
		{
			base.Assign(obj);
			if (obj is SunburstGradientColorizer sunburstGradientColorizer)
			{
				GradientColorizerBehavior.Assign(sunburstGradientColorizer.GradientColorizerBehavior);
			}
		}
	}

		}
	}

	public abstract class TextElement : IXtraSupportShouldSerialize, IXtraPartlyDeserializable, INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private System.Drawing.StringAlignment textAlignment;

		private string text;

		private Color textColor;

		private BackCompatibilityFontController fontController;

		protected abstract Color DefaultTextColor { get; }

		protected abstract System.Drawing.StringAlignment DefaultTextAlignment { get; }

		protected abstract string DefaultText { get; }

		protected abstract DXFont DefaultDXFont { get; }

		[Description("Gets or sets the element’s text.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public string Text
		{
			get
			{
				return text;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != text)
				{
					text = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Text");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the text color for the element.")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public Color TextColor
		{
			get
			{
				return textColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != TextColor)
				{
					textColor = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("TextColor");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the font used to display the element’s text.")]
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public Font Font
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.Font;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.Font = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("")]
		[Category("Appearance")]
		[RefreshProperties(RefreshProperties.All)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public DXFont DXFont
		{
			get
			{
				return fontController.DXFont;
			}
			set
			{
				fontController.DXFont = value;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets text alignment for the element.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(StringAlignmentTypeConvertor))]
		[Localizable(true)]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public System.Drawing.StringAlignment TextAlignment
		{
			get
			{
				return textAlignment;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != textAlignment)
				{
					textAlignment = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("TextAlignment");
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public TextElement()
		{
			text = DefaultText;
			textColor = DefaultTextColor;
			textAlignment = DefaultTextAlignment;
			fontController = new BackCompatibilityFontController(DefaultDXFont, null, delegate
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("DXFont");
			});
		}

		protected void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			this.PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
		}

		internal void Assign(TextElement textElement)
		{
			text = textElement.Text;
			textAlignment = textElement.TextAlignment;
			textColor = textElement.TextColor;
			fontController.Assign(textElement.fontController);
		}

		internal virtual Color GetActualTextColor(IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider)
		{
			if (TextColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				return appearanceProvider?.TextColor ?? TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultTextForeColor;
			}
			return TextColor;
		}

		bool IXtraSupportShouldSerialize.ShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			return XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName);
		}

		protected virtual bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			return propertyName switch
			{
				"Text" => ShouldSerializeText(), 
				"Font" => ShouldSerializeFont(), 
				"DXFont" => ShouldSerializeDXFont(), 
				"TextAlignment" => ShouldSerializeTextAlignment(), 
				"TextColor" => ShouldSerializeTextColor(), 
				_ => true, 
			};
		}

		private void ResetText()
		{
			text = DefaultText;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeText()
		{
			return text != DefaultText;
		}

		private void ResetFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeFont()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void ResetDXFont()
		{
			fontController.Reset();
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeDXFont()
		{
			return fontController.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		private void ResetTextAlignment()
		{
			textAlignment = DefaultTextAlignment;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextAlignment()
		{
			return textAlignment != DefaultTextAlignment;
		}

		private void ResetTextColor()
		{
			textColor = DefaultTextColor;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTextColor()
		{
			return textColor != DefaultTextColor;
		}

		internal virtual bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeFont() && !ShouldSerializeText() && !ShouldSerializeTextAlignment())
			{
				return ShouldSerializeTextColor();
			}
			return true;
		}

		void IXtraPartlyDeserializable.Deserialize(object rootObject, IXtraPropertyCollection properties)
		{
			SerializationUtils.ConvertFontToDXFont(properties, "Font");
		}
	}
	public class TitleBase : TextElement
	{
		private const bool DefaultVisible = true;

		private const int DefaultMaxLineCount = 0;

		private const bool DefaultWordWrap = false;

		private bool visible = true;

		private bool wordWrap;

		private int maxLineCount;

		protected override string DefaultText => "Title";

		protected override DXFont DefaultDXFont => DefaultFonts.Tahoma14;

		protected override Color DefaultTextColor => Color.Empty;

		protected override System.Drawing.StringAlignment DefaultTextAlignment => System.Drawing.StringAlignment.Center;

		internal bool WordWrap
		{
			get
			{
				return wordWrap;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != wordWrap)
				{
					wordWrap = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal int MaxLineCount
		{
			get
			{
				return maxLineCount;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != maxLineCount)
				{
					maxLineCount = value;
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Specifies whether the title is visible.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != visible)
				{
					visible = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Visible");
				}
			}
		}

		private void ResetVisible()
		{
			visible = true;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeVisible()
		{
			return !visible;
		}

		internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!base.ShouldSerialize())
			{
				return ShouldSerializeVisible();
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == "Visible")
			{
				return ShouldSerializeVisible();
			}
			return base.XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName);
		}

		internal void Assign(TitleBase title)
		{
			Assign((TextElement)title);
			visible = title.Visible;
		}
	}
	public class LegendTitle : TitleBase
	{
		protected override string DefaultText => "Legend";

		protected override DXFont DefaultDXFont => DefaultFonts.Tahoma12;
	}
	public enum TitleDockStyle
	{
		Top = 1,
		Bottom,
		Left,
		Right
	}
	public class Title : TitleBase
	{
		private const int DefaultIndent = 5;

		private const TitleDockStyle DefaultDock = TitleDockStyle.Top;

		private const bool DefaultIsOverlay = false;

		private int indent = 5;

		private TitleDockStyle dock = TitleDockStyle.Top;

		private bool isOverlay;

		internal bool Rotated
		{
			get
			{
				if (Dock != TitleDockStyle.Right)
				{
					return Dock == TitleDockStyle.Left;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		internal int BoundsHeight { get; set; }

		[Description("Gets or sets the control’s edge to which the title is docked.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public TitleDockStyle Dock
		{
			get
			{
				return dock;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != dock)
				{
					dock = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Dock");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Specifies whether the title is rendered within or outside the chart area.")]
		[Category("Behavior")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool IsOverlay
		{
			get
			{
				return isOverlay;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != isOverlay)
				{
					isOverlay = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("IsOverlay");
				}
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets or sets the distance between the title and chart content in pixels.")]
		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public int Indent
		{
			get
			{
				return indent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != indent)
				{
					indent = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Indent");
				}
			}
		}

		protected override bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			return propertyName switch
			{
				"Dock" => ShouldSerializeDock(), 
				"Indent" => ShouldSerializeIndent(), 
				"IsOverlay" => ShouldSerializeIsOverlay(), 
				_ => base.XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName), 
			};
		}

		internal void Assign(Title title)
		{
			Assign((TitleBase)title);
			dock = title.Dock;
			indent = title.Indent;
			base.TextColor = title.TextColor;
		}

		private void ResetDock()
		{
			dock = TitleDockStyle.Top;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeDock()
		{
			return dock != TitleDockStyle.Top;
		}

		private void ResetIndent()
		{
			indent = 5;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeIndent()
		{
			return indent != 5;
		}

		private void ResetIsOverlay()
		{
			isOverlay = false;
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeIsOverlay()
		{
			return isOverlay;
		}

		internal override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!base.ShouldSerialize() && !ShouldSerializeDock())
			{
				return ShouldSerializeIndent();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return base.Text;
		}
	}
	public class ElementNotificationCollection<T> : NotificationCollection<T> where T : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private void OnItemChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NotificationCollection<T>)this).OnCollectionChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<T>((CollectionChangedAction)2, (T)sender));
		}

		protected override bool OnRemove(int index, T value)
		{
			value.PropertyChanged -= OnItemChanged;
			return base.OnRemove(index, value);
		}

		protected override bool OnInsert(int index, T value)
		{
			value.PropertyChanged += OnItemChanged;
			return base.OnInsert(index, value);
		}

		protected override bool OnClear()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).InnerList.Count; i++)
			{
				T val = ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).InnerList[i];
				val.PropertyChanged -= OnItemChanged;
			}
			return base.OnClear();
		}
	}
	public class TitleCollection : ElementNotificationCollection<Title>
	{
		internal void Assign(TitleCollection collection)
		{
			if (collection == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("collection");
			}
			((DXCollectionBase<Title>)(object)this).InnerList.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < ((DXCollectionBase<Title>)(object)collection).Count; i++)
			{
				Title title = new Title();
				title.Assign(((DXCollection<Title>)(object)collection)[i]);
				((DXCollectionBase<Title>)(object)this).InnerList.Add(title);
			}
		}
	}
	[DesignerSerializer("DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Design.TreeMapCollectionSerializer,DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.v23.2.Design, Version=23.2.4.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b88d1754d700e49a", "System.ComponentModel.Design.Serialization.CodeDomSerializer, System.Design, Version=4.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b03f5f7f11d50a3a")]
	public abstract class HierarchicalItemCollectionBase<T> : NotificationCollection<T>, IList<IHierarchicalItem>, ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>, IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem>, IEnumerable, IOwnedElement where T : IOwnedElement, IHierarchicalItem
	{
		private class HierarchicalItemEnumerator : IEnumerator<IHierarchicalItem>, IDisposable, IEnumerator
		{
			private readonly IList collection;

			private int currentIndex;

			public object Current => collection[currentIndex];

			IHierarchicalItem IEnumerator<IHierarchicalItem>.Current => (IHierarchicalItem)Current;

			public HierarchicalItemEnumerator(IList collection)
			{
				this.collection = collection;
				Reset();
			}

			public void Dispose()
			{
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				currentIndex = -1;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				return ++currentIndex < collection.Count;
			}
		}

		private HierarchicalElement owner;

		private HierarchicalItemEnumerator enumerator;

		HierarchicalElement IOwnedElement.Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return owner;
			}
			set
			{
				SetOwnerForChildren(this, null);
				owner = value;
				SetOwnerForChildren(this, owner);
			}
		}

		bool ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.IsReadOnly => false;

		IHierarchicalItem IList<IHierarchicalItem>.this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).InnerList[index];
			}
			set
			{
				((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).InnerList[index] = (T)value;
			}
		}

		void ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Add(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).Add((T)item);
		}

		bool ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Contains(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			return ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).Contains((T)item);
		}

		void ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.CopyTo(IHierarchicalItem[] array, int arrayIndex)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = arrayIndex;
			while (num < ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).Count)
			{
				array[num2] = ((DXCollection<T>)(object)this)[num];
				num++;
				num2++;
			}
		}

		bool ICollection<IHierarchicalItem>.Remove(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			return ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).Remove((T)item);
		}

		int IList<IHierarchicalItem>.IndexOf(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			return ((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).InnerList.IndexOf((T)item);
		}

		void IList<IHierarchicalItem>.Insert(int index, IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).Insert(index, (T)item);
		}

		IEnumerator<IHierarchicalItem> IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem>.GetEnumerator()
		{
			if (enumerator == null)
			{
				enumerator = new HierarchicalItemEnumerator((IList)this);
			}
			enumerator.Reset();
			return enumerator;
		}

		protected HierarchicalItemCollectionBase()
			: base((DXCollectionUniquenessProviderType)3)
		{
		}

		private void SetOwnerForItem(IHierarchicalItem item, HierarchicalElement owner)
		{
			((IOwnedElement)item).Owner = owner;
			SetOwnerForChildren(item.Children, owner);
		}

		private void SetOwnerForChildren(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> children, HierarchicalElement owner)
		{
			foreach (IOwnedElement child in children)
			{
				child.Owner = owner;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(CollectionChangedEventArgs<T> e)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (e.Element != null && ((int)e.Action == 1 || (int)e.Action == 3))
			{
				SetOwnerForItem(e.Element, null);
			}
			if (e.Element != null && ((int)e.Action == 0 || (int)e.Action == 2))
			{
				SetOwnerForItem(e.Element, owner);
			}
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
		}

		internal void AddInternal(T item)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this).InnerList.Add(item);
		}

		public void AddRange(params IHierarchicalItem[] items)
		{
			base.AddRange((ICollection)items);
		}
	}

	public abstract class HierarchicalElement : IDisposable, ICloneable, IOwnedElement, IXtraSupportShouldSerialize, ISupportDesignerUpdate
	{
		private bool isDisposed;

		private HierarchicalElement owner;

		private object tag;

		protected internal HierarchicalElement Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return owner;
			}
			set
			{
				HierarchicalElement oldOwner = owner;
				owner = value;
				OnOwnerChanged(oldOwner, owner);
			}
		}

		internal bool IsDisposed => isDisposed;

		HierarchicalElement IOwnedElement.Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return Owner;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Owner != value)
				{
					Owner = value;
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual bool XtraSerializing
		{
			get
			{
				if (owner != null)
				{
					return owner.XtraSerializing;
				}
				return XtraSerializingHelper.XtraSerializing;
			}
		}

		protected HierarchicalElement()
			: this(null)
		{
		}

		protected HierarchicalElement(HierarchicalElement owner)
		{
			this.owner = owner;
		}

		~HierarchicalElement()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		private void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && !IsDisposed)
			{
				isDisposed = true;
				DisposeOverride();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DisposeOverride()
		{
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		public virtual object Clone()
		{
			HierarchicalElement hierarchicalElement = CreateObjectForCloneCore();
			hierarchicalElement.AssignCore(this);
			return hierarchicalElement;
		}

		void ISupportDesignerUpdate.RaiseDesignerControlChanged()
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanged();
		}

		void ISupportDesignerUpdate.RaiseDesignerControlChanging()
		{
			ApplyPropertyChanging();
		}

		bool IXtraSupportShouldSerialize.ShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			return XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName);
		}

		protected virtual bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == "Tag")
			{
				return XtraShouldSerializeTag();
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual bool XtraShouldSerializeTag()
		{
			return XtraShouldSerializeTag(tag);
		}

		protected bool XtraShouldSerializeTag(object tagValue)
		{
			if (tagValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tagValue as string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return XtraPropertyInfo.ShouldSerializeTag(tagValue, ObjectConverter.Instance);
		}

		private bool ShouldSerializeTag()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(tag as string);
		}

		protected internal virtual bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return ShouldSerializeTag();
		}

		protected virtual void OnOwnerChanged(HierarchicalElement oldOwner, HierarchicalElement newOwner)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void ApplyPropertyChanging()
		{
			if (owner != null)
			{
				owner.ApplyPropertyChanging();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void ApplyPropertyChanged()
		{
			if (owner != null)
			{
				owner.ApplyPropertyChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual bool ProcessChanged(UpdateType updateType)
		{
			if (owner != null)
			{
				return owner.ProcessChanged(updateType);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected void RaiseControlChanged()
		{
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.NonSpecific);
		}

		protected abstract HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore();

		protected virtual void AssignCore(HierarchicalElement obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("obj");
			}
			tag = obj.tag;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"({GetType().Name})";
		}
	}
	public abstract class TreeMapElement : HierarchicalElement
	{
		protected TreeMapElement()
			: this(null)

		}

		protected TreeMapElement(HierarchicalElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected abstract TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone();

		protected override HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore()
		{
			return CreateObjectForClone();
		}

		public virtual void Assign(TreeMapElement obj)
		{
			AssignCore(obj);
		}
	}

		}

		protected override HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore()

			return CreateObjectForClone();
		}

		{
			AssignCore(obj);
		}
	}

		}

		}

namespace DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.Native
