	public class RectangularBorderLayout
	{
		private readonly RectangularBorder border;

		private Rectangle topRect;

		private Rectangle bottomRect;

		private Rectangle leftRect;

		private Rectangle rightRect;

		public Rectangle TopRect => topRect;

		public Rectangle BottomRect => bottomRect;

		public Rectangle LeftRect => leftRect;

		public Rectangle RightRect => rightRect;

		public RectangularBorderLayout(RectangularBorder border, Rectangle rect, int actualThickness)
		{
			this.border = border;
			CalculateLayout(rect, actualThickness);
		}

		private void CalculateLayout(Rectangle rect, int actualThickness)
		{
			topRect = border.CalcBorderItem(rect, RectangularBorder.RectangularBorderItem.TopItem, actualThickness);
			bottomRect = border.CalcBorderItem(rect, RectangularBorder.RectangularBorderItem.BottomItem, actualThickness);
			leftRect = border.CalcBorderItem(rect, RectangularBorder.RectangularBorderItem.LeftItem, actualThickness);
			rightRect = border.CalcBorderItem(rect, RectangularBorder.RectangularBorderItem.RightItem, actualThickness);
		}
	}
	public sealed class BorderHelper
	{
		public static void RenderBorder(IRenderer renderer, RectangularBorder border, Rectangle rect)
		{
			if (border.Visible)
			{
				RectangularBorderLayout layout = new RectangularBorderLayout(border, rect, border.ActualThickness);
				RectangularBorder.Render(renderer, layout, border.ActualColor);
			}
		}

		public static Color CalculateBorderColor(Color borderColor, Color automaticBorderColor)
		{
			if (!borderColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				return borderColor;
			}
			return automaticBorderColor;
		}

		public static Color CalculateBorderColor(BorderBase border, Color automaticBorderColor)
		{
			return CalculateBorderColor(border.ActualColor, automaticBorderColor);
		}

		public static int CalculateBorderThickness(BorderBase border, int thickness)
		{
			if (!border.Visible)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return thickness;
		}
	}
	public interface IColorizerController
	{
		ILegendItemProvider LegendItemsProvider { get; }

		Color GetItemColor(IColorizerElement item, int groupLevel, int maxGroupLevel, int groupIndex, int index, int itemCount, double minValue, double maxValue);

		void ColorizeItems(IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> items, int maxGroupLevel);
	}
	public class ColorizerControllerBehavior
	{
		protected struct MinMax
		{
			private double min;

			private double max;

			public static MinMax Empty => new MinMax(double.NaN, double.NaN);

			public double Min => min;

			public double Max => max;

			public MinMax(double min, double max)
			{
				this.min = min;
				this.max = max;
			}

			public void ApplyValue(double value)
			{
				if (!double.IsNaN(value))
				{
					min = (double.IsNaN(min) ? value : Math.Min(value, min));
					max = (double.IsNaN(max) ? value : Math.Max(value, max));
				}
			}
		}

		protected MinMax CalculateMinMax(IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> elements, IColorizerValueProvider valueProvider)
		{
			MinMax empty = MinMax.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (IColorizerElement element in elements)
			{
				empty.ApplyValue(valueProvider?.GetValue(element.Item, num++) ?? element.Item.Value);
			}
			return empty;
		}

		public virtual int ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController, IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> items, int maxGroupLevel, int groupLevel, int groupIndex, IColorizerValueProvider valueProvider)
		{
			MinMax minMax = CalculateMinMax(items, valueProvider);
			int num = 1;
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (IColorizerElement item in items)
			{
				item.Color = colorizerController.GetItemColor(item, groupLevel, maxGroupLevel, groupIndex, num2, items.Count(), minMax.Min, minMax.Max);
				if (item.Children.Any())
				{
					num += ColorizeItems(colorizerController, item.Children, maxGroupLevel, groupLevel + 1, groupIndex + num, valueProvider);
				}
				num2++;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void ResetItemsColorizerColor(IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> items)
		{
			foreach (IColorizerElement item in items)
			{
				item.Color = Color.Empty;
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					ResetItemsColorizerColor(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class SpreadsheetColorizerControllerBehavior : ColorizerControllerBehavior
	{
		private readonly InnerHierarchicalControlBase innerControl;

		public SpreadsheetColorizerControllerBehavior(InnerHierarchicalControlBase innerControl)
		{
			this.innerControl = innerControl;
		}

		public override int ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController, IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> items, int maxGroupLevel, int groupLevel, int groupIndex, IColorizerValueProvider valueProvider)
		{
			MinMax minMax = CalculateMinMax(items, valueProvider);
			int num = 1;
			int num2 = 0;
			IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> enumerable2;
			if (!items.All((IColorizerElement item) => !item.IsGroup))
			{
				IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> enumerable = items.OrderBy((IColorizerElement item) => innerControl.GroupIndexProvider.GetGroupIndex(((IGroupValueProvider)item).GroupValue));
				enumerable2 = enumerable;
			}
			else
			{
				enumerable2 = items;
			}
			IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> enumerable3 = enumerable2;
			int itemCount = items.Count();
			foreach (IColorizerElement item in enumerable3)
			{
				item.Color = colorizerController.GetItemColor(item, groupLevel, maxGroupLevel, groupIndex, num2, itemCount, minMax.Min, minMax.Max);
				if (item.Children.Any())
				{
					num += ColorizeItems(colorizerController, item.Children, maxGroupLevel, groupLevel + 1, groupIndex++, valueProvider);
				}
				num2++;
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapColorizerController : TreeMapElement, IColorizerController
	{
		private readonly ColorizerControllerBehavior behavior;

		private readonly TreeMapPaletteColorizer defaultColorizer;

		private ITreeMapColorizer colorizer;

		private TreeMapColorizerBase colorizerBase;

		public ITreeMapColorizer Colorizer
		{
			get
			{
				return colorizer;
			}
			set
			{
				if (colorizer == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (colorizer != null)
				{
					colorizer.ColorizerChanged -= delegate
					{
						ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
					};
				}
				SetColorizer(value);
				ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
			}
		}

		ILegendItemProvider IColorizerController.LegendItemsProvider => Colorizer as ILegendItemProvider;

		public TreeMapColorizerController(HierarchicalElement innerControl, ColorizerControllerBehavior behavior)
			: base(innerControl)
		{
			defaultColorizer = new TreeMapPaletteColorizer();
			this.behavior = behavior;
			SetColorizer(defaultColorizer);
		}

		Color IColorizerController.GetItemColor(IColorizerElement item, int groupLevel, int maxGroupLevel, int groupIndex, int index, int itemCount, double minValue, double maxValue)
		{
			if (CanColorizeItem(item))
			{
				return colorizer.GetItemColor((ITreeMapItem)item.Item, new TreeMapItemGroupInfo(groupLevel, groupIndex, index, minValue, maxValue));
			}
			return Color.Empty;
		}

		private bool CanColorizeItem(IColorizerElement item)
		{
			if (item.IsGroup)
			{
				return colorizerBase == null;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void SetColorizer(ITreeMapColorizer value)
		{
			colorizer = value;
			if (colorizer != null)
			{
				colorizerBase = colorizer as TreeMapColorizerBase;
				if (colorizerBase != null)
				{
					ElementHelper.ChangeOwner(this, colorizerBase);
				}
				colorizer.ColorizerChanged += delegate
				{
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				};
			}
		}

		protected override TreeMapElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public void UpdateColorizer(Palette paletteFromSkin)
		{
			if (colorizerBase != null)
			{
				colorizerBase.UpdateDefaults(paletteFromSkin);
			}
		}

		public void ColorizeItems(IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> items, int maxGroupLevel)
		{
			if (items != null && colorizer != null && (colorizerBase == null || colorizerBase.CanColorizeItems))
			{
				behavior.ColorizeItems(this, items, maxGroupLevel, 0, 0, colorizerBase?.ValueProvider);
			}
			else
			{
				behavior.ResetItemsColorizerColor(items);
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeColorizer()
		{
			if (colorizer == defaultColorizer)
			{
				return defaultColorizer.Palette != null;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void ResetColorizer()
		{
			colorizer = defaultColorizer;
		}
	}
	public class SunburstColorizerController : SunburstElement, IColorizerController
	{
		private readonly ColorizerControllerBehavior behavior;

		private readonly SunburstPaletteColorizer defaultColorizer;

		private ISunburstColorizer colorizer;

		private SunburstColorizerBase colorizerBase;

		public ISunburstColorizer Colorizer
		{
			get
			{
				return colorizer;
			}
			set
			{
				if (colorizer == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (colorizer != null)
				{
					colorizer.ColorizerChanged -= delegate
					{
						ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
					};
				}
				SetColorizer(value);
				ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
			}
		}

		ILegendItemProvider IColorizerController.LegendItemsProvider => Colorizer as ILegendItemProvider;

		public SunburstColorizerController(HierarchicalElement innerControl, ColorizerControllerBehavior behavior)
			: base(innerControl)
		{
			defaultColorizer = new SunburstPaletteColorizer();
			this.behavior = behavior;
			SetColorizer(defaultColorizer);
		}

		Color IColorizerController.GetItemColor(IColorizerElement item, int groupLevel, int maxGroupLevel, int groupIndex, int index, int itemCount, double minValue, double maxValue)
		{
			return colorizer.GetItemColor((ISunburstItem)item.Item, new SunburstItemGroupInfo(groupLevel, maxGroupLevel, groupIndex, index, itemCount, minValue, maxValue));
		}

		private void SetColorizer(ISunburstColorizer value)
		{
			colorizer = value;
			if (colorizer != null)
			{
				colorizerBase = colorizer as SunburstColorizerBase;
				if (colorizerBase != null)
				{
					ElementHelper.ChangeOwner(this, colorizerBase);
				}
				colorizer.ColorizerChanged += delegate
				{
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
				};
			}
		}

		protected override SunburstElement CreateObjectForClone()
		{
			throw new NotSupportedException();
		}

		public void UpdateColorizer(Palette paletteFromSkin)
		{
			if (colorizerBase != null)
			{
				colorizerBase.UpdateDefaults(paletteFromSkin);
			}
		}

		public void ColorizeItems(IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> items, int maxGroupLevel)
		{
			if (items != null && colorizer != null && (colorizerBase == null || colorizerBase.CanColorizeItems))
			{
				behavior.ColorizeItems(this, items, maxGroupLevel, 0, 0, null);
			}
			else
			{
				behavior.ResetItemsColorizerColor(items);
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeColorizer()
		{
			if (!(colorizer is HierarchicalElement hierarchicalElement))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return hierarchicalElement.ShouldSerialize();
		}

		public void ResetColorizer()
		{
			colorizer = defaultColorizer;
		}
	}
	public class PaletteTypeConverter : ExpandableObjectConverter
	{
		public override PropertyDescriptorCollection GetProperties(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object value, Attribute[] attributes)
		{
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = base.GetProperties(context, value, attributes);
			PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor = properties.Find("ScaleMode", ignoreCase: false);
			return new PropertyDescriptorCollection(new PropertyDescriptor[1] { propertyDescriptor });
		}

		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			if (!(sourceType == typeof(string)))
			{
				return base.CanConvertFrom(context, sourceType);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (!(destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor)) && !(destinationType == typeof(string)))
			{
				return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("destinationType");
			}
			if (value is Palette palette && destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor))
			{
				MemberInfo memberInfo = null;
				object[] arguments = null;
				if (palette.IsPredefined)
				{
					string name = (Palettes.ListPalettes[palette] + "Palette").Replace(" ", "");
					memberInfo = typeof(Palette).GetProperty(name);
				}
				else
				{
					memberInfo = typeof(Palette).GetConstructor(new Type[1] { typeof(Color[]) });
					Color[] array = ((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)palette).ToArray();
					object[] array2 = new Color[1][] { array };
					arguments = array2;
				}
				if (memberInfo != null)
				{
					return new InstanceDescriptor(memberInfo, arguments);
				}
			}
			return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
		}
	}
	public abstract class ColorizerBehavior : LegendSupportColorizerBehavior
	{
		private IColorizerValueProvider valueProvider;

		protected ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer Colorizer { get; private set; }

		public abstract bool CanColorizeItems { get; }

		public IColorizerValueProvider ValueProvider
		{
			get
			{
				return valueProvider;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!object.Equals(valueProvider, value))
				{
					Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					valueProvider = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public string TypeNameSerializable => Colorizer.GetType().Name;

		public event ColorizerChangedEventHandler ColorizerChanged;

		public ColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer)
			: base(WinLegendItemCreator.Instance)
		{
			Colorizer = colorizer;
		}

		protected void RaiseColorizerChanged()
		{
			this.ColorizerChanged?.Invoke(this, new ColorizerChangedEventArgs());
		}

		protected abstract Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group);

		public Color GetItemColor(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return GetItemColorCore(item, group);
		}

		public virtual void UpdateDefaults(Palette paletteFromSkin)
		{
		}
	}
	public abstract class PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase : ColorizerBehavior
	{
		private Palette palette;

		private string legendItemPattern;

		public Palette ActualPalette => palette ?? base.Colorizer.DefaultPalette;

		public override bool CanColorizeItems => !ActualPalette.IsEmpty;

		public Palette Palette
		{
			get
			{
				return palette;
			}
			set
			{
				if (palette != value)
				{
					UnregisterPalette(palette);
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					palette = value;
					RegisterPalette(palette);
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public string LegendItemPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return legendItemPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				if (legendItemPattern != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					legendItemPattern = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
					UpdateLegendItemPattern(legendItemPattern);
				}
			}
		}

		public PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer)
			: base(colorizer)
		{
		}

		private void RegisterPalette(Palette palette)
		{
			if (palette != null)
			{
				((NotificationCollection<Color>)palette).CollectionChanged += OnPaletteChanged;
			}
		}

		private void UnregisterPalette(Palette palette)
		{
			if (palette != null)
			{
				((NotificationCollection<Color>)palette).CollectionChanged -= OnPaletteChanged;
			}
		}

		private void OnPaletteChanged(object sender, CollectionChangedEventArgs<Color> e)
		{
			RaiseColorizerChanged();
		}

		public virtual void Assign(PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase behavior)
		{
			Palette = behavior.Palette;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializePalette()
		{
			if (palette != null)
			{
				return palette.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeLegendItemPattern()
		{
			return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(legendItemPattern);
		}

		public virtual bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializePalette())
			{
				return ShouldSerializeLegendItemPattern();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public bool XtraShouldSerialize()
		{
			return ShouldSerialize();
		}

		public void ResetPalette()
		{
			Palette = null;
		}

		public void ResetLegendItemPattern()
		{
			legendItemPattern = null;
		}
	}
	public class DefaultColorizerBehavior : ColorizerBehavior
	{
		private static readonly DefaultColorizerBehavior instance = new DefaultColorizerBehavior();

		public static DefaultColorizerBehavior Instance => instance;

		public override bool CanColorizeItems => true;

		public DefaultColorizerBehavior()
			: base(null)
		{
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return Color.Empty;
		}
	}
	public class GradientColorizerBehavior : ColorizerBehavior
	{
		private Color startColor;

		private Color endColor;

		public override bool CanColorizeItems
		{
			get
			{
				if (startColor.IsEmpty)
				{
					return !endColor.IsEmpty;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public Color StartColor
		{
			get
			{
				return startColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (startColor != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					startColor = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public Color EndColor
		{
			get
			{
				return endColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (endColor != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					endColor = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			double num = group.MaxValue - group.MinValue;
			double num2 = ((base.ValueProvider != null) ? base.ValueProvider.GetValue(item, group.ItemIndex) : item.Value);
			if (num > 0.0 && num2 >= group.MinValue && num2 <= group.MaxValue)
			{
				double ratio = (group.MaxValue - num2) / num;
				return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(StartColor, EndColor, ratio);
			}
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				return StartColor;
			}
			return Color.Empty;
		}

		public GradientColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer)
			: base(colorizer)
		{
		}

		public void Assign(GradientColorizerBehavior behavior)
		{
			startColor = behavior.startColor;
			endColor = behavior.endColor;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeStartColor()
		{
			return startColor != Color.Empty;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeEndColor()
		{
			return endColor != Color.Empty;
		}

		public void ResetStartColor()
		{
			StartColor = Color.Empty;
		}

		public void ResetEndColor()
		{
			EndColor = Color.Empty;
		}
	}
	public class GroupGradientColorizerBehavior : PaletteColorizerBehavior
	{
		private double max;

		private double min = 0.5;

		private Color gradientColor = Color.Transparent;

		public double Min
		{
			get
			{
				return min;
			}
			set
			{
				if (min != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					min = CoerceMinMax(value);
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double Max
		{
			get
			{
				return max;
			}
			set
			{
				if (max != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					max = CoerceMinMax(value);
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public Color GradientColor
		{
			get
			{
				return gradientColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (gradientColor != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					gradientColor = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public GroupGradientColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer, double max)
			: base(colorizer, defaultVaryColorInGroup: false)
		{
			this.max = max;
		}

		private double CoerceMinMax(double value)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 1.0;
			double num3 = value;
			if (num3 > num2)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			if (num3 < num)
			{
				return num;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return CalcGradientColor((base.ValueProvider != null) ? base.ValueProvider.GetValue(item, group.ItemIndex) : item.Value, group.MaxValue, group.MinValue, group.GroupIndex, Max, -1);
		}

		protected Color CalcGradientColor(double currentValue, double maxVal, double minVal, int index, double startRatio, int sign)
		{
			double ratio = ((maxVal > minVal) ? (startRatio + (double)sign * (maxVal - currentValue) * ((Max - Min) / (maxVal - minVal))) : Max);
			return ColorizerCalculator.MixColors(GradientColor, base.ActualPalette.GetColor(index), ratio);
		}

		public override void Assign(PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase behaviorBase)
		{
			base.Assign(behaviorBase);
			if (behaviorBase is GroupGradientColorizerBehavior groupGradientColorizerBehavior)
			{
				min = groupGradientColorizerBehavior.min;
				max = groupGradientColorizerBehavior.max;
				gradientColor = groupGradientColorizerBehavior.gradientColor;
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeGradientColor()
		{
			return gradientColor != Color.Transparent;
		}

		public void ResetGradientColor()
		{
			GradientColor = Color.Transparent;
		}
	}
	public class PaletteColorizerBehavior : PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase
	{
		private readonly bool defaultVaryColorInGroup;

		private bool varyColorInGroup;

		public bool VaryColorInGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return varyColorInGroup;
			}
			set
			{
				if (varyColorInGroup != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					varyColorInGroup = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public PaletteColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer, bool defaultVaryColorInGroup)
			: base(colorizer)
		{
			this.defaultVaryColorInGroup = defaultVaryColorInGroup;
			varyColorInGroup = defaultVaryColorInGroup;
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			int num = (VaryColorInGroup ? group.GroupIndex : group.ItemIndex);
			if (base.ValueProvider == null)
			{
				return base.ActualPalette.GetColor(num);
			}
			return base.ActualPalette.GetColor((int)base.ValueProvider.GetValue(item, num));
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeVaryColorInGroup()
		{
			return varyColorInGroup != defaultVaryColorInGroup;
		}

		public void ResetVaryColorInGroup()
		{
			VaryColorInGroup = defaultVaryColorInGroup;
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!ShouldSerializeVaryColorInGroup())
			{
				return base.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override void Assign(PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase behaviorBase)
		{
			base.Assign(behaviorBase);
			if (behaviorBase is PaletteColorizerBehavior paletteColorizerBehavior)
			{
				varyColorInGroup = paletteColorizerBehavior.varyColorInGroup;
			}
		}
	}
	public class RangeColorizerBehavior : PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase
	{
		private const string defaultLegendPattern = "{V1} - {V2}";

		private readonly DoubleCollection rangeStops;

		private readonly List<double> sortedRangeStops = new List<double>();

		private Color groupColor;

		private bool shouldSortRangeStops = true;

		protected override string DefaultLegendPattern => "{V1} - {V2}";

		internal List<double> SortedRangeStops
		{
			get
			{
				CheckAndSortRangeStopsIfNessesary();
				return sortedRangeStops;
			}
		}

		public Color GroupColor
		{
			get
			{
				return groupColor;
			}
			set
			{
				if (groupColor != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					groupColor = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public DoubleCollection RangeStops => rangeStops;

		public string RangeStopsSerializable
		{
			get
			{
				return ((object)rangeStops).ToString();
			}
			set
			{
				DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection(value);
				((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)rangeStops).Clear();
				((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)rangeStops).AddRange((ICollection)doubleCollection);
				shouldSortRangeStops = true;
			}
		}

		public RangeColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer)
			: base(colorizer)
		{
			rangeStops = new DoubleCollection();
			((NotificationCollection<double>)rangeStops).CollectionChanging += OnRangeStopChanging;
			((NotificationCollection<double>)rangeStops).CollectionChanged += OnRangeStopsChanged;
		}

		private void OnRangeStopChanging(object sender, CollectionChangingEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
		}

		private void OnRangeStopsChanged(object sender, CollectionChangedEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			shouldSortRangeStops = true;
			RaiseColorizerChanged();
		}

		private void CheckAndSortRangeStopsIfNessesary()
		{
			if (shouldSortRangeStops)
			{
				sortedRangeStops.Clear();
				sortedRangeStops.AddRange(((IEnumerable<double>)RangeStops).Distinct());
				sortedRangeStops.Remove(double.NaN);
				shouldSortRangeStops = false;
			}
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			return ColorizerCalculator.GetItemColor((base.ValueProvider != null) ? base.ValueProvider.GetValue(item, group.ItemIndex) : item.Value, (IList<double>)RangeStops, base.ActualPalette);
		}

		protected override PatternDataProvider CreateDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new ValueRangeLegendItemDataProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		public void Assign(RangeColorizerBehavior behavior)
		{
			base.Assign(behavior);
			groupColor = behavior.groupColor;
			((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)rangeStops).Clear();
			((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)rangeStops).AddRange((ICollection)behavior.rangeStops);
		}

		public List<ILegendItem> CreateLegendItems()
		{
			Palette actualPalette = base.ActualPalette;
			List<ILegendItem> list = new List<ILegendItem>();
			List<double> list2 = SortedRangeStops;
			int num = list2.Count - 1;
			if (((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)actualPalette).Count == 0 || num == 0)
			{
				return list;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				double num2 = list2[i];
				double num3 = list2[i + 1];
				string text = GetText(new double[2] { num2, num3 });
				Color colorByIndex = ColorizerCalculator.GetColorByIndex(i, num, base.ActualPalette);
				list.Add(new LegendItem
				{
					Text = text,
					Colors = new Color[1] { colorByIndex }
				});
			}
			return list;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeGroupColor()
		{
			return groupColor != Color.Transparent;
		}

		public void ResetGroupColor()
		{
			GroupColor = Color.Transparent;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeRangeStops()
		{
			return ((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)rangeStops).Count > 0;
		}

		public void ResetRangeStops()
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<double>)(object)rangeStops).Clear();
		}
	}
	public class FlatDataAdapterBehavior<T> where T : IOwnedElement, IHierarchicalItem
	{
		private readonly GroupDataMemberCollection groupDataMembers;

		private readonly ISupportUpdateDataController groupController;

		private readonly IItemsHolder<T> dataAdapter;

		private string dataMember = string.Empty;

		private string valueDataMember = string.Empty;

		private string labelDataMember = string.Empty;

		public object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return groupController.DataSource;
			}
			set
			{
				if (groupController.DataSource != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					groupController.DataSource = value;
					UpdateData();
				}
			}
		}

		public string DataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return dataMember;
			}
			set
			{
				if (dataMember != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					dataMember = value;
					UpdateData();
				}
			}
		}

		public string ValueDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return valueDataMember;
			}
			set
			{
				if (valueDataMember != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					valueDataMember = value;
					UpdateData();
				}
			}
		}

		public string LabelDataMember
		{
			get
			{
				return labelDataMember;
			}
			set
			{
				if (labelDataMember != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					labelDataMember = value;
					UpdateData();
				}
			}
		}

		public GroupDataMemberCollection GroupDataMembers => groupDataMembers;

		public string GroupDataMembersSerializable
		{
			get
			{
				return ((object)groupDataMembers).ToString();
			}
			set
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					GroupDataMemberCollection groupDataMemberCollection = new GroupDataMemberCollection(value);
					((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)groupDataMembers).Clear();
					((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)groupDataMembers).AddRange((ICollection)groupDataMemberCollection);
				}
			}
		}

		public string ColorDataMember { get; set; }

		public FlatDataAdapterBehavior(IItemsHolder<T> dataAdapter, ISupportUpdateDataController groupController)
		{
			this.dataAdapter = dataAdapter;
			this.groupController = groupController;
			groupDataMembers = new GroupDataMemberCollection();
			((NotificationCollection<string>)groupDataMembers).CollectionChanged += GroupsCollectionChanged;
		}

		private void ApplyPropertyChanging()
		{
			dataAdapter.PropertyChanging();
		}

		private void GroupsCollectionChanged(object sender, CollectionChangedEventArgs<string> e)
		{
			UpdateData();
		}

		public int GetGroupIndex(object groupValue)
		{
			if (((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)GroupDataMembers).Count <= 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return groupController.GetRowIndex(((DXCollection<string>)(object)GroupDataMembers)[0], groupValue);
		}

		public void UpdateData()
		{
			((NotificationCollection<T>)dataAdapter.Items).BeginUpdate();
			try
			{
				groupController.UpdateData();
			}
			finally
			{
				((NotificationCollection<T>)dataAdapter.Items).EndUpdate();
			}
		}

		public void Assign(FlatDataAdapterBehavior<T> behavior)
		{
			DataMember = behavior.DataMember;
			ValueDataMember = behavior.ValueDataMember;
			LabelDataMember = behavior.LabelDataMember;
			((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)GroupDataMembers).Clear();
			((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)GroupDataMembers).AddRange((ICollection)behavior.GroupDataMembers);
		}
	}
	public interface ISupportUpdateHierarchicalData
	{
		void UpdateData();
	}
	public class ColumnInfos
	{
		public DataColumnInfo ValueColumn { get; }

		public DataColumnInfo LabelColumn { get; }

		public DataColumnInfo ChildrenColumn { get; }

		public DataColumnInfo ColorColumn { get; }

		public ColumnInfos(DataColumnInfo valueColumn, DataColumnInfo labelColumn, DataColumnInfo colorColumn, DataColumnInfo childrenColumn)
		{
			ValueColumn = valueColumn;
			LabelColumn = labelColumn;
			ChildrenColumn = childrenColumn;
			ColorColumn = colorColumn;
		}
	}
	public abstract class DataMappingCollection<T> : NotificationCollection<T> where T : HierarchicalDataMapping
	{
		protected override void OnInsertComplete(int index, T value)
		{
			((INotifyPropertyChanged)value).PropertyChanged += ValuePropertyChanged;
			base.OnInsertComplete(index, value);
		}

		private void ValuePropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NotificationCollection<T>)this).RaiseCollectionChanged(new CollectionChangedEventArgs<T>((CollectionChangedAction)2, (T)sender));
		}

		protected override void OnRemoveComplete(int index, T value)
		{
			((INotifyPropertyChanged)value).PropertyChanged -= ValuePropertyChanged;
			base.OnRemoveComplete(index, value);
		}

		protected override bool OnClear()
		{
			foreach (T item in (DXCollectionBase<T>)(object)this)
			{
				((INotifyPropertyChanged)item).PropertyChanged -= ValuePropertyChanged;
			}
			return base.OnClear();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapDataMappingCollection : DataMappingCollection<TreeMapHierarchicalDataMapping>
	{
	}
	public class SunburstDataMappingCollection : DataMappingCollection<SunburstHierarchicalDataMapping>
	{
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalDataController<T> : ListSourceDataController, IDataControllerData where T : class, IOwnedElement, IHierarchicalItem, new()
	{
		private readonly ISupportUpdateHierarchicalData behavior;

		private Type previousDataType;

		public HierarchicalDataController(ISupportUpdateHierarchicalData behavior)
		{
			this.behavior = behavior;
			((DataControllerBase)this).DataClient = (IDataControllerData)(object)this;
		}

		UnboundColumnInfoCollection IDataControllerData.GetUnboundColumns()
		{
			return null;
		}

		object IDataControllerData.GetUnboundData(int row, DataColumnInfo col, object value)
		{
			return null;
		}

		void IDataControllerData.SetUnboundData(int row, DataColumnInfo col, object value)
		{
		}

		private void LoadItems(HierarchicalItemCollectionBase<T> items, HierarchicalDataMapping[] mappings, int level, Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos> columns)
		{
			Dictionary<T, IList> dictionary = null;
			level++;
			int listSourceRowCount = ((DataControllerBase)this).ListSourceRowCount;
			for (int i = 0; i < listSourceRowCount; i++)
			{
				object row = ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRow(i, (OperationCompleted)null, false);
				if (row == null || !GetColumns(columns, GetMappingByType(row.GetType(), mappings), out var valueColumn, out var labelColumn, out var colorColumn, out var childrenColumn))
				{
					continue;
				}
				object value = ((valueColumn != null) ? ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValue(i, valueColumn, (OperationCompleted)null, false) : null);
				double value2 = Convert.ToDouble(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				object labelObject = ((labelColumn != null) ? ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValue(i, labelColumn, (OperationCompleted)null, false) : null);
				object color = ((colorColumn != null) ? ((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValue(i, colorColumn, (OperationCompleted)null, false) : null);
				T val = CreateItem(value2, labelObject, row, i, color, items);
				items.AddInternal(val);
				if (childrenColumn == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				IList dataSource = MasterDetailHelper.GetDataSource(((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.GetRowValueDetail(i, childrenColumn), (string)null);
				if (dataSource != null && dataSource.Count > 0)
				{
					if (dictionary == null)
					{
						dictionary = new Dictionary<T, IList>();
					}
					dictionary.Add(val, dataSource);
				}
			}
			if (dictionary == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<T, IList> item in dictionary)
			{
				UpdateData(item.Value, GetChildren(item.Key), mappings, level, columns);
			}
		}

		private bool GetColumns(Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos> columnsCache, HierarchicalDataMapping mapping, out DataColumnInfo valueColumn, out DataColumnInfo labelColumn, out DataColumnInfo colorColumn, out DataColumnInfo childrenColumn)
		{
			if (mapping == null)
			{
				valueColumn = null;
				labelColumn = null;
				childrenColumn = null;
				colorColumn = null;
				return false;
			}
			if (!columnsCache.TryGetValue(mapping.Type, out var value))
			{
				valueColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.ValueDataMember);
				labelColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.LabelDataMember);
				childrenColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.ChildrenDataMember);
				colorColumn = GetColumnInfo(mapping.ColorDataMember);
				columnsCache.Add(mapping.Type, new ColumnInfos(valueColumn, labelColumn, colorColumn, childrenColumn));
			}
			else
			{
				valueColumn = value.ValueColumn;
				labelColumn = value.LabelColumn;
				colorColumn = value.ColorColumn;
				childrenColumn = value.ChildrenColumn;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private DataColumnInfo GetColumnInfo(string dataMember)
		{
			DataColumnInfo val = ((DataControllerBase)this).Columns[dataMember];
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val;
			}
			return ((DataControllerBase)this).DetailColumns[dataMember];
		}

		private HierarchicalDataMapping GetMappingByType(Type type, HierarchicalDataMapping[] mappings)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < mappings.Length; i++)
			{
				if (type == mappings[i].Type)
				{
					return mappings[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override void OnListSourceChanged()
		{
			Type type = ((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource[0].GetType();
			if (!(previousDataType == type))
			{
				previousDataType = type;
				((DataControllerBase)this).OnListSourceChanged();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnBindingListChanged(ListChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((DataController)this).OnBindingListChanged(e);
			behavior.UpdateData();
		}

		public void UpdateData(IList listSource, HierarchicalItemCollectionBase<T> items, HierarchicalDataMapping[] mappings, int level, Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos> columns)
		{
			if (((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource != null)
			{
				((DataControllerBase)this).UnsubscribeListChanged(((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.NotificationProvider, (object)((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource);
			}
			((ListSourceDataController)this).ListSource = listSource;
			if (listSource != null)
			{
				((DataControllerBase)this).SubscribeListChanged(((DataControllerBase)this).Helper.NotificationProvider, (object)listSource);
			}
			LoadItems(items, mappings, level, columns);
		}

		protected abstract T CreateItem(double value, object labelObject, object sourceObject, int groupIndex, object color, IOwnedElement ownedCollection);

		protected abstract HierarchicalItemCollectionBase<T> GetChildren(T item);
	}
	public class TreeMapHierarchicalDataController : HierarchicalDataController<TreeMapItem>

	public abstract class HierarchicalDataAdapterBehavior<TItem, TMapping> : ISupportUpdateHierarchicalData where TItem : class, IOwnedElement, IHierarchicalItem, new() where TMapping : HierarchicalDataMapping
	{
		private readonly IItemsHolder<TItem> dataAdapter;

		private readonly DataMappingCollection<TMapping> mappings;

		private readonly HierarchicalDataController<TItem> dataController;

		private object dataSource;

		public object DataSource
		{
			get
			{
				return dataSource;
			}
			set
			{
				if (dataSource != value)
				{
					dataAdapter.PropertyChanging();
					dataSource = value;
					UpdateData();
				}
			}
		}

		public DataMappingCollection<TMapping> Mappings => mappings;

		public HierarchicalDataAdapterBehavior(IItemsHolder<TItem> dataAdapter)
		{
			this.dataAdapter = dataAdapter;
			dataController = CreateDataController(this);
			mappings = CreateMappings();
			((NotificationCollection<?>)(object)mappings).CollectionChanged += delegate
			{
				UpdateData();
			};
		}

		private void UpdateDataCore(object dataSource, HierarchicalItemCollectionBase<TItem> items, int level)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<TItem>)(object)items).Clear();
			IList list = MasterDetailHelper.GetDataSource(dataSource, (string)null);
			if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
			{
				HierarchicalDataController<TItem> hierarchicalDataController = dataController;
				HierarchicalDataMapping[] array = ((DXCollectionBase<?>)(object)mappings).ToArray();
				hierarchicalDataController.UpdateData(list, items, array, level, new Dictionary<Type, ColumnInfos>());
			}
		}

		protected abstract HierarchicalDataController<TItem> CreateDataController(ISupportUpdateHierarchicalData behavior);

		protected abstract DataMappingCollection<TMapping> CreateMappings();

		public void UpdateData()
		{
			UpdateDataCore(dataSource, dataAdapter.Items, 0);
			dataAdapter.PropertyChanged(UpdateType.Data);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeMappings()
		{
			return ((DXCollectionBase<?>)(object)mappings).Count > 0;
		}

		public void ResetMappings()
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<?>)(object)mappings).Clear();
		}

		public void Assign(HierarchicalDataAdapterBehavior<TItem, TMapping> behavior)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<?>)(object)mappings).Clear();
			((DXCollectionBase<?>)(object)mappings).AddRange((ICollection)behavior.mappings);
		}

		public int GetGroupIndex(object groupValue)
		{
			if (!(groupValue is int))
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return (int)groupValue;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			((DataControllerBase)dataController).Dispose();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapterBehavior : HierarchicalDataAdapterBehavior<TreeMapItem, TreeMapHierarchicalDataMapping>
	{
		public TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapterBehavior(IItemsHolder<TreeMapItem> dataAdapter)
			: base(dataAdapter)
		{
		}

		protected override HierarchicalDataController<TreeMapItem> CreateDataController(ISupportUpdateHierarchicalData behavior)
		{
			return new TreeMapHierarchicalDataController(behavior);
		}

		protected override DataMappingCollection<TreeMapHierarchicalDataMapping> CreateMappings()
		{
			return new TreeMapDataMappingCollection();
		}
	}

	public abstract class DataController : HierarchicalElement
	{
		public abstract IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> Items { get; }

		public DataController(HierarchicalElement treeMap)
			: base(treeMap)
		{
		}

		private void SetOwnerForItems(HierarchicalElement owner)
		{
			foreach (IHierarchicalItem item in Items)
			{
				if (item is IOwnedElement ownedElement)
				{
					ownedElement.Owner = owner;
				}
			}
		}

		protected void OnDataAdapterDataChanged(object sender, DataAdapterChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender != null && !(sender is IOwnedElement) && Items != null)
			{
				SetOwnerForItems(base.Owner);
			}
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Data);
		}

		protected void ChangeAdapterOwner(IOwnedElement ownedElement, HierarchicalElement owner)
		{
			if (ownedElement != null)
			{
				ownedElement.Owner = owner;
			}
			else
			{
				SetOwnerForItems(owner);
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapDataController : DataController
	{
		private ITreeMapDataAdapter dataAdapter;

		public ITreeMapDataAdapter DataAdapter
		{
			get
			{
				return dataAdapter;
			}
			set
			{
				if (dataAdapter != value)
				{
					if (dataAdapter != null)
					{
						UnregisterDataAdapter(dataAdapter);
					}
					dataAdapter = value;
					RegisterDataAdapter(dataAdapter);
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Data);
				}
			}
		}

		public override IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> Items
		{
			get
			{
				if (dataAdapter == null || dataAdapter.Items == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return dataAdapter.Items;
			}
		}

		public TreeMapDataController(HierarchicalElement treeMap)
			: base(treeMap)
		{
		}

		private void RegisterDataAdapter(ITreeMapDataAdapter adapter)
		{
			if (adapter != null)
			{
				ChangeAdapterOwner(adapter as IOwnedElement, base.Owner);
				adapter.DataAdapterChanged += base.OnDataAdapterDataChanged;
			}
		}

		private void UnregisterDataAdapter(ITreeMapDataAdapter adapter)
		{
			ChangeAdapterOwner(adapter as IOwnedElement, null);
			adapter.DataAdapterChanged -= base.OnDataAdapterDataChanged;
		}

		protected override HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore()
		{
			return new TreeMapDataController(null);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeDataAdapter()
		{
			return dataAdapter != null;
		}

		public void ResetDataAdapter()
		{
			dataAdapter = null;
		}
	}
	public class SunburstDataController : DataController
	{
		private ISunburstDataAdapter dataAdapter;

		public ISunburstDataAdapter DataAdapter
		{
			get
			{
				return dataAdapter;

			set

						UnregisterDataAdapter(dataAdapter);

					dataAdapter = value;
					RegisterDataAdapter(dataAdapter);
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Data);
				}
			}
		}

		public override IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> Items
		{
			get
			{
				if (dataAdapter == null || dataAdapter.Items == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return dataAdapter.Items;
			}
		}

		public SunburstDataController(HierarchicalElement treeMap)
			: base(treeMap)

		}

			{
				ChangeAdapterOwner(adapter as IOwnedElement, base.Owner);
				adapter.DataAdapterChanged += base.OnDataAdapterDataChanged;
			}
		}

		private void UnregisterDataAdapter(ISunburstDataAdapter adapter)
		{
			ChangeAdapterOwner(adapter as IOwnedElement, null);
			adapter.DataAdapterChanged -= base.OnDataAdapterDataChanged;
		}

		protected override HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore()
		{
			return new TreeMapDataController(null);
		}
	}
	public class DXRenderer : IRenderer
	{
		private readonly Stack<DXSmoothingMode> smoothingCache = new Stack<DXSmoothingMode>();

		private IDXGraphics context;

		private DXStringFormat stringFormat;

		private object graphicsForItem;

		private DXStringFormat GetStringFormat(DXStringAlignment horizontalAlignment, DXStringAlignment verticalAlignment = DXStringAlignment.Near)
		{
			if (stringFormat == null)
			{
				stringFormat = DXStringFormat.CreateGenericDefault();
				stringFormat.FormatFlags = DXStringFormatFlags.NoWrap | DXStringFormatFlags.LineLimit | DXStringFormatFlags.NoClip;
			}
			stringFormat.Alignment = horizontalAlignment;
			stringFormat.LineAlignment = verticalAlignment;
			return stringFormat;
		}

		private void InitTextImageGraphics(IDXGraphics gr)
		{
			gr.TextRenderingHint = DXTextRenderingHint.AntiAliasGridFit;
			gr.SmoothingMode = DXSmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
			gr.InterpolationMode = DXInterpolationMode.High;
		}

		public void UpdateContext(IDXGraphics graphics, bool useAntialiasing)
		{
			context = graphics;
			if (context != null && useAntialiasing)
			{
				graphics.SmoothingMode = DXSmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
			}
		}

		public void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, DXBrush brush)
		{
			context.FillRectangle(brush, bounds);
		}

		public void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, Color color)
		{
			using DXBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(color);
			FillRectangle(bounds, brush);
		}

		public void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, DXHatchStyle hatch, Color color, Color backColor)
		{
			using DXBrush brush = new DXHatchBrush(hatch, color, backColor);
			context.FillRectangle(brush, bounds);
		}

		public void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, DXHatchStyle hatch, Color color)
		{
			FillRectangle(bounds, hatch, color, Color.Transparent);
		}

		public void DrawRectangle(Rectangle bounds, Color color, float thickness)
		{
			RectangleF[] rects = new RectangleF[4]
			{
				new RectangleF(bounds.Location, new SizeF(bounds.Width, thickness)),
				new RectangleF(new PointF(bounds.X, (float)bounds.Y + thickness), new SizeF(thickness, (float)bounds.Height - 2f * thickness)),
				new RectangleF(new PointF(bounds.X, (float)bounds.Bottom - thickness), new SizeF(bounds.Width, thickness)),
				new RectangleF(new PointF((float)bounds.Right - thickness, (float)bounds.Y + thickness), new SizeF(thickness, (float)bounds.Height - 2f * thickness))
			};
			using DXBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(color);
			context.FillRectangles(brush, rects);
		}

		public void DrawBoundedText(string text, DXFont font, Color color, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, DXStringAlignment lineAlignment, DXTextRenderingHint textRenderingHint)
		{
			Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(MathUtils.StrongRound(bounds.X), MathUtils.StrongRound(bounds.Y), MathUtils.Ceiling(bounds.Width), MathUtils.Ceiling(bounds.Height));
			DXTextRenderingHint textRenderingHint2 = context.TextRenderingHint;
			context.TextRenderingHint = textRenderingHint;
			DXStringFormat dXStringFormat = new DXStringFormat();
			dXStringFormat.Alignment = alignment;
			dXStringFormat.LineAlignment = lineAlignment;
			dXStringFormat.TabStops = TextMeasurer.TabStops;
			using (DXBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(color))
			{
				context.DrawString(text, TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(font), brush, rectangle, dXStringFormat);
			}
			context.TextRenderingHint = textRenderingHint2;
		}

		public void DrawGlowText(string text, Color textColor, Color glowTextColor, DXFont font, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, DXStringAlignment verticalAlignment, float strokeSize)
		{
			Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(MathUtils.StrongRound(bounds.X), MathUtils.StrongRound(bounds.Y), MathUtils.Ceiling(bounds.Width), MathUtils.Ceiling(bounds.Height));
			graphicsForItem = context.Save();
			InitTextImageGraphics(context);
			using (DXGraphicsPath dXGraphicsPath = new DXGraphicsPath())
			{
				font = TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(font);
				float num = ((context.DpiY > 0f) ? context.DpiY : 96f);
				float newSize = num * font.Size / 72f;
				DXFont font2 = new DXFont(font, newSize);
				DXStringFormat dXStringFormat = GetStringFormat(alignment, verticalAlignment);
				dXGraphicsPath.AddString(text, font2, rectangle, dXStringFormat);
				using (DXPen pen = new DXPen(glowTextColor, strokeSize)
				{
					LineJoin = DXLineJoin.Round
				})
				{
					context.DrawPath(pen, dXGraphicsPath);
				}
				using DXSolidBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(textColor);
				context.FillPath(brush, dXGraphicsPath);
			}
			context.Restore(graphicsForItem);
		}

		public void DrawGlowTextRotated(string text, Color textColor, Color glowTextColor, DXFont font, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, float strokeSize, float angle)
		{
			Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(MathUtils.StrongRound(bounds.X), MathUtils.StrongRound(bounds.Y), MathUtils.Ceiling(bounds.Width), MathUtils.Ceiling(bounds.Height));
			graphicsForItem = context.Save();
			InitTextImageGraphics(context);
			angle = Math.Abs(angle % 360f);
			if (angle > 90f && angle < 270f)
			{
				angle += 180f;
			}
			string[] array = text.Split('\n');
			using (DXGraphicsPath dXGraphicsPath = new DXGraphicsPath())
			{
				DXMatrix dXMatrix = new DXMatrix();
				dXMatrix.RotateAt(angle, new PointF(bounds.X + 0.5f * bounds.Width, bounds.Y + 0.5f * bounds.Height));
				font = TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(font);
				float num = ((context.DpiY > 0f) ? context.DpiY : 96f);
				float newSize = num * font.Size / 72f;
				DXFont font2 = new DXFont(font, newSize);
				DXStringFormat dXStringFormat = GetStringFormat(alignment);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					dXGraphicsPath.AddString(array[i].Trim('\r'), font2, rectangle, dXStringFormat);
					rectangle.Y += font.Height;
				}
				dXGraphicsPath.Transform(dXMatrix);
				using (DXPen pen = new DXPen(glowTextColor, strokeSize)
				{
					LineJoin = DXLineJoin.Round
				})
				{
					context.DrawPath(pen, dXGraphicsPath);
				}
				using DXSolidBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(textColor);
				context.FillPath(brush, dXGraphicsPath);
			}
			context.Restore(graphicsForItem);
		}

		public void DrawHVText(string text, Color textColor, DXFont font, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, DockStyleCore textDockStyle)
		{
			object state = context.Save();
			InitTextImageGraphics(context);
			DXStringFormat format = GetStringFormat(alignment);
			if (textDockStyle == DockStyleCore.Left || textDockStyle == DockStyleCore.Right)
			{
				DXMatrix dXMatrix = new DXMatrix();
				if (textDockStyle == DockStyleCore.Left)
				{
					dXMatrix.RotateAt(-270f, new PointF(bounds.X + bounds.Height * 0.5f, bounds.Y + bounds.Height * 0.5f));
					dXMatrix.RotateAt(180f, new PointF(bounds.X + bounds.Width * 0.5f, bounds.Y + bounds.Height * 0.5f));
				}
				else
				{
					dXMatrix.RotateAt(90f, new PointF(bounds.X + bounds.Height * 0.5f, bounds.Y + bounds.Height * 0.5f));
				}
				context.Transform = dXMatrix;
			}
			using (DXSolidBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(textColor))
			{
				context.DrawString(text, TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(font), brush, bounds, format);
			}
			context.Restore(state);
		}

		public void DrawImage(DXImage image, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			if (image != null)
			{
				context.DrawImage(image, bounds);
			}
		}

		public void FillPath(Color color, DXGraphicsPath path)
		{
			using DXSolidBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(color);
			context.FillPath(brush, path);
		}

		public void DrawPath(DXPen pen, DXGraphicsPath path)
		{
			context.DrawPath(pen, path);
		}

		public void FillPath(DXGraphicsPath path, DXHatchStyle hatch, Color backColor, Color color)
		{
			using DXBrush brush = new DXHatchBrush(hatch, color, backColor);
			context.FillPath(brush, path);
		}

		public void FillEllipse(Rectangle bounds, Color color)
		{
			using DXSolidBrush brush = new DXSolidBrush(color);
			context.FillEllipse(brush, bounds);
		}

		public void SetSmoothingMode(DXSmoothingMode smoothingMode)
		{
			smoothingCache.Push(context.SmoothingMode);
			context.SmoothingMode = smoothingMode;
		}

		public void RestoreSmoothingMode()
		{
			if (smoothingCache.Count > 0)
			{
				context.SmoothingMode = smoothingCache.Pop();
			}
		}
	}
	public class EmptyTreeMapDpiProvider : ITreeMapDpiProvider
	{
		private static EmptyTreeMapDpiProvider instance;

		private readonly SizeF defaultSize = new SizeF(1f, 1f);

		public static EmptyTreeMapDpiProvider Instance
		{
			get
			{
				if (instance == null)
				{
					instance = new EmptyTreeMapDpiProvider();
				}
				return instance;
			}
		}

		SizeF ITreeMapDpiProvider.DpiScaleFactor => defaultSize;

		private EmptyTreeMapDpiProvider()
		{
		}

		DXFont ITreeMapDpiProvider.ScaleFont(DXFont font)
		{
			return font;
		}
	}
	public class TextMeasurer : IDisposable
	{
		private const int CacheLimit = 100;

		public const float TabStop = 20f;

		public static readonly float[] TabStops = new float[1] { 20f };

		private readonly DXBitmap bitmap;

		private readonly IDXGraphics graphics;

		private readonly Dictionary<DXFont, float> fontSymbolWidhtCache = new Dictionary<DXFont, float>();

		private readonly ITreeMapDpiProvider dpiProvider;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static TextMeasurer instance = null;

		public static TextMeasurer Default
		{
			get
			{
				if (instance == null)
				{
					RecreateDefault();
				}
				return instance;
			}
		}

		public static void RecreateDefault(ITreeMapDpiProvider fontScaleProvider = null)
		{
			if (instance != null)
			{
				instance.Dispose();
			}
			instance = new TextMeasurer(fontScaleProvider);
		}

		private TextMeasurer(ITreeMapDpiProvider dpiProvider)
		{
			bitmap = new DXBitmap(100, 100);
			graphics = bitmap.CreateGraphics();
			this.dpiProvider = dpiProvider;
		}

		~TextMeasurer()
		{
			DisposeCore();
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			DisposeCore();
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		private void DisposeCore()
		{
			graphics?.Dispose();
			bitmap?.Dispose();
		}

		public SizeF MeasureString(string str, DXFont font, int availableWidth, DXStringTrimming stringTrimming)
		{
			font = ScaleFont(font);
			DXStringFormat dXStringFormat = new DXStringFormat
			{
				Trimming = stringTrimming
			};
			dXStringFormat.TabStops = TabStops;
			return graphics.MeasureString(str, font, availableWidth, dXStringFormat);
		}

		public SizeF MeasureString(string str, DXFont font)
		{
			return MeasureString(str, font, 999999, DXStringTrimming.Character);
		}

		public SizeF MeasureString(string str, DXFont font, DXStringAlignment alignment, DXStringAlignment lineAlignment)
		{
			font = ScaleFont(font);
			DXStringFormat dXStringFormat = new DXStringFormat();
			dXStringFormat.Alignment = alignment;
			dXStringFormat.LineAlignment = lineAlignment;
			return graphics.MeasureString(str, font, 2.1474836E+09f, dXStringFormat);
		}

		public float GetSymbolWidth(DXFont font, DXStringAlignment horizontalAlignment, DXStringAlignment verticalAlignment)
		{
			DXFont key = ScaleFont(font);
			if (!fontSymbolWidhtCache.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
			{
				if (fontSymbolWidhtCache.Count == 100)
				{
					fontSymbolWidhtCache.Clear();
				}
				value = MeasureString("a", font, horizontalAlignment, verticalAlignment).Width;
				fontSymbolWidhtCache[key] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public DXFont ScaleFont(DXFont font)
		{
			if (dpiProvider == null)
			{
				return font;
			}
			return dpiProvider.ScaleFont(font);
		}
	}
	public interface IRenderer
	{
		void UpdateContext(IDXGraphics graphics, bool useAntiAliasing);

		void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, Color color);

		void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, DXBrush brush);

		void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, DXHatchStyle hatch, Color color, Color backColor);

		void FillRectangle(Rectangle bounds, DXHatchStyle hatch, Color color);

		void DrawRectangle(Rectangle bounds, Color color, float thickness);

		void DrawBoundedText(string text, DXFont font, Color color, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, DXStringAlignment lineAlignment, DXTextRenderingHint textRenderingHint);

		void DrawGlowText(string text, Color textColor, Color glowTextColor, DXFont font, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, DXStringAlignment verticalAlignment, float strokeSize);

		void DrawGlowTextRotated(string text, Color textColor, Color glowTextColor, DXFont nativeFont, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, float strokeSize, float angle);

		void DrawHVText(string text, Color textColor, DXFont font, RectangleF bounds, DXStringAlignment alignment, DockStyleCore textDockStyle);

		void DrawImage(DXImage image, Rectangle bounds);

		void FillPath(Color color, DXGraphicsPath path);

		void FillPath(DXGraphicsPath path, DXHatchStyle hatch, Color backColor, Color color);

		void FillEllipse(Rectangle bounds, Color color);

		void DrawPath(DXPen pen, DXGraphicsPath path);

		void RestoreSmoothingMode();

		void SetSmoothingMode(DXSmoothingMode smoothingMode);
	}
	public class HierarchicalItemBehavior : IGroupValueProvider, IXtraSupportDeserializeCollectionItem
	{
		private readonly ISupportPropertyChanged item;

		private readonly IList<IHierarchicalItem> children;

		private double value;

		private object label;

		private object groupValue;

		private object tag;

		private double childrenValue = double.NaN;

		public IList<IHierarchicalItem> Children => children;

		public string TypeNameSerializable => item.GetType().Name;

		public object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return tag;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != tag)
				{
					tag = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public object Label
		{
			get
			{
				return label;
			}
			set
			{
				item.PropertyChanging();
				label = value;
				item.PropertyChanged(UpdateType.Data);
			}
		}

		public double Value
		{
			get
			{
				return value;
			}
			set
			{
				if (this.value != value)
				{
					item.PropertyChanging();
					this.value = value;
					item.PropertyChanged(UpdateType.Data);
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsGroup => children.Count > 0;

		public object Color { get; }

		public object GroupValue => groupValue ?? item;

		public HierarchicalItemBehavior(ISupportPropertyChanged item, IList<IHierarchicalItem> children, double value, object label, object tag, object groupValue, object color)
		{
			this.item = item;
			this.children = children;
			this.value = value;
			this.label = label;
			this.tag = tag;
			this.groupValue = groupValue;
			Color = color;
		}

		object IXtraSupportDeserializeCollectionItem.CreateCollectionItem(string propertyName, XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			return XtraSerializingUtils.GetKnownContentPropertyInstance(e, SerializationUtils.TreeMapAssembly, SerializationUtils.PublicNamespace);
		}

		void IXtraSupportDeserializeCollectionItem.SetIndexCollectionItem(string propertyName, XtraSetItemIndexEventArgs e)
		{
			children.Add((IHierarchicalItem)((XtraItemEventArgs)e).Item.Value);
		}

		public double CalcTotalValue()
		{
			if (Value > 0.0 || children.Count == 0)
			{
				return Value;
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(childrenValue))
			{
				return childrenValue;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (IHierarchicalItem child in children)
			{
				num += child.Value;
			}
			childrenValue = num;
			return num;
		}
	}
	[SerializationContext(typeof(TreeMapSerializationContext))]

		private IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> ActualItems => View.InteractiveItems;

		private IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestableElements => View.HitTestableItems;

		internal ViewController ViewController => viewController;

		protected internal HitTestController HitTestController => hitTestController;

		protected internal InteractionController InteractionController => interactionController;

		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]

		SizeF IAppearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor => dpiProvider.DpiScaleFactor;

		public InnerHierarchicalControlBase()
			: this(new EmptyTreeMapContainer())
		{
		}

		public InnerHierarchicalControlBase(ITreeMapContainer container, bool isSpreadsheetContainer = false)
		{
			this.container = container;
			IsSpreadsheetContainer = isSpreadsheetContainer;
			interactionController = CreateInteractionController();
			groupInfoManager = new GroupInfoManager(this);
			selectionController = CreateSelectionController();
			hitTestController = CreateHitTestController();
			borderOptions = CreateBorderOptions();
			Printer = new InnerHierarchicalControlPrinter(this);
			IRenderer renderer = new DXRenderer();
			viewController = new ViewController(renderer, this);
			View = CreateView(renderer);
			DataController = CreateDataController();
			SetClientSize(container.ClientSize);
			Titles = new TitleCollection();
			Legends = new LegendCollection();
			SubscribeEvents();
			SetEventHandlerInternal(null);
		}

		public void BeginInit()
		{
			viewController.LockUpdate();
		}

		public void EndInit()
		{
			viewController.UnLockUpdate();
		}

		void ISupportSelectionContainer.RaiseSelectionChanged()
		{
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.NonSpecific);
			if (Container.EventsListener != null)
			{
				Container.EventsListener.OnSelectionChanged(new SelectionChangedEventArgs(SelectedItems, SelectedGroups));
			}
		}

		PatternDataProvider IPatternHolder.GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new ToolTipPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		object IXtraSupportCreateContentPropertyValue.Create(XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!NeedToCreateContentPropertyValue(e))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return XtraSerializingUtils.GetKnownContentPropertyInstance(e, SerializationUtils.TreeMapAssembly, SerializationUtils.PublicNamespace);
		}

		void IXtraSupportAfterDeserialize.AfterDeserialize(XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			AfterDeserializeCore(e);
		}

		void IBasePrintable.Initialize(PrintingSystemBase ps, LinkBase link)
		{
			Printer.Initialize(ps, link);
		}

		void IBasePrintable.Finalize(PrintingSystemBase ps, LinkBase link)
		{
			Printer.Finalize(ps, link);
			Printer.Dispose();
			Printer = null;
		}

		void IBasePrintable.CreateArea(string areaName, BrickGraphics graph)
		{
			Printer.CreateArea(areaName, graph);
		}

		IList<ILegendItem> IViewDataProvider.ObtainLegendItems(LegendBase legend)
		{
			return ObtainLegendItems();
		}

		private void DrawContentCore(IDXGraphics graphics, Rectangle bounds, bool drawToMetaFile)
		{
			viewController.Render(bounds, graphics, drawToMetaFile);
		}

		private void SubscribeEvents()
		{
			((NotificationCollection<Title>)Titles).CollectionChanged += OnTitleCollectionChanged;
			((NotificationCollection<LegendBase>)Legends).CollectionChanged += OnLegendCollectionChanged;
		}

		private void UnsubscribeEvents()
		{
			((NotificationCollection<Title>)Titles).CollectionChanged -= OnTitleCollectionChanged;
			((NotificationCollection<LegendBase>)Legends).CollectionChanged -= OnLegendCollectionChanged;
		}

		private void OnTitleCollectionChanged(object sender, CollectionChangedEventArgs<Title> e)
		{
			EventHandler.OnTitlesChanged(e);
		}

		private void OnLegendCollectionChanged(object sender, CollectionChangedEventArgs<LegendBase> e)
		{
			EventHandler.OnLegendsChanged(e);
		}

		private ILegendItemProvider GetActualLegendItemsProvider()
		{
			return ColorizerControllerCore.LegendItemsProvider;
		}

		private IList<ILegendItem> ObtainLegendItems()
		{
			IList<ILegendItem> list = GetActualLegendItemsProvider()?.CreateLegendItems(View.LegendItemsSourceHolder);
			return list ?? new ILegendItem[0];
		}

		protected abstract HitTestController CreateHitTestController();

		protected abstract InteractionController CreateInteractionController();

		protected abstract InsideRectangularBorder CreateBorderOptions();

		protected abstract SelectionController CreateSelectionController();

		protected abstract IHierarchicalView CreateView(IRenderer renderer);

		protected abstract DataController CreateDataController();

		protected ColorizerControllerBehavior CreateColorizerControllerBehavior()
		{
			if (!IsSpreadsheetContainer)
			{
				return new ColorizerControllerBehavior();
			}
			return new SpreadsheetColorizerControllerBehavior(this);
		}

		protected virtual bool NeedToCreateContentPropertyValue(XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected virtual void AfterDeserializeCore(XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected void UpdateInteractionController()
		{
			interactionController.UpdateHighlightedElement(null);
			interactionController = CreateInteractionController();
		}

		protected override bool XtraShouldSerialize(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyName == "Padding")
			{
				return Padding.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return base.XtraShouldSerialize(propertyName);
		}

		protected override void DisposeOverride()
		{
			UnsubscribeEvents();
			eventHandler = null;
			viewController.Dispose();
			base.DisposeOverride();
		}

		internal void ClearGroups()
		{
			groupInfoManager.ClearGroups();
		}

		internal void SetEventHandlerInternal(IControlEventHandler handler)
		{
			eventHandler = handler ?? new ControlEventHandler(this);
		}

		public void SetHatch(HierarchicalChartHatchBrush selectedItemHatch, HierarchicalChartHatchBrush highlightedItemHatch)
		{
			this.selectedItemHatch = selectedItemHatch;
			this.highlightedItemHatch = highlightedItemHatch;
		}

		public string CalculateToolTipText<T>(T item, string pattern) where T : IHierarchicalItem
		{
			PatternParser patternParser = new PatternParser(pattern, this);
			patternParser.SetContext(item);
			return patternParser.GetText();
		}

		public abstract bool ShouldSerializeColorizer();

		public abstract void ResetColorizer();

		public void UpdateAppearance()
		{
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Appearance);
		}

		public void UpdateHighlighting()
		{
			ProcessChanged(UpdateType.NonSpecific);
		}

		public void OnMouseMove(PortableMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			interactionController.OnMouseMove(e);
		}

		public void OnMouseClick(PortableMouseEventArgs e, PortableKeys modifierKeys)
		{
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			interactionController.OnMouseClick(e, modifierKeys);
		}

		public void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e)
		{
			interactionController.OnMouseLeave(e);
		}

		public void DrawContent(Graphics graphics, Rectangle bounds, bool drawToMetaFile)
		{
			DrawContent(new GdiPlusGraphics(graphics), bounds, drawToMetaFile);
		}

		public void DrawContent(IDXGraphics graphics, Rectangle bounds, bool drawToMetaFile)
		{
			DrawContentCore(graphics, bounds, drawToMetaFile);
		}

		public void OnStyleChanged()
		{
			UpdateAppearance();
		}

		public void SaveLayout(Stream stream)
		{
			XtraSerializingHelper.SaveLayoutToStream(this, stream, ControlName);
		}

		public void LoadLayout(Stream stream)
		{
			HierarchicalElement hierarchicalElement = CreateObjectForCloneCore();
			try
			{
				XtraSerializingHelper.LoadLayoutFromStream((InnerHierarchicalControlBase)hierarchicalElement, stream, ControlName);
			}
			catch (XmlException)
			{
				throw new Exception("The specified XML file can't be opened,\nbecause it is either not a supported file type,\nor because the file has been damaged.");
			}
			container.Changing();
			AssignCore(hierarchicalElement);
			container.Changed();
		}

		public void SetClientSize(Size size)
		{
			clientSize = size;
		}

		protected override void AssignCore(HierarchicalElement obj)
		{
			base.AssignCore(obj);
			if (obj is InnerHierarchicalControlBase innerHierarchicalControlBase)
			{
				borderOptions.Assign(innerHierarchicalControlBase.borderOptions);
				showToolTips = innerHierarchicalControlBase.ShowToolTips;
				toolTipLeafPattern = innerHierarchicalControlBase.ToolTipLeafPattern;
				LeafTextPattern = innerHierarchicalControlBase.LeafTextPattern;
				backColor = innerHierarchicalControlBase.backColor;
				padding = innerHierarchicalControlBase.padding;
				Legends.Assign(innerHierarchicalControlBase.Legends);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateDpiProvider(ITreeMapDpiProvider dpiProvider)
		{
			this.dpiProvider = dpiProvider ?? EmptyTreeMapDpiProvider.Instance;
			TextMeasurer.RecreateDefault(this.dpiProvider);
		}
	}
	public class EmptyTreeMapContainer : ITreeMapContainer

		}

		public void Changing()
		{
		}

		public void Invalidate()
		{
		}

		public ComponentExporter CreateComponentExporter()
		{
			return null;
		}

		public IDefaultStyleProvider GetStyleProvider()

			return new TreeMapItemStyleProvider();
		}
	}
	public interface IControlEventHandler
	{
		void OnTitlesChanged(CollectionChangedEventArgs<Title> args);

		void OnLegendsChanged(CollectionChangedEventArgs<LegendBase> e);
	}
	public class ControlEventHandler : IControlEventHandler
	{
		private InnerHierarchicalControlBase HierarchicalControl { get; }

		public ControlEventHandler(InnerHierarchicalControlBase control)
		{
			Guard.ArgumentNotNull((object)control, "control");
			HierarchicalControl = control;
		}

		void IControlEventHandler.OnTitlesChanged(CollectionChangedEventArgs<Title> args)
		{
			InvalidateLayout();
		}

		void IControlEventHandler.OnLegendsChanged(CollectionChangedEventArgs<LegendBase> e)
		{
			InvalidateLayout();
		}

		private void InvalidateLayout()
		{
			HierarchicalControl.ViewController.InvalidateLayout();
		}
	}
	[SerializationContext(typeof(TreeMapSerializationContext))]
	public class InnerSunburst : InnerHierarchicalControlBase, ISunburstAppearanceProvider, IAppearanceProvider, IMaxVisibleLevelProvider
	{
		private const string controlName = "Sunburst";

		private const int DefaultHoleRadiusPercent = 20;

		private const string HoleRadiusPercentErrMsg = "The hole percentage should be greater than or equal to 0 and less than or equal to 100.";

		private TreeMapPadding defaultPadding = new TreeMapPadding(2);

		private readonly SunburstColorizerController colorizerController;

		private readonly SunburstCenterLabel centerLabel;

		private readonly SunburstLabel label;

		private readonly SunburstDefaultStyleProvider styleProvider;

		private readonly SunburstItemStyle style;

		private SunburstHighlightMode highlightMode = SunburstHighlightMode.PathFromRoot;

		private SunburstSweepDirection sweepDirection;

		private double startAngle;

		private int holeRadiusPercent = 20;

		protected override string ControlName => "Sunburst";

		protected internal override IColorizerController ColorizerControllerCore => ColorizerController;

		protected internal new ISunBurstView View => (ISunBurstView)base.View;

		public override IGroupIndexProvider GroupIndexProvider => DataAdapter as IGroupIndexProvider;

		public SunburstColorizerController ColorizerController => colorizerController;

		public new SunburstDataController DataController => (SunburstDataController)base.DataController;

		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public ISunburstDataAdapter DataAdapter
		{
			get
			{
				return DataController.DataAdapter;
			}
			set
			{
				DataController.DataAdapter = value;
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public bool ShowCenterLabel
		{
			get
			{
				return centerLabel.Visible;
			}
			set
			{
				centerLabel.Visible = value;
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public SunburstHighlightMode HighlightMode
		{
			get
			{
				return highlightMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (highlightMode != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					highlightMode = value;
					UpdateInteractionController();
				}
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public ISunburstColorizer Colorizer
		{
			get
			{
				return colorizerController.Colorizer;
			}
			set
			{
				colorizerController.Colorizer = value;
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public SunburstSweepDirection SweepDirection
		{
			get
			{
				return sweepDirection;
			}
			set
			{
				if (sweepDirection != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					sweepDirection = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public double StartAngle
		{
			get
			{
				return startAngle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (startAngle != value)
				{
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					startAngle = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public int HoleRadiusPercent
		{
			get
			{
				return holeRadiusPercent;
			}
			set
			{
				if (holeRadiusPercent != value)
				{
					if (value < 0 || value > 100)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("The hole percentage should be greater than or equal to 0 and less than or equal to 100.");
					}
					ApplyPropertyChanging();
					holeRadiusPercent = value;
					ProcessChanged(UpdateType.Layout);
				}
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public SunburstCenterLabel CenterLabel => centerLabel;

		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public SunburstItemStyle ItemStyle => style;

		[XtraSerializableProperty]
		public override string LeafTextPattern
		{
			get
			{
				return Label.TextPattern;
			}
			set
			{
				Label.TextPattern = value;
			}
		}

		[XtraSerializableProperty(/*Could not decode attribute arguments.*/)]
		public SunburstLabel Label => label;

		ICenterLabelAppearanceProvider ISunburstAppearanceProvider.CenterLabel => centerLabel;

		ILabelAppearanceProvider ISunburstAppearanceProvider.Label => label;

		SunburstDefaultStyleProvider ISunburstAppearanceProvider.DefaultStyleProvider => styleProvider;

		public InnerSunburst()
			: this(new EmptyTreeMapContainer())
		{
		}

		public InnerSunburst(ITreeMapContainer container, bool isSpreadsheetContainer = false)
			: base(container, isSpreadsheetContainer)
		{
			centerLabel = new SunburstCenterLabel(this);
			label = new SunburstLabel(this);
			style = ElementHelper.CreateTreeMapElement<SunburstItemStyle>(this);
			colorizerController = new SunburstColorizerController(this, CreateColorizerControllerBehavior());
			styleProvider = new SunburstDefaultStyleProvider();
			base.Padding = defaultPadding;
		}

		private void AssignDataAdapter(InnerSunburst sunburst)
		{
			if (sunburst.DataAdapter != null)
			{
				Type type = ((DataAdapter == null) ? null : DataAdapter.GetType());
				Type type2 = sunburst.DataAdapter.GetType();
				if (type != type2)
				{
					DataAdapter = (SunburstDataAdapterBase)Activator.CreateInstance(type2);
				}
				((SunburstElement)DataAdapter).Assign(sunburst.DataAdapter as SunburstElement);
			}
		}

		private void AssignColorizer(InnerSunburst sunburst)
		{
			if (sunburst.Colorizer != null)
			{
				Type type = ((Colorizer == null) ? null : Colorizer.GetType());
				Type type2 = sunburst.Colorizer.GetType();
				if (type != type2)
				{
					Colorizer = (ISunburstColorizer)Activator.CreateInstance(type2);
				}
				((SunburstElement)Colorizer).Assign(sunburst.Colorizer as SunburstElement);
			}
		}

		protected override bool NeedToCreateContentPropertyValue(XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!base.NeedToCreateContentPropertyValue(e) && !(e.Item.Name == "DataAdapter"))
			{
				return e.Item.Name == "Colorizer";
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override IHierarchicalView CreateView(IRenderer renderer)
		{
			return new SunburstView(renderer, this);
		}

		protected override DataController CreateDataController()
		{
			return new SunburstDataController(this);
		}

		protected override HitTestController CreateHitTestController()
		{
			return new SunburstHitTestController(this);
		}

		protected override InteractionController CreateInteractionController()
		{
			return new InteractionController(this, highlightMode);
		}

		protected override InsideRectangularBorder CreateBorderOptions()
		{
			return new InsideRectangularBorder(visible: false, TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultTreeMapBorderColor, this);
		}

		protected override SelectionController CreateSelectionController()
		{
			return new SunburstSelectionController(this);
		}

		protected override HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore()
		{
			return new InnerSunburst();
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeColorizer()
		{
			return colorizerController.ShouldSerializeColorizer();
		}

		public override void ResetColorizer()
		{
			colorizerController.ResetColorizer();
		}

		protected override void AfterDeserializeCore(XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Item.Name == "DataAdapter")
			{
				DataAdapter = e.Item.Value as ISunburstDataAdapter;
			}
			else if (e.Item.Name == "Colorizer")
			{
				Colorizer = e.Item.Value as ISunburstColorizer;
			}
		}

		protected override void AssignCore(HierarchicalElement obj)
		{
			base.AssignCore(obj);
			if (obj is InnerSunburst innerSunburst)
			{
				AssignDataAdapter(innerSunburst);
				AssignColorizer(innerSunburst);
				startAngle = innerSunburst.startAngle;
				sweepDirection = innerSunburst.sweepDirection;
				highlightMode = innerSunburst.highlightMode;
				holeRadiusPercent = innerSunburst.holeRadiusPercent;
				base.SelectionMode = innerSunburst.SelectionMode;
				label.Assign(innerSunburst.label);
				centerLabel.Assign(innerSunburst.centerLabel);
			}
		}

		public List<ISunburstItem> GetItemPath(ISunburstItem item)
		{
			return TreeMapItemHelper.GetItemPath(item, DataAdapter.Items);
		}

		public SunburstHitInfo CalcHitInfo(Point hitPoint)
		{
			return new SunburstHitInfo(base.HitTestController.HitTest(hitPoint));
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeItemStyle()
		{
			return ItemStyle.ShouldSerialize();
		}
	}

		protected override void AssignCore(HierarchicalElement obj)
		{
			//IL_0039: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			base.AssignCore(obj);
			if (obj is InnerTreeMap innerTreeMap)
			{
				appearance.Assign(innerTreeMap.Appearance);
				AssignLayoutAlgorithm(innerTreeMap);
				AssignDataAdapter(innerTreeMap);
				AssignColorizer(innerTreeMap);
				leafBorderVisible = innerTreeMap.LeafBorderVisible;
				groupBorderVisible = innerTreeMap.GroupBorderVisible;
				groupHeaderTextPattern = innerTreeMap.GroupTextPattern;
				toolTipGroupPattern = innerTreeMap.ToolTipGroupPattern;
				EnableHighlighting = innerTreeMap.EnableHighlighting;
				base.SelectionMode = innerTreeMap.SelectionMode;
			}
		}
