		public Rectangle GetItemBounds(IEnumerable<TreeMapItemViewData> items, ITreeMapItem item)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData item2 in items)
			{
				if (item2.Item == item)
				{
					return item2.RoundedBounds;
				}
				if (item2.IsGroup)
				{
					Rectangle itemBounds = GetItemBounds(item2.Children, item);
					if (!itemBounds.IsEmpty)
					{
						return itemBounds;
					}
				}
			}
			return Rectangle.Empty;
		}

		public Rectangle GetItemBounds(ITreeMapItem item)
		{
			return GetItemBounds(View.Items, item);
		}
	}
	public class GroupInfoManager
	{
		private readonly ISupportSelectionContainer container;

		private readonly List<GroupInfo> groupInfos = new List<GroupInfo>();

		private readonly List<List<object>> groupValues = new List<List<object>>();

		public IEnumerable<GroupInfo> Groups
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureGroups();
				return groupInfos;
			}
		}

		public GroupInfoManager(ISupportSelectionContainer container)
		{
			this.container = container;
		}

		private void EnsureGroups()
		{
			if (groupInfos.Count == 0)
			{
				FillGroupsInfos(container.ActualItems, groupInfos, new List<IEnumerable<object>>(), null, 0);
			}
		}

		private void FillGroupsInfos(IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> items, IList<GroupInfo> groupsInfos, List<IEnumerable<object>> listChildren, GroupInfo parent, int level)
		{
			groupValues.Add(new List<object>());
			foreach (IInteractiveElement item in items)
			{
				if (item.Children.Count() > 0)
				{
					object groupValue = item.GroupValue;
					GroupInfo groupInfo = new GroupInfo(item, groupValue, parent);
					groupValues[level].Add(groupValue);
					groupsInfos.Add(groupInfo);
					listChildren.Add(groupInfo.SourceItems);
					FillGroupsInfos(item.Children, (IList<GroupInfo>)groupInfo.ChildGroups, listChildren, groupInfo, level + 1);
					continue;
				}
				foreach (List<object> listChild in listChildren)
				{
					listChild.Add(item.Source);
				}
			}
			if (listChildren.Count > 0)
			{
				listChildren.RemoveAt(listChildren.Count - 1);
			}
		}

		private GroupInfo FindGroupByPath(IList<object> groupValues)
		{
			IEnumerable enumerable = VerifyAndSortByGroupLevel(groupValues);
			GroupInfo groupInfo = null;
			IEnumerable<GroupInfo> childGroups = groupInfos;
			foreach (object item in enumerable)
			{
				groupInfo = FilterGroupInfos(childGroups, item);
				if (groupInfo == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				childGroups = groupInfo.ChildGroups;
			}
			return groupInfo;
		}

		private GroupInfo FilterGroupInfos(IEnumerable<GroupInfo> groupInfos, object value)
		{
			foreach (GroupInfo groupInfo in groupInfos)
			{
				if (groupInfo.GroupValue.Equals(value))
				{
					return groupInfo;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private IEnumerable VerifyAndSortByGroupLevel(IList<object> groupValues)
		{
			SortedList<int, object> sortedList = new SortedList<int, object>();
			foreach (object groupValue in groupValues)
			{
				int groupLevel = GetGroupLevel(groupValue);
				if (groupLevel >= 0)
				{
					sortedList.Add(groupLevel, groupValue);
				}
			}
			return sortedList.Values;
		}

		private int GetGroupLevel(object groupValue)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < groupValues.Count; i++)
			{
				if (groupValues[i].Contains(groupValue))
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		private GroupInfo FindGroupByItem(IInteractiveElement item, IEnumerable<GroupInfo> groups)
		{
			foreach (GroupInfo group in groups)
			{
				if (group.Item == item)
				{
					return group;
				}
				if (group.ChildGroups.Count() > 0)
				{
					GroupInfo groupInfo = FindGroupByItem(item, group.ChildGroups);
					if (groupInfo != null)
					{
						return groupInfo;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void ClearGroups()
		{
			groupInfos.Clear();
			groupValues.Clear();
		}

		public IEnumerable<object> GetGroupChildren(IList<object> groupValues)
		{
			if (groupValues.Count == 0)
			{
				return new List<object>();
			}
			EnsureGroups();
			GroupInfo groupInfo = FindGroupByPath(groupValues);
			if (groupInfo == null)
			{
				return new List<object>();
			}
			return groupInfo.SourceItems;
		}

		public void FillSelectedGroup(SelectedGroupCollection selectedGroups, IInteractiveElement item)
		{
			EnsureGroups();
			selectedGroups.AddInternal(FindGroupByItem(item, groupInfos).GroupPath);
		}
	}
	public abstract class HitTestController
	{
		private IHitTestableElementsContainer container;

		public HitTestController(IHitTestableElementsContainer container)
		{
			this.container = container;
		}

		protected abstract IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestCore(Point point, IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> hitTestableElements);

		public IHitTestableElement[] HitTest(Point point)
		{
			return HitTestCore(point, container.HitTestableElements).ToArray();
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapHitTestController : HitTestController
	{
		public TreeMapHitTestController(IHitTestableElementsContainer container)
			: base(container)
		{
		}

		private List<IHitTestableElement> HitTestTreeMap(Point point, IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> hitTestableElements)
		{
			List<IHitTestableElement> list = new List<IHitTestableElement>();
			foreach (IHitTestableElement hitTestableElement in hitTestableElements)
			{
				if (hitTestableElement.HitTest(point, out var _))
				{
					if (hitTestableElement.IsHitTestVisible)
					{
						list.Add(hitTestableElement);
					}
					List<IHitTestableElement> collection = HitTestTreeMap(point, hitTestableElement.Children);
					list.AddRange(collection);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestCore(Point point, IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> hitTestableElements)
		{
			return HitTestTreeMap(point, hitTestableElements);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstHitTestController : HitTestController
	{
		public SunburstHitTestController(IHitTestableElementsContainer container)
			: base(container)
		{
		}

		private IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestSunburst(Point hitPoint, IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> elements, List<IHitTestableElement> result)
		{
			foreach (IHitTestableElement element in elements)
			{
				if (element.HitTest(hitPoint, out var isInElementRange))
				{
					return new IHitTestableElement[1] { element };
				}
				if (!isInElementRange)
				{
					continue;
				}
				IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> source = HitTestSunburst(hitPoint, element.Children, result);
				if (source.Count() > 0)
				{
					if (result.Count == 0)
					{
						result.Add(source.First());
					}
					result.Insert(0, element);
				}
				return result;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestCore(Point point, IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> hitTestableElements)
		{
			List<IHitTestableElement> result = new List<IHitTestableElement>();
			return HitTestSunburst(point, hitTestableElements, result);
		}
	}
	public class InteractionController
	{
		private readonly InnerHierarchicalControlBase treeMap;

		private readonly HighlightElementsExtractor highlightElementsExtractor;

		private readonly bool enableHighlighting = true;

		private readonly List<IInteractiveElement> highlightedElements = new List<IInteractiveElement>();

		private InteractionController(InnerHierarchicalControlBase treeMap)
		{
			this.treeMap = treeMap;
		}

		public InteractionController(InnerHierarchicalControlBase treeMap, SunburstHighlightMode highlightingMode)
			: this(treeMap)
		{
			enableHighlighting = highlightingMode != SunburstHighlightMode.None;
			highlightElementsExtractor = new SunBurstHighlightElementsExtractor(highlightingMode);
		}

		public InteractionController(InnerHierarchicalControlBase treeMap, bool enableHighlighting)
			: this(treeMap)
		{
			this.enableHighlighting = enableHighlighting;
			highlightElementsExtractor = new HighlightElementsExtractor();
		}

		protected internal void UpdateHighlightedElement(IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> items)
		{
			List<IInteractiveElement> list = new List<IInteractiveElement>(highlightedElements);
			if (items != null)
			{
				foreach (IHitTestableElement item in items)
				{
					if (item is IInteractiveElement interactiveElement)
					{
						if (highlightedElements.Contains(interactiveElement))
						{
							list.Remove(interactiveElement);
							continue;
						}
						interactiveElement.IsHighlighted = true;
						highlightedElements.Add(interactiveElement);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (IInteractiveElement item2 in list)
			{
				item2.IsHighlighted = false;
				highlightedElements.Remove(item2);
			}
			treeMap.UpdateHighlighting();
		}

		public void OnMouseLeave(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (enableHighlighting)
			{
				UpdateHighlightedElement(null);
			}
		}

		public void OnMouseMove(PortableMouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (enableHighlighting)
			{
				IHitTestableElement[] elements = treeMap.HitTestController.HitTest(e.Location);
				UpdateHighlightedElement(highlightElementsExtractor.GetElementsForHighlight(elements));
			}
		}

		public void OnMouseClick(PortableMouseEventArgs e, PortableKeys modifierKeys)
		{
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> source = treeMap.HitTestController.HitTest(e.Location);
			IInteractiveElement selectedItem = source.LastOrDefault() as IInteractiveElement;
			treeMap.SelectionController.UpdateSelection(selectedItem, modifierKeys);
		}
	}
	public class HighlightElementsExtractor
	{
		private void FillAllChildren(IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> elements, List<IHitTestableElement> result)
		{
			foreach (IHitTestableElement element in elements)
			{
				result.Add(element);
				if (element.Children.Any())
				{
					FillAllChildren(element.Children, result);
				}
			}
		}

		protected IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> GetHighlightingElementWithChildren(IHitTestableElement element)
		{
			List<IHitTestableElement> list = new List<IHitTestableElement>();
			list.Add(element);
			FillAllChildren(element.Children, list);
			return list;
		}

		protected virtual IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> GetElementsForHighlightCore(IHitTestableElement[] elements)
		{
			return GetHighlightingElementWithChildren(elements[elements.Length - 1]);
		}

		public IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> GetElementsForHighlight(IHitTestableElement[] elements)
		{
			if (elements.Length != 0)
			{
				return GetElementsForHighlightCore(elements);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class SunBurstHighlightElementsExtractor : HighlightElementsExtractor
	{
		private readonly SunburstHighlightMode highlightingMode;

		public SunBurstHighlightElementsExtractor(SunburstHighlightMode highlightingMode)
		{
			this.highlightingMode = highlightingMode;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> GetElementsForHighlightCore(IHitTestableElement[] elements)
		{
			return highlightingMode switch
			{
				SunburstHighlightMode.PathFromRoot => elements, 
				SunburstHighlightMode.WholeGroup => GetHighlightingElementWithChildren(elements[elements.Length - 1]), 
				SunburstHighlightMode.Single => new List<IHitTestableElement> { elements[elements.Length - 1] }, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}
	}
	public class SelectionController
	{
		protected enum SelectionAction
		{
			None,
			Clear,
			Add,
			Remove,
			ReplaceAll
		}

		private readonly ISupportSelectionContainer container;

		private readonly SelectedGroupCollection selectedGroups;

		private readonly SelectedItemsCollection selectedItems;

		private readonly GroupInfoManager groupInfoManager;

		private ElementSelectionMode selectionMode;

		protected IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> ActualItems => container.ActualItems;

		public ElementSelectionMode SelectionMode
		{
			get
			{
				return selectionMode;
			}
			set
			{
				selectionMode = value;
			}
		}

		public SelectedItemsCollection SelectedItems => selectedItems;

		public SelectedGroupCollection SelectedGroups => selectedGroups;

		public SelectionController(ISupportSelectionContainer container)
		{
			this.container = container;
			groupInfoManager = container.GroupInfoManager;
			selectedItems = new SelectedItemsCollection();
			((NotificationCollection<object>)selectedItems).CollectionChanged += OnSelectedItemsChanged;
			selectedGroups = new SelectedGroupCollection(this);
		}

		private void OnSelectedItemsChanged(object sender, CollectionChangedEventArgs<object> e)
		{
			UpdateActualItemsSelection(needUpdateSelectedGroups: true);
		}

		private void SynchronizeSelectedItems(CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>> e)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			CollectionChangedAction action = e.Action;
			switch ((int)action)
			{
			case 1:
				selectedItems.RemoveRangeInternal(groupInfoManager.GetGroupChildren(e.Element));
				break;
			case 0:
				selectedItems.AddRangeInternal(groupInfoManager.GetGroupChildren(e.Element));
				break;
			case 3:
				selectedItems.ClearInternal();
				break;
			case 2:
				break;
			}
		}

		private SelectionAction CalculateSelectionAction(ElementSelectionMode selectionMode, PortableKeys keyModifiers, bool isItemSelected, IInteractiveElement selectedItem)
		{
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0032: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0042: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			switch (selectionMode)
			{
			case ElementSelectionMode.Single:
				return CalculateActionOnSingleModeSelection(selectedItem);
			case ElementSelectionMode.Multiple:
				if (!isItemSelected)
				{
					return SelectionAction.Add;
				}
				return SelectionAction.Remove;
			case ElementSelectionMode.Extended:
				if ((keyModifiers & 0x10000) == 65536)
				{
					return SelectionAction.Add;
				}
				if ((keyModifiers & 0x20000) == 131072)
				{
					if (!isItemSelected)
					{
						return SelectionAction.Add;
					}
					return SelectionAction.Remove;
				}
				return SelectionAction.ReplaceAll;
			default:
				return SelectionAction.None;
			}
		}

		private List<object> FillSelectedTreeMapItems(IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> children)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			foreach (IInteractiveElement child in children)
			{
				list.AddRange(GetSelectedItems(child));
			}
			return list;
		}

		private List<object> GetSelectedItems(IInteractiveElement item)
		{
			List<object> list = new List<object>();
			if (item != null)
			{
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					list.AddRange(FillSelectedTreeMapItems(item.Children));
				}
				else
				{
					object source = item.Source;
					if (source != null)
					{
						list.Add(item.Source);
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private bool IsAllItemsSelected(List<object> items)
		{
			if (items.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (object item in items)
				{
					if (!((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)selectedItems).Contains(item))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void UpdateActualItemsSelection(bool needUpdateSelectedGroups)
		{
			UpdateItemsSelection(container.ActualItems);
			if (needUpdateSelectedGroups)
			{
				selectedGroups.ClearInternal();
				foreach (IInteractiveElement selectedGroupItem in GetSelectedGroupItems(container.ActualItems))
				{
					groupInfoManager.FillSelectedGroup(selectedGroups, selectedGroupItem);
				}
			}
			container.RaiseSelectionChanged();
		}

		private List<IInteractiveElement> GetSelectedGroupItems(IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> items)
		{
			List<IInteractiveElement> list = new List<IInteractiveElement>();
			FillSelectedGroups(items, list);
			return list;
		}

		private void FillSelectedGroups(IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> items, List<IInteractiveElement> target)
		{
			foreach (IInteractiveElement item in items)
			{
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					if (item.IsSelected)
					{
						target.Add(item);
					}
					else
					{
						FillSelectedGroups(item.Children, target);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateItemsSelection(IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> itemCollection)
		{
			if (itemCollection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IInteractiveElement item in itemCollection)
			{
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					UpdateItemsSelection(item.Children);
					continue;
				}
				object source = item.Source;
				item.IsSelected = source != null && ((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)selectedItems).Contains(source);
			}
		}

		private bool IsSelectionChanged(IList<object> newSelectedItems)
		{
			if (((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)selectedItems).Count != newSelectedItems.Count)
			{
				return true;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < ((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)selectedItems).Count; i++)
			{
				if (((DXCollection<object>)(object)selectedItems)[i] != newSelectedItems[i])
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void OnSelectedGroupChanged(CollectionChangedEventArgs<IList<object>> e)
		{
			SynchronizeSelectedItems(e);
			UpdateActualItemsSelection(needUpdateSelectedGroups: false);
		}

		protected virtual SelectionAction CalculateActionOnSingleModeSelection(IInteractiveElement selectedItem)
		{
			if (selectedItem == null || selectedItem.IsGroup)
			{
				return SelectionAction.None;
			}
			if (!selectedItem.IsSelected)
			{
				return SelectionAction.ReplaceAll;
			}
			return SelectionAction.Remove;
		}

		public void UpdateSelection(IInteractiveElement selectedItem, PortableKeys keyModifiers)
		{
			//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			List<object> list = GetSelectedItems(selectedItem);
			bool isItemSelected = IsAllItemsSelected(list);
			SelectionAction selectionAction = ((selectedItem == null) ? SelectionAction.Clear : CalculateSelectionAction(SelectionMode, keyModifiers, isItemSelected, selectedItem));
			bool flag = false;
			switch (selectionAction)
			{
			case SelectionAction.ReplaceAll:
			{
				bool flag2 = IsSelectionChanged(list);
				selectedItems.ClearInternal();
				if (flag2)
				{
					selectedItems.AddRangeInternal(list);
				}
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
			case SelectionAction.Add:
				foreach (object item in list)
				{
					if (!((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)selectedItems).Contains(item))
					{
						selectedItems.AddInternal(item);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				break;
			case SelectionAction.Remove:
				foreach (object item2 in list)
				{
					selectedItems.RemoveInternal(item2);
				}
				flag = true;
				break;
			case SelectionAction.Clear:
				selectedItems.ClearInternal();
				flag = true;
				break;
			case SelectionAction.None:
				return;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				UpdateActualItemsSelection(needUpdateSelectedGroups: true);
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstSelectionController : SelectionController
	{
		public SunburstSelectionController(ISupportSelectionContainer container)
			: base(container)
		{
		}

		protected override SelectionAction CalculateActionOnSingleModeSelection(IInteractiveElement selectedItem)
		{
			if (selectedItem != null)
			{
				return SelectionAction.ReplaceAll;
			}
			return SelectionAction.None;
		}
	}
	public class SelectedItemsCollection : NotificationCollection<object>
	{
		internal void AddInternal(object item)
		{
			if (!((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)this).InnerList.Contains(item))
			{
				((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)this).InnerList.Add(item);
			}
		}

		internal void AddRangeInternal(IEnumerable<object> collection)
		{
			foreach (object item in collection)
			{
				AddInternal(item);
			}
		}

		internal void RemoveInternal(object item)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)this).InnerList.Remove(item);
		}

		internal void RemoveRangeInternal(IEnumerable<object> collection)
		{
			foreach (object item in collection)
			{
				RemoveInternal(item);
			}
		}

		internal void ClearInternal()
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<object>)(object)this).InnerList.Clear();
		}
	}

	public static class TreeMapLayoutHelper
	{
		public static Rectangle RoundChildLayoutInternal(TreeMapRect childLayout, RectangleF bounds)
		{
			bool flag = Math.Round(childLayout.X - Math.Floor(childLayout.X), 3) >= 1.0;
			bool flag2 = Math.Round(childLayout.Y - Math.Floor(childLayout.Y), 3) >= 1.0;
			double num = (flag ? Math.Ceiling(childLayout.X) : Math.Floor(childLayout.X));
			double num2 = (flag2 ? Math.Ceiling(childLayout.Y) : Math.Floor(childLayout.Y));
			double value = childLayout.X + childLayout.Width - Math.Floor(childLayout.Width) - num;
			double value2 = childLayout.Y + childLayout.Height - Math.Floor(childLayout.Height) - num2;
			double num3 = ((Math.Round(value, 3) >= 1.0) ? (Math.Floor(childLayout.Width + 1.0) + 1.0) : (Math.Floor(childLayout.Width) + 1.0));
			double num4 = ((Math.Round(value2, 3) >= 1.0) ? (Math.Floor(childLayout.Height + 1.0) + 1.0) : (Math.Floor(childLayout.Height) + 1.0));
			num3 = ((num3 + num > (double)(bounds.X + bounds.Width)) ? (num3 - 1.0) : num3);
			num4 = ((num4 + num2 > (double)(bounds.Y + bounds.Height)) ? (num4 - 1.0) : num4);
			return new Rectangle((int)num, (int)num2, (int)num3, (int)num4);
		}
	}
	public class WinLegendItemCreator : LegendItemCreator
	{
		private static WinLegendItemCreator instance;

		public static WinLegendItemCreator Instance
		{
			get
			{
				if (instance == null)
				{
					instance = new WinLegendItemCreator();
				}
				return instance;
			}
		}

		private WinLegendItemCreator()
		{
		}

		public static Color[] CreateLegendItemBrushSource(ILegendSourceItem item, Color gradientColor)
		{
			if (gradientColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				return new Color[1] { (Color)item.Brush };
			}
			return new Color[2]
			{
				(Color)item.Brush,
				gradientColor
			};
		}

		public override ILegendItem CreateItem(string text, object brushSource, object source)
		{
			return new LegendItem
			{
				Text = text,
				Colors = (Color[])brushSource
			};
		}
	}
	public struct OverlayPositionKey : IEquatable<OverlayPositionKey>
	{
		public LegendHorizontalAlignment Horizontal { get; }

		public LegendVerticalAlignment Vertical { get; }

		public OverlayPositionKey(LegendHorizontalAlignment horizontal, LegendVerticalAlignment vertical)
		{
			Horizontal = horizontal;
			Vertical = vertical;
		}

		public bool Equals(OverlayPositionKey legendPosition)
		{
			if (Horizontal == legendPosition.Horizontal)
			{
				return Vertical == legendPosition.Vertical;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is OverlayPositionKey)
			{
				return Equals((OverlayPositionKey)obj);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return HashCodeHelper.CalculateGeneric<int, int>((int)Horizontal, (int)Vertical);
		}
	}
	public static class LegendViewDataCalculator
	{
		public static Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle> CalculateViewData(Dictionary<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>> legendsData, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			List<ViewDataBase> list = new List<ViewDataBase>();
			if (legendsData != null && legendsData.Count < 1)
			{
				return new Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle>(list, bounds);
			}
			Rectangle item = CalculateLegendsViewData(legendsData, list, appearanceProvider, bounds);
			return new Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle>(list, item);
		}

		private static Rectangle ObtainAvailableBounds(Dictionary<OverlayPositionKey, Size> sizeByPosition, OverlayPositionKey position, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			if (!sizeByPosition.TryGetValue(position, out var value))
			{
				sizeByPosition.Add(position, Size.Empty);
			}
			else
			{
				bounds.Height -= value.Height;
				if (position.Horizontal == LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside)
				{
					bounds.Width -= value.Width;
				}
			}
			return bounds;
		}

		private static Rectangle UpdateBoundsCorrection(Dictionary<OverlayPositionKey, Size> sizeByPosition, OverlayPositionKey position, Size size, Rectangle boundsCorrection)
		{
			Size size2 = sizeByPosition[position];
			Size size3 = (sizeByPosition[position] = new Size(Math.Max(size2.Width, size.Width), size2.Height + size.Height));
			if (position.Vertical == LegendVerticalAlignment.TopOutside)
			{
				boundsCorrection.Y = Math.Max(boundsCorrection.Y, size3.Height);
			}
			else if (position.Vertical == LegendVerticalAlignment.BottomOutside)
			{
				boundsCorrection.Height = Math.Max(boundsCorrection.Height, size3.Height);
			}
			if (position.Horizontal == LegendHorizontalAlignment.LeftOutside)
			{
				boundsCorrection.X = Math.Max(boundsCorrection.X, size3.Width);
			}
			else if (position.Horizontal == LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside)
			{
				boundsCorrection.Width = Math.Max(boundsCorrection.Width, size3.Width);
			}
			return boundsCorrection;
		}

		private static Rectangle CalculateLegendsViewData(Dictionary<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>> legendsData, List<ViewDataBase> legendViewDataList, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Rectangle diagramBounds)
		{
			Rectangle boundsCorrection = default(Rectangle);
			Dictionary<OverlayPositionKey, Size> sizeByPosition = new Dictionary<OverlayPositionKey, Size>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>> legendsDatum in legendsData)
			{
				OverlayPositionKey position = new OverlayPositionKey(legendsDatum.Key.HorizontalAlignment, legendsDatum.Key.VerticalAlignment);
				Rectangle bounds = ObtainAvailableBounds(sizeByPosition, position, diagramBounds);
				LegendViewDataBase legendViewDataBase = legendsDatum.Key.CreateViewData(legendsDatum.Value, appearanceProvider, bounds);
				if (legendViewDataBase != null)
				{
					boundsCorrection = UpdateBoundsCorrection(sizeByPosition, position, legendViewDataBase.OuterSize, boundsCorrection);
					legendViewDataList.Add(legendViewDataBase);
				}
			}
			diagramBounds = new Rectangle(diagramBounds.X + boundsCorrection.X, diagramBounds.Y + boundsCorrection.Y, diagramBounds.Width - boundsCorrection.X - boundsCorrection.Width, diagramBounds.Height - boundsCorrection.Y - boundsCorrection.Height);
			Dictionary<OverlayPositionKey, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<OverlayPositionKey, int>();
			foreach (LegendViewDataBase legendViewData in legendViewDataList)
			{
				OverlayPositionKey key = new OverlayPositionKey(legendViewData.Legend.HorizontalAlignment, legendViewData.Legend.VerticalAlignment);
				if (!dictionary.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
				{
					value = (dictionary[key] = 0);
				}
				legendViewData.UpdateLocationAfterBoundsCorrection(diagramBounds, value, boundsCorrection.Height);
				dictionary[key] += legendViewData.OuterSize.Height;
			}
			return diagramBounds;
		}
	}
	public abstract class LegendItemGroupingAlgorithm
	{
		private readonly LegendBase legend;

		private readonly IList<LegendItemViewData> items;

		private readonly Size maxSize;

		private readonly TreeMapPadding padding;

		protected LegendBase Legend => legend;

		protected IList<LegendItemViewData> Items => items;

		protected Size MaxSize => maxSize;

		protected TreeMapPadding Padding => padding;

		protected abstract int Indent { get; }

		private static LegendItemGroupingAlgorithm CreateInstance(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> items, Size maxSize)
		{
			switch (legend.Direction)
			{
			case LegendDirection.TopToBottom:
			case LegendDirection.BottomToTop:
				return new VerticalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm(legend, items, maxSize);
			case LegendDirection.LeftToRight:
			case LegendDirection.RightToLeft:
				if (legend.EquallySpacedItems)
				{
					return new HorizontalGroupingAlgorithmWithVerticalAlignment(legend, items, maxSize);
				}
				return new HorizontalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm(legend, items, maxSize);
			default:
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
		}

		public static LegendItemGroup[] Calculate(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> items, Size maxSize)
		{
			return CreateInstance(legend, items, maxSize).CalculateGroups();
		}

		public LegendItemGroupingAlgorithm(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> items, Size maxSize)
		{
			this.legend = legend;
			if (legend.Direction == LegendDirection.BottomToTop || legend.Direction == LegendDirection.RightToLeft)
			{
				items.Reverse();
			}
			this.items = items;
			this.maxSize = maxSize;
			padding = legend.Padding;
		}

		protected internal virtual LegendItemGroup[] CalculateGroups()
		{
			List<LegendItemGroup> list = new List<LegendItemGroup>();
			LegendItemGroup nextGroup;
			for (int i = 0; i < items.Count; i += nextGroup.Count)
			{
				nextGroup = GetNextGroup(i);
				list.Add(nextGroup);
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		protected virtual LegendItemGroup GetNextGroup(int index)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = GetCharacteristicLength(maxSize) - GetPadding();
			List<LegendItemViewData> list = new List<LegendItemViewData>();
			for (int i = index; i < Items.Count; i++)
			{
				num += GetCharacteristicLength(Items[i].Size);
				if (i > index && num > num2)
				{
					break;
				}
				num += Indent;
				list.Add(Items[i]);
			}
			return new LegendItemGroup(list.ToArray());
		}

		protected abstract int GetPadding();

		protected abstract int GetCharacteristicLength(Size size);
	}
	public class VerticalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm : LegendItemGroupingAlgorithm
	{
		protected override int Indent => base.Legend.VerticalIndent;

		public VerticalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> itemData, Size maxSize)
			: base(legend, itemData, maxSize)
		{
		}

		protected override int GetPadding()
		{
			return base.Padding.Vertical;
		}

		protected override int GetCharacteristicLength(Size size)
		{
			return size.Height;
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm : LegendItemGroupingAlgorithm
	{
		protected override int Indent => base.Legend.HorizontalIndent;

		public HorizontalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> itemData, Size maxSize)
			: base(legend, itemData, maxSize)
		{
		}

		protected override int GetPadding()
		{
			return base.Padding.Horizontal;
		}

		protected override int GetCharacteristicLength(Size size)
		{
			return size.Width;
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalGroupingAlgorithmWithVerticalAlignment : HorizontalLegendItemGroupingAlgorithm
	{
		private int actualDimension;

		public HorizontalGroupingAlgorithmWithVerticalAlignment(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> itemData, Size maxSize)
			: base(legend, itemData, maxSize)
		{
		}

		private bool IsLegendFitInLimits(LegendItemGroup[] groups)
		{
			if (groups.Length == 0 || groups[0].Count < 2)
			{
				return true;
			}
			int[] array = new int[groups[0].Count];
			foreach (LegendItemGroup legendItemGroup in groups)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < legendItemGroup.Count; j++)
				{
					array[j] = Math.Max(array[j], GetCharacteristicLength(legendItemGroup[j].Size));
				}
			}
			int num = 0;
			int[] array2 = array;
			foreach (int num2 in array2)
			{
				num += num2;
			}
			if (array.Length > 1)
			{
				num += (array.Length - 1) * base.Legend.HorizontalIndent;
			}
			return num <= GetCharacteristicLength(base.MaxSize) - GetPadding();
		}

		private int CalcInitialDimension()
		{
			if (base.Items.Count <= 1)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			int num = GetCharacteristicLength(base.MaxSize) - GetPadding();
			int num2 = GetCharacteristicLength(base.Items[0].Size) + Indent;
			for (int i = 1; i < base.Items.Count; i++)
			{
				num2 += GetCharacteristicLength(base.Items[i].Size);
				if (num2 > num)
				{
					return i;
				}
				num2 += Indent;
			}
			return base.Items.Count;
		}

		protected internal override LegendItemGroup[] CalculateGroups()
		{
			actualDimension = CalcInitialDimension();
			LegendItemGroup[] array = base.CalculateGroups();
			while (!IsLegendFitInLimits(array))
			{
				actualDimension--;
				array = base.CalculateGroups();
			}
			return array;
		}

		protected override LegendItemGroup GetNextGroup(int index)
		{
			int num = Math.Min(actualDimension, base.Items.Count - index);
			LegendItemViewData[] array = new LegendItemViewData[num];
			int num2 = 0;
			while (num2 < num)
			{
				array[num2] = base.Items[index];
				num2++;
				index++;
			}
			return new LegendItemGroup(array);
		}
	}
	public abstract class LegendSizeCalculator
	{
		private readonly LegendBase legend;

		private readonly LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups;

		private readonly TreeMapPadding padding;

		protected LegendBase Legend => legend;

		protected LegendItemGroup[] ItemGroups => itemGroups;

		protected TreeMapPadding Padding => padding;

		private static LegendSizeCalculator CreateInstance(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups)
		{
			switch (legend.Direction)
			{
			case LegendDirection.TopToBottom:
			case LegendDirection.BottomToTop:
				return new VerticalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator(legend, itemGroups);
			case LegendDirection.LeftToRight:
			case LegendDirection.RightToLeft:
				if (!legend.EquallySpacedItems)
				{
					return new HorizontalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator(legend, itemGroups);
				}
				return new HorizontalLayoutWithVerticalAlignmentLegendSizeCalculator(legend, itemGroups);
			default:
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
		}

		public static Size Calculate(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups)
		{
			LegendSizeCalculator legendSizeCalculator = CreateInstance(legend, itemGroups);
			return new Size(legendSizeCalculator.CalcWidth(), legendSizeCalculator.CalcHeight());
		}

		protected LegendSizeCalculator(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups)
		{
			this.legend = legend;
			this.itemGroups = itemGroups;
			padding = legend.Padding;
		}

		protected abstract int CalcWidth();

		protected abstract int CalcHeight();
	}
	public class VerticalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator : LegendSizeCalculator
	{
		internal VerticalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups)
			: base(legend, itemGroups)
		{
		}

		protected override int CalcWidth()
		{
			int num = 0;
			LegendItemGroup[] array = base.ItemGroups;
			foreach (LegendItemGroup legendItemGroup in array)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				foreach (LegendItemViewData item in (IEnumerable)legendItemGroup)
				{
					num2 = Math.Max(num2, item.Size.Width);
				}
				num += num2;
			}
			if (num > 0)
			{
				num += base.Padding.Horizontal;
				if (base.ItemGroups.Length > 1)
				{
					num += base.Legend.HorizontalIndent * (base.ItemGroups.Length - 1);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected override int CalcHeight()
		{
			int num = 0;
			LegendItemGroup[] array = base.ItemGroups;
			foreach (LegendItemGroup legendItemGroup in array)
			{
				int num2 = ((legendItemGroup.Count > 1) ? (base.Legend.VerticalIndent * (legendItemGroup.Count - 1)) : 0);
				foreach (LegendItemViewData item in (IEnumerable)legendItemGroup)
				{
					num2 += item.Size.Height;
				}
				num = Math.Max(num2, num);
			}
			if (num > 0)
			{
				num += base.Padding.Vertical;
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator : LegendSizeCalculator
	{
		internal HorizontalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups)
			: base(legend, itemGroups)
		{
		}

		protected override int CalcWidth()
		{
			int num = 0;
			LegendItemGroup[] array = base.ItemGroups;
			foreach (LegendItemGroup legendItemGroup in array)
			{
				int num2 = ((legendItemGroup.Count > 1) ? (base.Legend.HorizontalIndent * (legendItemGroup.Count - 1)) : 0);
				foreach (LegendItemViewData item in (IEnumerable)legendItemGroup)
				{
					num2 += item.Size.Width;
				}
				num = Math.Max(num, num2);
			}
			if (num > 0)
			{
				TreeMapPadding treeMapPadding = base.Padding;
				num += treeMapPadding.Left + treeMapPadding.Right;
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected override int CalcHeight()
		{
			int num = 0;
			LegendItemGroup[] array = base.ItemGroups;
			foreach (LegendItemGroup legendItemGroup in array)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				foreach (LegendItemViewData item in (IEnumerable)legendItemGroup)
				{
					num2 = Math.Max(item.Size.Height, num2);
				}
				num += num2;
			}
			if (num > 0)
			{
				TreeMapPadding treeMapPadding = base.Padding;
				num += treeMapPadding.Top + treeMapPadding.Bottom;
				if (base.ItemGroups.Length > 1)
				{
					num += base.Legend.VerticalIndent * (base.ItemGroups.Length - 1);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalLayoutWithVerticalAlignmentLegendSizeCalculator : HorizontalLayoutLegendSizeCalculator
	{
		internal HorizontalLayoutWithVerticalAlignmentLegendSizeCalculator(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups)
			: base(legend, itemGroups)
		{
		}

		protected override int CalcWidth()
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (base.ItemGroups.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.ItemGroups[0].Count; i++)
				{
					num += base.ItemGroups[0][i].Size.Width + base.ItemGroups[0][i].RightIndent;
					if (i < base.ItemGroups[0].Count - 1)
					{
						num += base.Legend.HorizontalIndent;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num > 0)
			{
				TreeMapPadding treeMapPadding = base.Padding;
				num += treeMapPadding.Left + treeMapPadding.Right;
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	public abstract class LegendItemsOffsetCalculator
	{
		private readonly LegendBase legend;

		private readonly IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups;

		protected LegendBase Legend => legend;

		protected IList<LegendItemGroup> ItemGroups => itemGroups;

		private static LegendItemsOffsetCalculator CreateInstance(LegendBase legend, IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups)
		{
			switch (legend.Direction)
			{
			case LegendDirection.TopToBottom:
			case LegendDirection.BottomToTop:
				return new VerticalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator(legend, itemGroups);
			case LegendDirection.LeftToRight:
			case LegendDirection.RightToLeft:
				if (!legend.EquallySpacedItems)
				{
					return new HorizontalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator(legend, itemGroups);
				}
				return new HorizontalLayoutWithVerticalAligningOffsetCalculator(legend, itemGroups);
			default:
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
		}

		public static void Calculate(LegendBase legend, IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups)
		{
			if (itemGroups.Count != 0)
			{
				LegendItemsOffsetCalculator legendItemsOffsetCalculator = CreateInstance(legend, itemGroups);
				legendItemsOffsetCalculator.CalculateRightIndents();
				legendItemsOffsetCalculator.CalculateOffsets();
			}
		}

		public LegendItemsOffsetCalculator(LegendBase legend, IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups)
		{
			this.legend = legend;
			this.itemGroups = itemGroups;
		}

		private int GetMaxCharacteristicLength(LegendItemGroup group)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (LegendItemViewData item in (IEnumerable)group)
			{
				num = Math.Max(num, GetCharacteristicLength(item.Size));
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected abstract int GetCharacteristicLength(Size size);

		protected abstract void CalculateOffsets();

		protected virtual void CalculateRightIndents()
		{
			foreach (LegendItemGroup itemGroup in ItemGroups)
			{
				int maxCharacteristicLength = GetMaxCharacteristicLength(itemGroup);
				foreach (LegendItemViewData item in (IEnumerable)itemGroup)
				{
					item.RightIndent = maxCharacteristicLength - GetCharacteristicLength(item.Size);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class VerticalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator : LegendItemsOffsetCalculator
	{
		public VerticalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator(LegendBase legend, IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups)
			: base(legend, itemGroups)
		{
		}

		protected override int GetCharacteristicLength(Size size)
		{
			return size.Width;
		}

		protected override void CalculateOffsets()
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.ItemGroups.Count; i++)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				for (int j = 0; j < base.ItemGroups[i].Count; j++)
				{
					LegendItemViewData legendItemViewData = base.ItemGroups[i][j];
					legendItemViewData.Arrange(new Rectangle(new Point(num, num2), legendItemViewData.Size));
					num2 += legendItemViewData.Size.Height + base.Legend.VerticalIndent;
				}
				num += base.ItemGroups[i][0].Size.Width + base.ItemGroups[i][0].RightIndent + base.Legend.HorizontalIndent;
			}
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator : LegendItemsOffsetCalculator
	{
		public HorizontalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator(LegendBase legend, IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups)
			: base(legend, itemGroups)
		{
		}

		protected override int GetCharacteristicLength(Size size)
		{
			return size.Height;
		}

		protected override void CalculateOffsets()
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.ItemGroups.Count; i++)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				for (int j = 0; j < base.ItemGroups[i].Count; j++)
				{
					LegendItemViewData legendItemViewData = base.ItemGroups[i][j];
					legendItemViewData.Arrange(new Rectangle(new Point(num2, num), legendItemViewData.Size));
					num2 += legendItemViewData.Size.Width + legendItemViewData.RightIndent + base.Legend.HorizontalIndent;
				}
				num += base.ItemGroups[i][0].Size.Height + base.Legend.VerticalIndent;
			}
		}
	}
	public class HorizontalLayoutWithVerticalAligningOffsetCalculator : HorizontalLayoutLegendItemsOffsetCalculator
	{
		public HorizontalLayoutWithVerticalAligningOffsetCalculator(LegendBase legend, IList<LegendItemGroup> itemGroups)
			: base(legend, itemGroups)
		{
		}

		private int[] CalculateWidths()
		{
			int[] array = new int[base.ItemGroups[0].Count];
			foreach (LegendItemGroup itemGroup in base.ItemGroups)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < itemGroup.Count; i++)
				{
					array[i] = Math.Max(array[i], itemGroup[i].Size.Width);
				}
			}
			return array;
		}

		protected override void CalculateRightIndents()
		{
			int[] array = CalculateWidths();
			foreach (LegendItemGroup itemGroup in base.ItemGroups)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < itemGroup.Count; i++)
				{
					itemGroup[i].RightIndent = array[i] - itemGroup[i].Size.Width;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class LegendItem : ILegendItem
	{
		public string Text { get; set; }

		public Color[] Colors { get; set; }
	}
	public class LegendItemGroup : IEnumerable
	{
		private readonly LegendItemViewData[] items;

		public int Count => items.Length;

		public LegendItemViewData this[int index] => items[index];

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return items.GetEnumerator();
		}

		public LegendItemGroup(LegendItemViewData[] items)
		{
			this.items = items;
		}

		public void Render(IRenderer renderer, Point location)
		{
			LegendItemViewData[] array = items;
			foreach (LegendItemViewData legendItemViewData in array)
			{
				legendItemViewData.Render(renderer, location);
			}
		}
	}
	public class LegendItemViewData : ViewDataBase
	{
		private const int MarkerSize = 12;

		private readonly LegendItem item;

		private readonly Color textColor;

		private readonly LegendBase legend;

		private Size markerSize;

		private SizeF textSize;

		internal int RightIndent { get; set; }

		public Size Size { get; }

		public LegendItemViewData(LegendBase legend, ILegendItem item, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider)
		{
			this.legend = legend;
			this.item = (LegendItem)item;
			textSize = TextMeasurer.Default.MeasureString(item.Text, legend.DXFont);
			int num = MathUtils.ScaleHorizontally(appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor, 12);
			markerSize = new Size(num, num);
			textColor = (legend.TextColor.IsEmpty ? appearanceProvider.TextColor : legend.TextColor);
			Size = new SizeF((float)(num + MathUtils.ScaleHorizontally(appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor, legend.TextOffset)) + textSize.Width, Math.Max(num, textSize.Height)).ToSize();
			Arrange(new Rectangle(Point.Empty, Size));
		}

		protected internal DXBrush CreateMarkerBrush(Rectangle markerRect)
		{
			Color[] colors = item.Colors;
			if (colors != null && colors.Length == 0)
			{
				return new DXSolidBrush(Color.Transparent);
			}
			if (item.Colors.Length == 2)
			{
				return new DXLinearGradientBrush(markerRect, item.Colors[0], item.Colors[1], DXLinearGradientMode.ForwardDiagonal);
			}
			return new DXSolidBrush(item.Colors[0]);
		}

		public void Render(IRenderer renderer, Point location)
		{
			location.Offset(base.Bounds.Location);
			float num = (float)location.Y + (float)Size.Height * 0.5f;
			Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(location.X, (int)(num - (float)markerSize.Height * 0.5f), markerSize.Width, markerSize.Height);
			renderer.SetSmoothingMode(DXSmoothingMode.None);
			using (DXBrush brush = CreateMarkerBrush(rectangle))
			{
				renderer.FillRectangle(rectangle, brush);
			}
			renderer.RestoreSmoothingMode();
			renderer.DrawBoundedText(item.Text, legend.DXFont, textColor, new RectangleF(location.X + markerSize.Width + legend.TextOffset, num - textSize.Height * 0.5f, textSize.Width, textSize.Height), DXStringAlignment.Near, DXStringAlignment.Near, DXTextRenderingHint.AntiAlias);
		}

		public override void Render(IRenderer renderer)
		{
			Render(renderer, Point.Empty);
		}
	}
	public abstract class LegendViewDataBase : ViewDataBase
	{
		private readonly LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups;

		protected IAppearanceProvider AppearanceProvider { get; }

		internal LegendTitleViewData TitleViewData { get; }

		internal Size OuterSize { get; private set; }

		public LegendBase Legend { get; }

		protected LegendViewDataBase(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups, LegendTitleViewData titleViewData, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Rectangle availableBounds, Size innerSize)
		{
			Legend = legend;
			TitleViewData = titleViewData;
			AppearanceProvider = appearanceProvider;
			this.itemGroups = itemGroups;
			CalculateViewParams(availableBounds, innerSize, appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor);
		}

		private void CalculateViewParams(Rectangle availableBounds, Size innerSize, SizeF scaleFactor)
		{
			TreeMapPadding treeMapPadding = MathUtils.ScalePadding(scaleFactor, Legend.Margins);
			Size size = new Size(innerSize.Width + treeMapPadding.Horizontal, innerSize.Height + treeMapPadding.Vertical);
			int num = AlignRectangleX(availableBounds, size, Legend.HorizontalAlignment);
			int num2 = AlignRectangleY(availableBounds, size, Legend.VerticalAlignment);
			OuterSize = size;
			Arrange(new Rectangle(new Point(num + treeMapPadding.Left, num2 + treeMapPadding.Top), innerSize));
		}

		private int AlignRectangleX(Rectangle outerRect, Size innerRectSize, LegendHorizontalAlignment alignment)
		{
			switch (alignment)
			{
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.LeftOutside:
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.Left:
				return outerRect.Left;
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.Center:
				return outerRect.Left + (outerRect.Width - innerRectSize.Width) / 2;
			default:
				return outerRect.Right - innerRectSize.Width;
			}
		}

		private int AlignRectangleY(Rectangle outerRect, Size innerRectSize, LegendVerticalAlignment alignment)
		{
			switch (alignment)
			{
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.Center:
				return outerRect.Top + (outerRect.Height - innerRectSize.Height) / 2;
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.Bottom:
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.BottomOutside:
				return outerRect.Bottom - innerRectSize.Height;
			default:
				return outerRect.Top;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateLocationAfterBoundsCorrection(Rectangle availableBounds, int verticalOffset, int bottomCorrection)
		{
			TreeMapPadding margins = Legend.Margins;
			int x = base.Bounds.X;
			switch (Legend.HorizontalAlignment)
			{
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.Left:
				x = availableBounds.Left + margins.Left;
				break;
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.Right:
				x = availableBounds.Right - base.Bounds.Width - margins.Left;
				break;
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.RightOutside:
				x = availableBounds.Right + margins.Left;
				break;
			case LegendHorizontalAlignment.Center:
				x = availableBounds.X + availableBounds.Width / 2 - base.Bounds.Width / 2 + margins.Left;
				break;
			}
			int y = base.Bounds.Y;
			switch (Legend.VerticalAlignment)
			{
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.TopOutside:
				y = base.Bounds.Location.Y + verticalOffset;
				break;
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.Top:
				y = availableBounds.Top + verticalOffset + margins.Top;
				break;
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.Bottom:
				y = availableBounds.Bottom - verticalOffset - base.Bounds.Height - margins.Top;
				break;
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.BottomOutside:
				y = availableBounds.Bottom + bottomCorrection - verticalOffset - base.Bounds.Height - margins.Top;
				break;
			case LegendVerticalAlignment.Center:
				y = availableBounds.Y + availableBounds.Height / 2 - base.Bounds.Height / 2 + verticalOffset + margins.Top;
				break;
			}
			Arrange(new Rectangle(new Point(x, y), base.Bounds.Size));
		}

		public override void Render(IRenderer renderer)
		{
			renderer.SetSmoothingMode(DXSmoothingMode.None);
			renderer.FillRectangle(base.Bounds, Legend.BackColor.IsEmpty ? AppearanceProvider.OverlayFillColor : Legend.BackColor);
			renderer.DrawRectangle(base.Bounds, Legend.BorderColor.IsEmpty ? AppearanceProvider.OverlayBorderColor : Legend.BorderColor, Legend.BorderWidth);
			renderer.RestoreSmoothingMode();
			Point location = base.Bounds.Location;
			TreeMapPadding padding = Legend.Padding;
			location.Offset(padding.Left, padding.Top);
			if (TitleViewData != null)
			{
				TitleViewData.Render(renderer);
				location.Offset(0, (int)TitleViewData.Size.Height + padding.Top);
			}
			LegendItemGroup[] array = itemGroups;
			foreach (LegendItemGroup legendItemGroup in array)
			{
				legendItemGroup.Render(renderer, location);
			}
		}

		public override void Arrange(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			base.Arrange(bounds);
			TitleViewData?.Arrange(new Rectangle(bounds.X, bounds.Y + Legend.Padding.Top, bounds.Width, bounds.Height));
		}
	}
	public class ColorListLegendViewData : LegendViewDataBase
	{
		public ColorListLegendViewData(LegendBase legend, LegendItemGroup[] itemGroups, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, LegendTitleViewData titleViewData, Rectangle availableBounds, Size innerSize)
			: base(legend, itemGroups, titleViewData, appearanceProvider, availableBounds, innerSize)
		{
		}

		public static LegendViewDataBase Create(LegendBase legend, IList<ILegendItem> legendItems, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Rectangle arrangeBounds)
		{
			if (legendItems.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = MathUtils.ScaleHorizontally(appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor, legend.BorderWidth * 2);
			TreeMapPadding treeMapPadding = MathUtils.ScalePadding(appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor, legend.Margins);
			int width = arrangeBounds.Width - num - treeMapPadding.Horizontal;
			int height = arrangeBounds.Height - num - treeMapPadding.Vertical;
			Size size = new Size(width, height);
			if (!TreeMapUtils.IsValidSize(size))
			{
				return null;
			}
			List<LegendItemViewData> list = new List<LegendItemViewData>(legendItems.Count);
			foreach (ILegendItem legendItem in legendItems)
			{
				list.Add(new LegendItemViewData(legend, legendItem, appearanceProvider));
			}
			LegendTitleViewData titleViewData;
			LegendItemGroup[] groups;
			Size size2 = CalculateViewDataParams(appearanceProvider, legend, size, list, out titleViewData, out groups);
			groups = groups ?? new LegendItemGroup[0];
			if (!TreeMapUtils.IsValidSize(size2) || size.Width < size2.Width || size.Height < size2.Height)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return new ColorListLegendViewData(legend, groups, appearanceProvider, titleViewData, arrangeBounds, size2);
		}

		private static Size CalculateTitleData(LegendTitle title, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, int availableWidth, out LegendTitleViewData titleViewData)
		{
			titleViewData = (title.Visible ? new LegendTitleViewData(title, appearanceProvider, availableWidth) : null);
			if (titleViewData == null)
			{
				return Size.Empty;
			}
			return titleViewData.Size.ToSize();
		}

		private static Size CalculateGroups(LegendBase legend, List<LegendItemViewData> itemsData, Size availableSize, out LegendItemGroup[] groups)
		{
			groups = LegendItemGroupingAlgorithm.Calculate(legend, itemsData, availableSize);
			LegendItemsOffsetCalculator.Calculate(legend, groups);
			return LegendSizeCalculator.Calculate(legend, groups);
		}

		private static Size CalculateViewDataParams(IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, LegendBase legend, Size maxLegendSize, List<LegendItemViewData> itemsData, out LegendTitleViewData titleViewData, out LegendItemGroup[] groups)
		{
			titleViewData = null;
			groups = null;
			TreeMapPadding treeMapPadding = MathUtils.ScalePadding(appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor, legend.Padding);
			int horizontal = treeMapPadding.Horizontal;
			Size result = maxLegendSize;
			bool flag = true;
			bool flag2 = false;
			Size size = Size.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			while (++num < 3)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					Size size2 = CalculateTitleData(legend.Title, appearanceProvider, result.Width - horizontal, out titleViewData);
					flag2 = size2 != size || groups == null;
					flag = false;
					size = size2;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					Size size3 = CalculateGroups(legend, itemsData, new Size(maxLegendSize.Width, maxLegendSize.Height - size.Height), out groups);
					flag = size3.Width < size.Width && legend.Title.Visible && legend.Title.WordWrap;
					flag2 = false;
					result = size3;
				}
				if (!flag && !flag2)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (titleViewData != null)
			{
				size = CalculateTitleData(legend.Title, appearanceProvider, result.Width - horizontal, out titleViewData);
				int val = size.Width + horizontal;
				result = new Size(Math.Max(result.Width, val), result.Height + size.Height + treeMapPadding.Top);
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalChartControllerFactory : ModelControllerFactoryBase
	{
		public override IChartRenderContext CreateRenderContext(ModelRect bounds, params object[] renderParams)
		{
			try
			{
				ModelRect windowBounds = ((renderParams.Length > 1) ? ((ModelRect)renderParams[1]) : bounds);
				ITreeMapDpiProvider dpiProvider = ((renderParams.Length > 2) ? (renderParams[2] as ITreeMapDpiProvider) : null);
				object obj = renderParams[0];
				if (obj is IDXGraphics gr)
				{
					return new HierarchicalChartRenderContext(gr, bounds, windowBounds, dpiProvider);
				}
				return new HierarchicalChartRenderContext((Graphics)obj, bounds, windowBounds, dpiProvider);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapModelControllerFactory : HierarchicalChartControllerFactory
	{
		public override IController CreateController()
		{
			return new TreeMapController();
		}
	}

	public abstract class HierarchicalChartController : Controller
	{
		internal HierarchicalChartConfigurator Configurator { get; }

		internal InnerHierarchicalControlBase HierarchicalChart { get; }

		public HierarchicalChartController()
		{
			HierarchicalChart = CreateHierarchicalChart();
			Configurator = CreateConfigurator(HierarchicalChart);
		}

		protected abstract InnerHierarchicalControlBase CreateHierarchicalChart();

		protected abstract HierarchicalChartConfigurator CreateConfigurator(InnerHierarchicalControlBase innerHierarchicalControlBase);

		public override HitInfo CalcHitInfo(double x, double y)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override void ClearSelection()
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override void Deselect(ModelElement element)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override void Select(ModelElement element)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		protected override void OnModelChanged()
		{
			Configurator.Configure((HierarchicalChart)base.ChartModel);
		}

		public override void RenderChart(IChartRenderContext renderContext)
		{
			if (renderContext is HierarchicalChartRenderContext { Bounds: var bounds } hierarchicalChartRenderContext)
			{
				Rectangle bounds2 = new Rectangle((int)bounds.Left, (int)bounds.Top, (int)bounds.Width, (int)bounds.Height);
				HierarchicalChart.UpdateDpiProvider(hierarchicalChartRenderContext.DpiProvider);
				HierarchicalChart.DrawContent(hierarchicalChartRenderContext.Graphics, bounds2, drawToMetaFile: true);
			}
		}
	}
