		}
	}
	internal class ModelSupportLabelFormatter : ILabelFormatter
	{
		private readonly IDataLabelFormatter formatter;

		public ModelSupportLabelFormatter(IDataLabelFormatter formatter)
		{
			this.formatter = formatter;
		}

		public string GetDataLabelText(object sourceObject, object labelObject)
		{
			LabelPointData pointData = new LabelPointData(sourceObject, labelObject, double.NaN);
			return formatter.GetDataLabelText(pointData);
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalChartConfigurator
	{
		public InnerHierarchicalControlBase HierarchicalChart { get; }

		public HierarchicalChartConfigurator(InnerHierarchicalControlBase hierarchicalChart)
		{
			HierarchicalChart = hierarchicalChart;
		}

		private void ConfigColorizer(HierarchicalChart hierarchicalChartModel)
		{
			Palette palette = new Palette();
			foreach (PaletteEntry entry in hierarchicalChartModel.Palette.Entries)
			{
				palette.Add(entry.Color.ToColor());
			}
			SetColorizer(hierarchicalChartModel, palette, hierarchicalChartModel.DataAdapter.HasGroupData);
		}

		private void ConfigureTitles(TitleCollection titles, ChartTitleCollection model, ChartTitleAppearance appearance)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<Title>)(object)titles).Clear();
			foreach (ChartTitle item in (DXCollectionBase<ChartTitle>)(object)model)
			{
				Title title = new Title();
				string text = string.Empty;
				string[] lines = item.Lines;
				if (lines != null)
				{
					string[] array = lines;
					foreach (string text2 in array)
					{
						text += text2;
						if (text2 != lines[lines.Length - 1])
						{
							text += "\n";
						}
					}
				}
				title.Text = text;
				title.IsOverlay = item.IsOverlay;
				title.DXFont = FontConfiguratorHelper.ConfigureDXFont(item.Font, title.DXFont.Name) ?? title.DXFont;
				if (appearance != null)
				{
					title.TextColor = appearance.TextColor.ToColor();
					title.Indent = appearance.Indent;
				}
				((DXCollectionBase<Title>)(object)titles).Add(title);
			}
		}

		protected abstract void SetDataAdapter(HierarchicalChart model);

		protected abstract void SetColorizer(HierarchicalChart treeMapModel, Palette palette, bool hasGroupData);

		protected abstract void ConfigureCore(HierarchicalChart model);

		public void Configure(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			if (model != null)
			{
				if (model.Palette != null)
				{
					ConfigColorizer(model);
				}
				HierarchicalChart.LabelFormatter = new ModelSupportLabelFormatter(model.LabelFormatter);
				SetDataAdapter(model);
				ApplyPlotAppearance(model.PlotAppearance);
				ConfigureTitles(HierarchicalChart.Titles, model.Titles, model.PlotAppearance?.TitleAppearance);
				ConfigureLegend(model.Legend, model.LegendAppearance);
				HierarchicalChart.SelectionMode = ElementSelectionMode.None;
				ConfigureCore(model);
			}
		}

		private void ApplyPlotAppearance(ChartAppearance plotAppearance)
		{
			if (plotAppearance != null)
			{
				HierarchicalChart.BackColor = plotAppearance.BackColor.ToColor();
				bool flag = plotAppearance.Border != null;
				HierarchicalChart.BorderOptions.Visible = flag;
				if (flag)
				{
					HierarchicalChart.BorderOptions.Thickness = plotAppearance.Border.Thickness;
					HierarchicalChart.BorderOptions.Color = plotAppearance.Border.Color.ToColor();
				}
			}
		}

		private void ConfigureLegend(Legend legendModel, LegendAppearance appearance)
		{
			((DXCollectionBase<LegendBase>)(object)HierarchicalChart.Legends).Clear();
			if (legendModel == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			LegendBase legendBase = new ColorListLegend();
			legendBase.Visible = true;
			legendBase.HorizontalAlignment = (LegendHorizontalAlignment)LegendPositionConverter.ConverterToHorizontalAlignment(legendModel.LegendPosition, legendModel.Overlay);
			legendBase.VerticalAlignment = (LegendVerticalAlignment)LegendPositionConverter.ConverterToVerticalAlignment(legendModel.LegendPosition, legendModel.Overlay);
			legendBase.Direction = ((legendModel.Orientation != LegendOrientation.Vertical) ? LegendDirection.LeftToRight : LegendDirection.TopToBottom);
			Border border = legendModel.Border;
			legendBase.BorderWidth = 0;
			if (border != null)
			{
				if (border.Color != ColorARGB.Empty)
				{
					legendBase.BorderColor = border.Color.ToColor();
				}
				legendBase.BorderWidth = border.Thickness;
			}
			if (appearance != null)
			{
				legendBase.BackColor = appearance.BackColor.ToColor();
				legendBase.TextColor = appearance.TextColor.ToColor();
			}
			legendBase.Title.Visible = false;
			DXFont dXFont = FontConfiguratorHelper.ConfigureDXFont(legendModel.Font, legendBase.DXFont.Name);
			if (dXFont != null)
			{
				legendBase.DXFont = dXFont;
			}
			((DXCollectionBase<LegendBase>)(object)HierarchicalChart.Legends).Add(legendBase);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapConfigurator : HierarchicalChartConfigurator
	{
		public InnerTreeMap TreeMap => (InnerTreeMap)base.HierarchicalChart;

		public TreeMapConfigurator(InnerTreeMap treeMap)
			: base(treeMap)
		{
		}

		private ITreeMapDataAdapter CreateFlatAdapter(HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter modelFlatDataAdapter, object dataSource)
		{
			TreeMapFlatDataAdapter treeMapFlatDataAdapter = new TreeMapFlatDataAdapter
			{
				LabelDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.LabelDataMember,
				ValueDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ValueDataMember,
				ColorDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ColorDataMember
			};
			if (modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers != null)
			{
				string[] groupDataMembers = modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers;
				foreach (string text in groupDataMembers)
				{
					((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)treeMapFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers).Add(text);
				}
			}
			treeMapFlatDataAdapter.DataSource = dataSource;
			return treeMapFlatDataAdapter;
		}

		private ITreeMapDataAdapter CreateHierarchicalAdapter(HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter modelHierarchicalDataAdapter, object dataSource)
		{
			TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter = new TreeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter();
			foreach (HierarchicalChartDataMapping mapping in modelHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings)
			{
				treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings.Add(new TreeMapHierarchicalDataMapping
				{
					LabelDataMember = mapping.LabelDataMember,
					ValueDataMember = mapping.ValueDataMember,
					ChildrenDataMember = mapping.ChildrenDataMember,
					Type = mapping.Type
				});
			}
			treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter.DataSource = dataSource;
			return treeMapHierarchicalDataAdapter;
		}

		private void ConfigureLeaf(HierarchicalItemAppearance itemAppearance)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			bool borderVisible = itemAppearance.BorderVisible;
			DefaultBoolean leafBorderVisible = (DefaultBoolean)(!borderVisible);
			TreeMap.LeafBorderVisible = leafBorderVisible;
			if (borderVisible)
			{
				TreeMap.LeafStyle.BorderColor = itemAppearance.BorderColor.ToColor();
			}
			TreeMap.LeafStyle.LabelAlignment = (System.Drawing.StringAlignment)itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.HorizontalAlignment;
			TreeMap.LeafStyle.LabelVerticalAlignment = (System.Drawing.StringAlignment)itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.VerticalAlignment;
			TreeMap.LeafStyle.BorderBrightnessCoeff = itemAppearance.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
			TreeMap.LeafStyle.ShowLabel = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.Visible;
			if (!itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				TreeMap.LeafStyle.TextColor = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextColor.ToColor();
			}
			if (!itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextGlowColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				TreeMap.LeafStyle.TextGlowColor = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextGlowColor.ToColor();
			}
			TreeMap.LeafStyle.DXFont = FontConfiguratorHelper.ConfigureDXFont(itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.Font, TreeMap.LeafStyle.DXFont.Name) ?? TreeMap.LeafStyle.DXFont;
		}

		private void ConfigureGroup(HierarchicalItemAppearance itemAppearance)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			bool borderVisible = itemAppearance.BorderVisible;
			DefaultBoolean groupBorderVisible = (DefaultBoolean)(!borderVisible);
			TreeMap.GroupBorderVisible = groupBorderVisible;
			if (borderVisible)
			{
				TreeMap.GroupStyle.BorderColor = itemAppearance.BorderColor.ToColor();
			}
			TreeMap.GroupStyle.ShowLabel = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.Visible;
			if (!itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				Color color = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextColor.ToColor();
				TreeMap.GroupStyle.TextColor = color;
				TreeMap.GroupStyle.SubGroupTextColor = color;
			}
			if (!itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextGlowColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				TreeMap.GroupStyle.TextGlowColor = itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.TextGlowColor.ToColor();
			}
			TreeMap.GroupStyle.DXFont = FontConfiguratorHelper.ConfigureDXFont(itemAppearance.LabelAppearance.Font, TreeMap.GroupStyle.DXFont.Name) ?? TreeMap.GroupStyle.DXFont;
		}

		protected override void ConfigureCore(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			DevExpress.Charts.Model.TreeMap treeMap = (DevExpress.Charts.Model.TreeMap)model;
			if (treeMap.ItemAppearance != null)
			{
				ConfigureLeaf(treeMap.ItemAppearance);
			}
			if (treeMap.GroupAppearance != null)
			{
				ConfigureGroup(treeMap.GroupAppearance);
			}
		}

		protected override void SetColorizer(HierarchicalChart treeMapModel, Palette palette, bool hasGroupData)
		{
			TreeMap.Colorizer = new TreeMapColorObjectColorizer
			{
				Palette = palette,
				ColorizeGroups = hasGroupData
			};
		}

		protected override void SetDataAdapter(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			if (model.DataAdapter != null)
			{
				HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter = model.DataAdapter as HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter;
				TreeMap.DataAdapter = ((hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter != null) ? CreateFlatAdapter(hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter, model.DataSource) : CreateHierarchicalAdapter((HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter)model.DataAdapter, model.DataSource));
			}
		}
	}
	public class HierarchicalChartRenderContext : IChartRenderContext
	{
		public ModelRect WindowBounds { get; }

		public ModelRect Bounds { get; }

		public IDXGraphics Graphics { get; }

		public ITreeMapDpiProvider DpiProvider { get; }

		public HierarchicalChartRenderContext(IDXGraphics gr, ModelRect bounds, ModelRect windowBounds, ITreeMapDpiProvider dpiProvider)
		{
			Bounds = bounds;
			WindowBounds = windowBounds;
			Graphics = gr;
			DpiProvider = dpiProvider;
		}

		public HierarchicalChartRenderContext(Graphics gr, ModelRect bounds, ModelRect windowBounds, ITreeMapDpiProvider dpiProvider)
		{
			Bounds = bounds;
			WindowBounds = windowBounds;
			Graphics = new GdiPlusGraphics(gr);
			DpiProvider = dpiProvider;
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapColorObjectColorizer : TreeMapPaletteColorizer
	{
		protected override Color GetItemColor(ITreeMapItem item, TreeMapItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			object obj = (item as TreeMapItem)?.Color;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return base.GetItemColor(item, group);
			}
			if (obj is int)
			{
				return Color.FromArgb((int)obj);
			}
			if (obj is Color)
			{
				return (Color)obj;
			}
			if (obj is string)
			{
				try
				{
					return ColorTranslator.FromHtml((string)obj);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return Color.Empty;
		}
	}
	public static class ColorArgbExtensions
	{
		public static Color ToColor(this ColorARGB colorModel)
		{
			if (!colorModel.IsEmpty)
			{
				return Color.FromArgb(colorModel.A, colorModel.R, colorModel.G, colorModel.B);
			}
			return Color.Empty;
		}

		public static ColorARGB ToColorARGB(this Color color)
		{
			return new ColorARGB(color.A, color.R, color.G, color.B);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstModelControllerFactory : HierarchicalChartControllerFactory
	{
		public override IController CreateController()
		{
			return new SunburstController();
		}
	}
	public class SunburstController : HierarchicalChartController
	{
		protected override HierarchicalChartConfigurator CreateConfigurator(InnerHierarchicalControlBase innerHierarchicalControlBase)
		{
			return new SunburstConfigurator((InnerSunburst)innerHierarchicalControlBase);
		}

		protected override InnerHierarchicalControlBase CreateHierarchicalChart()
		{
			return new InnerSunburst(new ModelContainer(), isSpreadsheetContainer: true);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstConfigurator : HierarchicalChartConfigurator
	{
		public InnerSunburst Sunburst => (InnerSunburst)base.HierarchicalChart;

		public SunburstConfigurator(InnerSunburst sunburst)
			: base(sunburst)
		{
		}

		protected override void SetColorizer(HierarchicalChart treeMapModel, Palette palette, bool hasGroupData)
		{
			Sunburst.Colorizer = new SunburstColorObjectColorizer
			{
				Palette = palette,
				VaryColorInGroup = !hasGroupData
			};
		}

		protected override void SetDataAdapter(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			if (model.DataAdapter != null)
			{
				HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter = model.DataAdapter as HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter;
				Sunburst.DataAdapter = ((hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter != null) ? CreateFlatAdapter(hierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter, model.DataSource) : CreateHierarchicalAdapter((HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter)model.DataAdapter, model.DataSource));
			}
		}

		private ISunburstDataAdapter CreateFlatAdapter(HierarchicalChartFlatDataAdapter modelFlatDataAdapter, object dataSource)
		{
			SunburstFlatDataAdapter sunburstFlatDataAdapter = new SunburstFlatDataAdapter
			{
				LabelDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.LabelDataMember,
				ValueDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ValueDataMember,
				ColorDataMember = modelFlatDataAdapter.ColorDataMember
			};
			if (modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers != null)
			{
				string[] groupDataMembers = modelFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers;
				foreach (string text in groupDataMembers)
				{
					((DXCollectionBase<string>)(object)sunburstFlatDataAdapter.GroupDataMembers).Add(text);
				}
			}
			sunburstFlatDataAdapter.DataSource = dataSource;
			return sunburstFlatDataAdapter;
		}

		private ISunburstDataAdapter CreateHierarchicalAdapter(HierarchicalChartHierarchicalDataAdapter modelHierarchicalDataAdapter, object dataSource)
		{
			SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter = new SunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter();
			foreach (HierarchicalChartDataMapping mapping in modelHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings)
			{
				sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter.Mappings.Add(new SunburstHierarchicalDataMapping
				{
					LabelDataMember = mapping.LabelDataMember,
					ValueDataMember = mapping.ValueDataMember,
					ChildrenDataMember = mapping.ChildrenDataMember,
					Type = mapping.Type
				});
			}
			sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter.DataSource = dataSource;
			return sunburstHierarchicalDataAdapter;
		}

		protected override void ConfigureCore(HierarchicalChart model)
		{
			Sunburst sunburst = (Sunburst)model;
			Sunburst.HighlightMode = SunburstHighlightMode.None;
			Sunburst.StartAngle = sunburst.StartAngle;
			Sunburst.HoleRadiusPercent = sunburst.HoleRadiusPercent;
			Sunburst.ItemStyle.BorderColor = (sunburst.ItemAppearance.BorderVisible ? sunburst.ItemAppearance.BorderColor.ToColor() : Color.Transparent);
			LabelAppearance labelAppearance = sunburst.ItemAppearance.LabelAppearance;
			Sunburst.Label.Visible = labelAppearance.Visible;
			Sunburst.ItemStyle.BorderBrightnessCoeff = sunburst.ItemAppearance.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
			Sunburst.ItemStyle.ShowLabel = labelAppearance.Visible;
			if (!labelAppearance.TextColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				Sunburst.ItemStyle.TextColor = labelAppearance.TextColor.ToColor();
			}
			if (!labelAppearance.TextGlowColor.IsEmpty)
			{
				Sunburst.ItemStyle.TextGlowColor = labelAppearance.TextGlowColor.ToColor();
			}
			DXFont dXFont = FontConfiguratorHelper.ConfigureDXFont(labelAppearance.Font, Sunburst.ItemStyle.DXFont.Name);
			if (dXFont != null)
			{
				Sunburst.ItemStyle.DXFont = dXFont;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstColorObjectColorizer : SunburstPaletteColorizer
	{
		protected override Color GetItemColor(ISunburstItem item, SunburstItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			Color itemColor = base.GetItemColor(item, group);
			object obj = (item as SunburstItem)?.Color;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return itemColor;
			}
			if (obj is int)
			{
				return Color.FromArgb((int)obj);
			}
			if (obj is Color)
			{
				return (Color)obj;
			}
			if (obj is string)
			{
				try
				{
					return ColorTranslator.FromHtml((string)obj);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return Color.Empty;
		}
	}
	public class InnerHierarchicalControlPrinter : IDisposable, IBasePrintable
	{
		private readonly InnerHierarchicalControlBase treeMap;

		private ComponentExporter componentPrinter;

		private PrintingSystemBase printingSystem;

		private LinkBase link;

		private BrickGraphics graphics;

		private PrintImageFormat imageFormat;

		protected InnerHierarchicalControlBase TreeMap => treeMap;

		protected ComponentExporter ComponentPrinter
		{
			get
			{
				if (componentPrinter == null)
				{
					componentPrinter = CreateComponentPrinter();
				}
				return componentPrinter;
			}
		}

		public PrintImageFormat ImageFormat
		{
			get
			{
				return imageFormat;
			}
			set
			{
				imageFormat = value;
			}
		}

		public bool? DeviceIndependentPixel { get; set; }

		public bool CreatesIntersectedBricks => true;

		public InnerHierarchicalControlPrinter(InnerHierarchicalControlBase treeMap)
		{
			Guard.ArgumentNotNull((object)treeMap, "treeMap");
			this.treeMap = treeMap;
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && componentPrinter != null)
			{
				componentPrinter.Dispose();
				componentPrinter = null;
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		private PrintingSystemCommand[] GetSupportedCommands()
		{
			PrintingSystemCommand[] array = new PrintingSystemCommand[15];
			RuntimeHelpers.InitializeArray(array, (RuntimeFieldHandle)/*OpCode not supported: LdMemberToken*/);
			return (PrintingSystemCommand[])(object)array;
		}

		private void SetPrintingSystem(PrintingSystemBase ps)
		{
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (printingSystem != null)
			{
				printingSystem.AfterChange -= new ChangeEventHandler(OnPrintingSystemAfterChange);
			}
			printingSystem = ps;
			if (printingSystem != null)
			{
				printingSystem.AfterChange += new ChangeEventHandler(OnPrintingSystemAfterChange);
			}
		}

		private void SetCommandVisibility(IPrintingSystem ps, PrintingSystemCommand[] commands)
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			foreach (PrintingSystemCommand val in commands)
			{
				ps.SetCommandVisibility(val, true);
			}
		}

		private void OnPrintingSystemAfterChange(object sender, ChangeEventArgs e)
		{
			if (printingSystem != null && link != null)
			{
				string eventName = e.EventName;
				if (eventName == "PageSettingsChanged" || eventName == "AfterMarginsChange")
				{
					link.Margins = printingSystem.PageMargins;
					link.CreateDocument();
				}
			}
		}

		internal DXBitmap CreateDetailBitmap(Size size)
		{
			DXBitmap dXBitmap = CreateBitmapInstance(size);
			RenderToImageInternal(dXBitmap, size);
			return dXBitmap;
		}

		internal DXMetafile CreateDetailMetafile(Size clientSize)
		{
			DXMetafile dXMetafile = CreateMetafile(null, clientSize);
			try
			{
				DrawMetafile(dXMetafile, clientSize);
				return dXMetafile;
			}
			catch
			{
				dXMetafile.Dispose();
				throw;
			}
		}

		private DXBitmap CreateBitmapInstance(Size size)
		{
			return new DXBitmap(size.Width, size.Height);
		}

		private static DXMetafile CreateMetafile(Stream stream, Size size)
		{
			return DXMetafileCreator.Create(stream, size, DXMetafileFrameUnit.Pixel, DXEmfType.EmfPlusDual);
		}

		private void PrintDetailArea(BrickGraphics graphics)
		{
			if (treeMap != null)
			{
				this.graphics = graphics;
				if (DeviceIndependentPixel.HasValue)
				{
					this.graphics.DeviceIndependentPixel = DeviceIndependentPixel.Value;
				}
				Size size = CalculatePrintBounds(TreeMap.ClientSize, treeMap.PrintOptions.SizeMode);
				if (size.Width != 0 || size.Height != 0)
				{
					DXImage image = ((imageFormat == PrintImageFormat.Metafile) ? ((DXImage)CreateDetailMetafile(size)) : ((DXImage)CreateDetailBitmap(size)));
					DrawToBrickGraphics(this.graphics, size, image);
				}
			}
		}

		private void DrawToBrickGraphics(BrickGraphics brickGraph, Size size, DXImage image)
		{
			//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0032: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (image == null || (((brickGraph == null) | (size.Width == 0)) && size.Height == 0))
			{
				return;
			}
			DXGraphicsUnit pageUnit = brickGraph.PageUnit;
			try
			{
				brickGraph.PageUnit = DXGraphicsUnit.Pixel;
				ImageBrick val = new ImageBrick();
				val.ImageSource = new ImageSource(image);
				((VisualBrick)val).Sides = (BorderSide)0;
				val.DisposeImage = true;
				brickGraph.DrawBrick<ImageBrick>(val, new RectangleF(PointF.Empty, size));
			}
			finally
			{
				brickGraph.PageUnit = pageUnit;
			}
		}

		private void DrawMetafile(DXMetafile metafile, Size clientSize)
		{
			using IDXGraphics iDXGraphics = metafile.CreateGraphics();
			treeMap.DrawContent(iDXGraphics, new Rectangle(Point.Empty, clientSize), drawToMetaFile: true);
		}

		private void RenderToImageInternal(DXImage image, Size clientSize)
		{
			using IDXGraphics iDXGraphics = image.CreateGraphics();
			treeMap.DrawContent(iDXGraphics, new Rectangle(Point.Empty, clientSize), drawToMetaFile: false);
		}

		private SizeF GetPSSize()
		{
			PrintingSystemBase val = printingSystem;
			if (val == null)
			{
				return SizeF.Empty;
			}
			Rectangle pageBounds = val.PageBounds;
			DXMargins pageMargins = val.PageMargins;
			float width = GraphicsUnitConverter.Convert((float)pageBounds.Width - pageMargins.Left - pageMargins.Right - 1f, 100f, DXGraphicsDpi.UnitToDpi(DXGraphicsUnit.Pixel));
			float height = GraphicsUnitConverter.Convert((float)pageBounds.Height - pageMargins.Top - pageMargins.Bottom - 1f, 100f, DXGraphicsDpi.UnitToDpi(DXGraphicsUnit.Pixel));
			return new SizeF(width, height);
		}

		protected virtual ComponentExporter CreateComponentPrinter()
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ComponentExporter val = treeMap.Container.CreateComponentExporter();
			return (ComponentExporter)(((object)val) ?? ((object)new ComponentExporter((IBasePrintable)(object)this)));
		}

		protected virtual void ExecuteAction(Action action)
		{
			ComponentPrinter.ClearDocument();
			action();
		}

		public void Export(ExportTarget exportTarget, string fileName, ExportOptionsBase options = null)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (options == null)
			{
				ExecuteAction(delegate
				{
					//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					ComponentPrinter.Export(exportTarget, fileName);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				ExecuteAction(delegate
				{
					//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					ComponentPrinter.Export(exportTarget, fileName, options);
				});
			}
		}

		public void Export(ExportTarget exportTarget, Stream stream, ExportOptionsBase options = null)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (options == null)
			{
				ExecuteAction(delegate
				{
					//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					ComponentPrinter.Export(exportTarget, stream);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				ExecuteAction(delegate
				{
					//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
					ComponentPrinter.Export(exportTarget, stream, options);
				});
			}
		}

		internal virtual void ExportToMetafile(Stream stream, Size clientSize)
		{
			using DXMetafile metafile = CreateMetafile(stream, clientSize);
			DrawMetafile(metafile, clientSize);
		}

		protected virtual void ExportToBitmap(Stream stream, DXImageFormat format, Size clientSize)
		{
			using DXImage dXImage = CreateBitmapInstance(clientSize);
			if (format == DXImageFormat.Jpeg)
			{
				DXJpegImageFormatOptions imageFormatOptions = new DXJpegImageFormatOptions
				{
					Quality = 100
				};
				RenderToImageInternal(dXImage, clientSize);
				dXImage.Save(stream, imageFormatOptions);
			}
			else
			{
				RenderToImageInternal(dXImage, clientSize);
				dXImage.Save(stream, format);
			}
		}

		protected internal Size CalculatePrintBounds(Size displaySize, PrintSizeMode sizeMode)
		{
			switch (sizeMode)
			{
			case PrintSizeMode.Stretch:
				return Size.Truncate(GetPSSize());
			case PrintSizeMode.Zoom:
			{
				float num = (float)displaySize.Width / (float)displaySize.Height;
				SizeF pSSize = GetPSSize();
				if (pSSize.IsEmpty)
				{
					return Size.Empty;
				}
				if (num > pSSize.Width / pSSize.Height)
				{
					pSSize.Height = pSSize.Width / num;
				}
				else
				{
					pSSize.Width = pSSize.Height * num;
				}
				return Size.Truncate(pSSize);
			}
			default:
				return displaySize;
			}
		}

		public void Initialize(PrintingSystemBase ps, LinkBase link)
		{
			SetPrintingSystem(ps);
			this.link = link;
			if (printingSystem != null)
			{
				SetCommandVisibility((IPrintingSystem)(object)ps, GetSupportedCommands());
			}
		}

		public void Finalize(PrintingSystemBase ps, LinkBase link)
		{
			SetPrintingSystem(null);
			link = null;
		}

		public void CreateArea(string areaName, BrickGraphics graphics)
		{
			if (areaName == "Detail")
			{
				PrintDetailArea(graphics);
			}
		}

		public void ExportToImage(Stream stream, DXImageFormat format)
		{
			if (format == DXImageFormat.Emf || format == DXImageFormat.Wmf)
			{
				ExportToMetafile(stream, TreeMap.ClientSize);
			}
			else
			{
				ExportToBitmap(stream, format, TreeMap.ClientSize);
			}
		}

		public void ExportToImage(string filePath, DXImageFormat format)
		{
			using FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(filePath, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.ReadWrite);
			ExportToImage(fileStream, format);
			fileStream.Close();
		}
	}

	public class RootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior : GroupGradientColorizerBehavior
	{
		private GradientColorizerMode mode;

		private int currentGroupIndex;

		public GradientColorizerMode Mode
		{
			get
			{
				return mode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (mode != value)
				{
					base.Colorizer.PropertyChanging();
					mode = value;
					RaiseColorizerChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public RootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer, double defaultMax)
			: base(colorizer, defaultMax)
		{
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			if (mode == GradientColorizerMode.ByGroupLevel)
			{
				if (group.GroupLevel == 0)
				{
					currentGroupIndex = group.ItemIndex;
				}
				return CalcGradientColor(group.GroupLevel, group.MaxGroupLevel - 1, 0.0, currentGroupIndex, base.Min, 1);
			}
			return CalcGradientColor(group.ItemIndex, group.ItemCount - 1, 0.0, group.GroupIndex, base.Min, 1);
		}

		public override void Assign(PaletteColorizerBehaviorBase behaviorBase)
		{
			base.Assign(behaviorBase);
			if (behaviorBase is RootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior rootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior)
			{
				mode = rootGroupGradientColorizerBehavior.Mode;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstPaletteColorizerBehavior : PaletteColorizerBehavior
	{
		private Color currentColor;

		public SunburstPaletteColorizerBehavior(ISupportingPropertyChangedColorizer colorizer)
			: base(colorizer, defaultVaryColorInGroup: false)
		{
		}

		protected override Color GetItemColorCore(IHierarchicalItem item, IItemGroupInfo group)
		{
			if (base.VaryColorInGroup || group.GroupLevel == 0)
			{
				currentColor = base.ActualPalette.GetColor(group.ItemIndex);
			}
			return currentColor;
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (!base.VaryColorInGroup)
			{
				return ShouldSerializePalette();
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	public abstract class ViewDataBase
	{
		public Rectangle Bounds { get; private set; }

		public abstract void Render(IRenderer renderer);

		public virtual void Arrange(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			Bounds = bounds;
		}
	}
	public abstract class TitleViewDataBase : ViewDataBase
	{
		protected virtual DockStyleCore Dock => DockStyleCore.Top;

		protected TitleBase Title { get; }

		internal List<StringBlock> Blocks { get; private set; }

		public TitleViewDataBase(TitleBase title, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider)
		{
			Title = title;
			CalculateViewParams(appearanceProvider);
		}

		private void CalculateViewParams(IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider)
		{
			Color actualTextColor = Title.GetActualTextColor(appearanceProvider);
			Blocks = StringParser.Parse(actualTextColor, Color.Transparent, new HyperlinkSettings(), TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(Title.DXFont).Size, Title.Text, allowNewLineSymbols: true);
		}

		public override void Render(IRenderer renderer)
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < Blocks.Count; i++)
			{
				StringBlock stringBlock = Blocks[i];
				Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(base.Bounds.X, base.Bounds.Y + num, base.Bounds.Width, base.Bounds.Height - num);
				renderer.DrawHVText(stringBlock.Text, stringBlock.FontSettings.Color, Title.DXFont, rectangle, (DXStringAlignment)Title.TextAlignment, Dock);
				num += Title.DXFont.Height;
			}
		}
	}
	public class TitleViewData : TitleViewDataBase
	{
		protected override DockStyleCore Dock => (DockStyleCore)((Title)base.Title).Dock;

		public TitleViewData(Title title, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider)
			: base(title, appearanceProvider)
		{
		}
	}
	public class LegendTitleViewData : TitleViewDataBase
	{
		public SizeF Size { get; }

		public LegendTitleViewData(LegendTitle title, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, int availableWidth)
			: base(title, appearanceProvider)
		{
			float num = 0f;
			float num2 = 0f;
			int availableWidth2 = (title.WordWrap ? availableWidth : int.MaxValue);
			for (int i = 0; i < base.Blocks.Count; i++)
			{
				SizeF sizeF = TextMeasurer.Default.MeasureString(base.Blocks[i].Text, title.DXFont, availableWidth2, DXStringTrimming.Word);
				num = Math.Max(sizeF.Width, num);
				num2 += sizeF.Height;
			}
			Size = new SizeF(num, num2);
			Arrange(new Rectangle(Point.Empty, Size.ToSize()));
		}
	}
	public static class TitleViewDataCalculator
	{
		public static Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle> CalculateViewData(IList<Title> titles, IAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			List<ViewDataBase> list = new List<ViewDataBase>();
			if (titles != null && titles.Count < 1)
			{
				return new Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle>(list, bounds);
			}
			Rectangle rectangle = ReserveSpaceForTitles(titles, bounds, appearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor.Width);
			Dictionary<TitleDockStyle, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<TitleDockStyle, int>();
			foreach (Title title in titles)
			{
				if (!dictionary.TryGetValue(title.Dock, out var value))
				{
					dictionary.Add(title.Dock, (title.Dock != TitleDockStyle.Bottom) ? title.Indent : 0);
				}
				int boundsHeight = title.BoundsHeight;
				int num = (title.Rotated ? rectangle.Height : rectangle.Width);
				dictionary[title.Dock] += boundsHeight;
				if (num > 0 && boundsHeight > 0)
				{
					Point titlePosition = GetTitlePosition(title, bounds, rectangle, boundsHeight, value);
					TitleViewData titleViewData = new TitleViewData(title, appearanceProvider);
					titleViewData.Arrange(new Rectangle(titlePosition.X, titlePosition.Y, num, boundsHeight));
					list.Add(titleViewData);
				}
			}
			return new Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle>(list, rectangle);
		}

		private static Rectangle ReserveSpaceForTitles(IList<Title> titles, Rectangle bounds, float dpiScaleFactor)
		{
			Rectangle result = bounds;
			if (titles != null && titles.Count > 0)
			{
				TreeMapPadding titleIndents = GetTitleIndents(titles, dpiScaleFactor);
				int num = bounds.Width - titleIndents.Left - titleIndents.Right;
				int num2 = bounds.Height - titleIndents.Top - titleIndents.Bottom;
				if (num <= 0 || num2 <= 0)
				{
					return Rectangle.Empty;
				}
				result = new Rectangle(bounds.Left + titleIndents.Left, bounds.Top + titleIndents.Top, num, num2);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static TreeMapPadding GetTitleIndents(IList<Title> titles, float dpiScaleFactor)
		{
			TreeMapPadding result = default(TreeMapPadding);
			for (int i = 0; i < titles.Count; i++)
			{
				Title title = titles[i];
				if (title.Visible)
				{
					SizeF sizeF = TextMeasurer.Default.MeasureString(title.Text, title.DXFont);
					title.BoundsHeight = (int)sizeF.Height;
					float num = (float)title.Indent * dpiScaleFactor;
					switch (title.Dock)
					{
					case TitleDockStyle.Top:
						result.Top += (int)(sizeF.Height + num);
						break;
					case TitleDockStyle.Bottom:
						result.Bottom += (int)(sizeF.Height + num);
						break;
					case TitleDockStyle.Left:
						result.Left += (int)(sizeF.Height + num);
						break;
					case TitleDockStyle.Right:
						result.Right += (int)(sizeF.Height + num);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static Point GetTitlePosition(Title title, Rectangle bounds, Rectangle contentBounds, int titleHeight, int indentFromBorder)
		{
			int x = 0;
			int y = 0;
			switch (title.Dock)
			{
			case TitleDockStyle.Top:
				x = contentBounds.Left;
				y = bounds.Top + indentFromBorder;
				break;
			case TitleDockStyle.Bottom:
				x = contentBounds.Left;
				y = contentBounds.Bottom + indentFromBorder + title.Indent;
				break;
			case TitleDockStyle.Left:
				x = bounds.Left + indentFromBorder;
				y = contentBounds.Top;
				break;
			case TitleDockStyle.Right:
				x = bounds.Right - indentFromBorder - titleHeight;
				y = contentBounds.Top;
				break;
			}
			return new Point(x, y);
		}
	}
	public class BackCompatibilityFontController
	{
		private readonly Action onChanging;

		private readonly Action onChanged;

		private DXFont defaultDXFont;

		private DXFont userDXFont;

		private DXFont dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont;

		private Font defaultFont;

		private Font userFont;

		private Font fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont;

		public bool IsDefaultFont => DXFont == defaultDXFont;

		public Font Font
		{
			get
			{
				if (userFont != null)
				{
					return userFont;
				}
				if (userDXFont != null)
				{
					fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont = fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont ?? SystemDrawingConverter.CreateFont(userDXFont);
					return fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont;
				}
				if (defaultFont == null && defaultDXFont != null)
				{
					defaultFont = SystemDrawingConverter.CreateFont(defaultDXFont);
				}
				return defaultFont;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException();
				}
				if (userFont != value)
				{
					fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont?.Dispose();
					fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont = null;
					dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont = null;
					onChanging?.Invoke();
					userDXFont = null;
					userFont = value;
					onChanged?.Invoke();
				}
			}
		}

		public DXFont DXFont
		{
			get
			{
				if (userDXFont != null)
				{
					return userDXFont;
				}
				if (userFont != null)
				{
					dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont = dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont ?? SystemDrawingConverter.CreateDXFont(userFont);
					return dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont;
				}
				return defaultDXFont;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException();
				}
				if (userDXFont != value)
				{
					fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont?.Dispose();
					fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont = null;
					dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont = null;
					onChanging?.Invoke();
					userFont = null;
					userDXFont = value;
					onChanged?.Invoke();
				}
			}
		}

		public BackCompatibilityFontController(DXFont defaultDXFont, Action onChanging, Action onChanged)
		{
			this.defaultDXFont = defaultDXFont;
			this.onChanging = onChanging;
			this.onChanged = onChanged;
		}

		public bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			return !IsDefaultFont;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont?.Dispose();
			fontGeneratedFromUserDXFont = null;
			dxFontGeneratedFromUserFont = null;
			onChanging?.Invoke();
			userFont = null;
			userDXFont = null;
			onChanged?.Invoke();
		}

		public void Assign(BackCompatibilityFontController fontController)
		{
			defaultDXFont = fontController.defaultDXFont;
			userFont = fontController.userFont;
			userDXFont = fontController.userDXFont;
		}
	}
	public static class MathUtils
	{
		public static int StrongRound(double value)
		{
			return Math.Sign(value) * (int)(Math.Abs(value) + 0.5);
		}

		public static int Ceiling(double doubleNumber)
		{
			return (int)Math.Ceiling(doubleNumber);
		}

		public static double Degree2Radian(double angleDegree)
		{
			return angleDegree * Math.PI / 180.0;
		}

		public static double Radian2Degree(double angleRadian)
		{
			return angleRadian * 180.0 / Math.PI;
		}

		public static int ScaleHorizontally(SizeF scaleFactor, int value)
		{
			return (int)((float)value * scaleFactor.Width);
		}

		public static int ScaleVertically(SizeF scaleFactor, int value)
		{
			return (int)((float)value * scaleFactor.Height);
		}

		public static TreeMapPadding ScalePadding(SizeF scaleFactor, TreeMapPadding padding)
		{
			return new TreeMapPadding(ScaleHorizontally(scaleFactor, padding.Left), ScaleVertically(scaleFactor, padding.Top), ScaleHorizontally(scaleFactor, padding.Right), ScaleVertically(scaleFactor, padding.Bottom));
		}
	}

		Layout = 4,
		Appearance = 8
	}
