	public static class TreeMapUtils
	{
		public static Assembly XtraTreeMapAssembly => typeof(InnerTreeMap).Assembly;

		public static bool IsColorEmpty(Color color)
		{
			return Color.Empty.ToArgb() == color.ToArgb();
		}

		public static bool IsValidSize(Size size)
		{
			if (size.Width > 0)
			{
				return size.Height > 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static Type[] GetTypeDescendants(Assembly assembly, Type ancestorType, List<Type> ignoredTypes)
		{
			Type[] types = assembly.GetTypes();
			List<Type> list = new List<Type>();
			Type[] array = types;
			foreach (Type type in array)
			{
				if (ancestorType.IsAssignableFrom(type) && type.IsClass && !type.IsAbstract && !ignoredTypes.Contains(type))
				{
					list.Add(type);
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}
	}
	public static class ElementHelper
	{
		public static void ChangeOwner(HierarchicalElement newOwner, IOwnedElement element)
		{
			if (newOwner != element.Owner)
			{
				if (newOwner != null)
				{
					element.Owner = newOwner;
				}
			}
		}

		public static T CreateTreeMapElement<T>(HierarchicalElement owner) where T : IOwnedElement, new()
		{
			T val = new T();
			ChangeOwner(owner, val);
			return val;
		}
	}
	public static class ColorizerCalculator
	{
		public static Color GetItemColor(double itemValue, IList<double> sortedRangeStops, Palette colors)
		{
			if (colors != null && !colors.IsEmpty && sortedRangeStops != null)
			{
				int num = sortedRangeStops.Count - 1;
				if (num > 0)
				{
					int rangeIndex = GetRangeIndex(itemValue, sortedRangeStops);
					if (rangeIndex >= 0)
					{
						return GetColorByIndex(rangeIndex, num, colors);
					}
				}
			}
			return Color.Empty;
		}

		public static Color GetColorByIndex(int index, int rangeCount, Palette colors)
		{
			if (((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)colors).Count < rangeCount)
			{
				return CalculateApproximatedColor((double)index / (double)(rangeCount - 1), colors);
			}
			return colors.GetColor(index);
		}

		public static Color MixColors(Color fromUnit, Color toUnit, double ratio)
		{
			return Color.FromArgb(MixChannel(fromUnit.A, toUnit.A, ratio), MixChannel(fromUnit.R, toUnit.R, ratio), MixChannel(fromUnit.G, toUnit.G, ratio), MixChannel(fromUnit.B, toUnit.B, ratio));
		}

		private static byte MixChannel(byte fromValue, byte toValue, double ratio)
		{
			return (byte)((double)(int)fromValue * (1.0 - ratio) + (double)(int)toValue * ratio);
		}

		private static Color CalculateApproximatedColor(double normalized, Palette colors)
		{
			double num = normalized * (double)(((DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)colors).Count - 1);
			int num2 = (int)Math.Floor(num);
			double ratio = num - (double)num2;
			return MixColors(colors.GetColor(num2), colors.GetColor(num2 + 1), ratio);
		}

		private static int GetRangeIndex(double value, IList<double> sortedRangeStops)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < sortedRangeStops.Count - 1; i++)
			{
				if (value >= sortedRangeStops[i] && value <= sortedRangeStops[i + 1])
				{
					return i;
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}
	}
	public class Locker
	{
		private int lockCount;

		public bool IsLocked => lockCount > 0;

		public void Lock()
		{
			lockCount++;
		}

		public void Unlock()
		{
			if (IsLocked)
			{
				lockCount--;
			}
		}
	}
	public static class GraphicUtils
	{
		public static Color XorColor(Color color)
		{
			if (IsGrayColor(color))
			{
				return Color.White;
			}
			return Color.FromArgb(255, color.R ^ 0xFF, color.G ^ 0xFF, color.B ^ 0xFF);
		}

		private static bool IsGrayColor(Color c)
		{
			if (IsGrayComponent(c.R) && IsGrayComponent(c.G))
			{
				return IsGrayComponent(c.B);
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static bool IsGrayComponent(int colorComponent)
		{
			if (colorComponent >= 110)
			{
				return colorComponent <= 150;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static float CalcFontEmSize(TextMeasurer textMeasurer, RectangleF bounds, string text, string fontFamily, DXStringAlignment alignment, DXStringAlignment lineAlignment)
		{
			if (text == "")
			{
				return 0f;
			}
			float num = 300f;
			DXFont font = new DXFont(fontFamily, num);
			SizeF sizeF = textMeasurer.MeasureString(text, font, alignment, lineAlignment);
			if (bounds.Height / bounds.Width > sizeF.Height / sizeF.Width)
			{
				return num * bounds.Width / sizeF.Width;
			}
			return num * bounds.Height / sizeF.Height;
		}

		public static Color ApplyTransparency(Color backColor, Color forwardColor)
		{
			double num = (double)(255 - forwardColor.A) / 255.0;
			byte red = (byte)((double)(int)forwardColor.R + (double)(backColor.R - forwardColor.R) * num);
			byte green = (byte)((double)(int)forwardColor.G + (double)(backColor.G - forwardColor.G) * num);
			byte blue = (byte)((double)(int)forwardColor.B + (double)(backColor.B - forwardColor.B) * num);
			return Color.FromArgb(255, red, green, blue);
		}
	}
	public static class Categories
	{
		public const string Behavior = "Behavior";

		public const string Appearance = "Appearance";

		public const string Elements = "Elements";

		public const string Data = "Data";

		public const string Layout = "Layout";
	}
	public static class TextPaintHelper
	{
		private class WordInfo
		{
			public int StartIndex { get; set; }

			public int EndIndex { get; set; }
		}

		private static IDXGraphics gr = new DXBitmap(1, 1).CreateGraphics();

		private const string ellipsisString = "…";

		private const string oneEllipsisChar = ".";

		private static DXStringFormat CreateStringFormat()
		{
			DXStringFormat dXStringFormat = DXStringFormat.CreateGenericDefault();
			dXStringFormat.FormatFlags = DXStringFormatFlags.LineLimit;
			return dXStringFormat;
		}

		public static Rectangle CorrectLabelBounds(Rectangle bounds, DXFont font)
		{
			DXFont dXFont = TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(font);
			return new Rectangle(bounds.X, bounds.Y + (bounds.Height - dXFont.Height + 2), bounds.Width, dXFont.Height);
		}

		public static string FormatSinglelineText(RectangleF labelBounds, DXFont font, string label)
		{
			DXStringFormat sf = CreateStringFormat();
			return GetTrimmedString(label, labelBounds.Width, font, sf);
		}

		public static List<string> FormatMultilineText(RectangleF labelBounds, DXFont font, string label, bool useEllipsis)
		{
			PointF location = new PointF(MathUtils.StrongRound(labelBounds.X), MathUtils.StrongRound(labelBounds.Y));
			labelBounds.Location = location;
			DXStringFormat sf = CreateStringFormat();
			List<string> resultText = FormatMultilineTextCore(labelBounds, font, label, sf);
			return ProcessText(resultText, labelBounds, font, sf, useEllipsis);
		}

		private static List<string> FormatMultilineTextCore(RectangleF bounds, DXFont font, string multilineText, DXStringFormat sf)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array = TextFormatter.SplitTextByNewLine(multilineText).ToArray();
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string textLine in array2)
			{
				list.AddRange(FormatOneLineText(bounds.Width, font, textLine, sf));
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static List<string> ProcessText(List<string> resultText, RectangleF bounds, DXFont font, DXStringFormat sf, bool useEllipsis)
		{
			List<string> actualVisibleText = GetActualVisibleText(resultText, bounds, font, sf, useEllipsis);
			if (useEllipsis)
			{
				EmulateTrimming(actualVisibleText, bounds.Width, font, sf);
			}
			return actualVisibleText;
		}

		private static List<string> GetActualVisibleText(List<string> resultText, RectangleF bounds, DXFont font, DXStringFormat sf, bool useEllipsis)
		{
			int num = (int)(bounds.Height / (float)font.Height);
			if (resultText.Count > num)
			{
				if (num > 0)
				{
					List<string> list = resultText.Take(num).ToList();
					if (useEllipsis)
					{
						list[list.Count - 1] += "…";
					}
					return list;
				}
				return new List<string>();
			}
			return resultText;
		}

		private static List<string> FormatOneLineText(float availableWidth, DXFont font, string textLine, DXStringFormat sf)
		{
			float width = gr.MeasureString(textLine, font, 99999f, sf).Width;
			if (width < availableWidth)
			{
				return new List<string> { textLine };
			}
			List<WordInfo> list = CreateWordInfo(textLine);
			if (list.Count == 1)
			{
				return new List<string> { textLine };
			}
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 1;
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				string text = textLine.Substring(list[num].StartIndex, list[i].EndIndex - list[num].StartIndex);
				float width2 = gr.MeasureString(text, font, 99999f, sf).Width;
				if (width2 > availableWidth)
				{
					list2.Add(text);
					num += num2;
					num2 = 1;
					continue;
				}
				if (i == list.Count - 1)
				{
					list2.Add(text);
					break;
				}
				string text2 = textLine.Substring(list[num].StartIndex, list[i + 1].EndIndex - list[num].StartIndex);
				float width3 = gr.MeasureString(text2, font, 99999f, sf).Width;
				if (width3 > availableWidth)
				{
					list2.Add(text);
					num += num2;
					num2 = 1;
				}
				else
				{
					num2++;
				}
			}
			return list2;
		}

		private static List<WordInfo> CreateWordInfo(string textSegment)
		{
			List<WordInfo> list = new List<WordInfo>();
			WordInfo wordInfo = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < textSegment.Length; i++)
			{
				if (char.IsWhiteSpace(textSegment[i]))
				{
					if (wordInfo != null)
					{
						wordInfo.EndIndex = i;
						list.Add(wordInfo);
						wordInfo = null;
					}
				}
				else if (wordInfo == null)
				{
					wordInfo = new WordInfo
					{
						StartIndex = i
					};
				}
			}
			if (wordInfo != null)
			{
				wordInfo.EndIndex = textSegment.Length;
				list.Add(wordInfo);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static void EmulateTrimming(List<string> textLines, float availableWidth, DXFont font, DXStringFormat sf)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < textLines.Count; i++)
			{
				textLines[i] = GetTrimmedString(textLines[i], availableWidth, font, sf);
			}
		}

		private static string GetTrimmedString(string textLine, float availableWidth, DXFont font, DXStringFormat sf)
		{
			if (IsTextOverflows(textLine, availableWidth, font, sf))
			{
				if (IsTextOverflows(textLine[0] + "…", availableWidth, font, sf))
				{
					if (IsTextOverflows(textLine[0] + ".", availableWidth, font, sf))
					{
						if (IsTextOverflows("…", availableWidth, font, sf))
						{
							return ".";
						}
						return "…";
					}
					return textLine[0] + ".";
				}
				string result = "…";
				for (int i = 1; i < textLine.Length; i++)
				{
					string text = textLine.Substring(0, i) + "…";
					if (IsTextOverflows(text, availableWidth, font, sf))
					{
						return result;
					}
					result = text;
				}
			}
			return textLine;
		}

		private static bool IsTextOverflows(string text, float availableWidth, DXFont font, DXStringFormat sf)
		{
			return gr.MeasureString(text, font, 99999f, sf).Width > availableWidth;
		}
	}
	public sealed class XmlUtils
	{
		private const string emptyColorName = "Empty";

		private const string xmlFontName = "Name";

		private const string xmlFontSize = "Size";

		private const string xmlFontBold = "Bold";

		private const string xmlFontItalic = "Italic";

		private const string xmlFontUnderline = "Underline";

		private const string xmlFontStrikeout = "Strikeout";

		private static Regex regex = new Regex("^(\\d+),\\s*(\\d+),\\s*(\\d+),\\s*(\\d+)$", RegexOptions.Compiled);

		public static string Color2String(Color color)
		{
			if (color.IsEmpty)
			{
				return "Empty";
			}
			if (color.IsKnownColor)
			{
				return color.Name;
			}
			return color.A + ", " + color.R + ", " + color.G + ", " + color.B;
		}

		public static Color String2Color(string colorString)
		{
			if (colorString == "Empty")
			{
				return Color.Empty;
			}
			Match match = regex.Match(colorString);
			if (match.Success)
			{
				int alpha = int.Parse(match.Groups[1].Value);
				int red = int.Parse(match.Groups[2].Value);
				int green = int.Parse(match.Groups[3].Value);
				int blue = int.Parse(match.Groups[4].Value);
				return Color.FromArgb(alpha, red, green, blue);
			}
			return Color.FromName(colorString);
		}

		public static string Enum2String(Enum enumValue)
		{
			return Enum.GetName(enumValue.GetType(), enumValue);
		}

		public static object String2Enum(string enumString, Type enumType)
		{
			Array values = Enum.GetValues(enumType);
			foreach (object item in values)
			{
				string name = Enum.GetName(enumType, item);
				if (name == enumString)
				{
					return item;
				}
			}
			throw new InvalidCastException();
		}

		public static void WriteColor(XmlWriter xmlWriter, Color color, string key)
		{
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString(key, Color2String(color));
		}

		public static Color ReadColor(XmlReader xmlReader, string key)
		{
			string colorString = xmlReader.ReadElementString(key);
			return String2Color(colorString);
		}

		public static void WriteEnum(XmlWriter xmlWriter, Enum enumValue, string key)
		{
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString(key, Enum2String(enumValue));
		}

		public static object ReadEnum(XmlReader xmlReader, string key, Type enumType)
		{
			string enumString = xmlReader.ReadElementString(key);
			return String2Enum(enumString, enumType);
		}

		public static void WriteInteger(XmlWriter xmlWriter, int value, string key)
		{
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString(key, value.ToString());
		}

		public static int ReadInteger(XmlReader xmlReader, string key)
		{
			string value = xmlReader.ReadElementString(key);
			return Convert.ToInt32(value);
		}

		public static float ReadFloat(XmlReader xmlReader, string key)
		{
			string value = xmlReader.ReadElementString(key);
			return Convert.ToSingle(value);
		}

		public static void WriteBoolean(XmlWriter xmlWriter, bool value, string key)
		{
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString(key, value.ToString());
		}

		public static bool ReadBoolean(XmlReader xmlReader, string key)
		{
			string value = xmlReader.ReadElementString(key);
			return Convert.ToBoolean(value);
		}

		public static void WriteFont(XmlWriter xmlWriter, Font font, string key)
		{
			xmlWriter.WriteStartElement(key);
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Name", font.Name);
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Size", font.Size.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Bold", font.Bold.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Italic", font.Italic.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Underline", font.Underline.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteElementString("Strikeout", font.Strikeout.ToString());
			xmlWriter.WriteEndElement();
		}

		public static Font ReadFont(XmlReader xmlReader, string key)
		{
			xmlReader.ReadStartElement(key);
			string familyName = xmlReader.ReadElementString("Name");
			float emSize = ReadFloat(xmlReader, "Size");
			bool flag = ReadBoolean(xmlReader, "Bold");
			bool flag2 = ReadBoolean(xmlReader, "Italic");
			bool flag3 = ReadBoolean(xmlReader, "Underline");
			bool flag4 = ReadBoolean(xmlReader, "Strikeout");
			xmlReader.ReadEndElement();
			FontStyle fontStyle = FontStyle.Regular;
			if (flag)
			{
				fontStyle |= FontStyle.Bold;
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				fontStyle |= FontStyle.Italic;
			}
			if (flag3)
			{
				fontStyle |= FontStyle.Underline;
			}
			if (flag4)
			{
				fontStyle |= FontStyle.Strikeout;
			}
			return new Font(familyName, emSize, fontStyle);
		}

		private XmlUtils()
		{
		}
	}
	public class ViewController : HierarchicalElement
	{
		private readonly IRenderer renderer;

		private bool updateLocked;

		private UpdateType updateActions;

		private bool needRedraw = true;

		private BitmapCache cacheBitmap;

		private bool DesignMode => Container.DesignMode;

		private bool TreeMapHasData
		{
			get
			{
				DataController dataController = InnerControl.DataController;
				if (dataController != null && dataController.Items != null)
				{
					return dataController.Items.Count() > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private InnerHierarchicalControlBase InnerControl { get; }

		private ITreeMapContainer Container => InnerControl.Container;

		private IHierarchicalView View => InnerControl.View;

		private IColorizerController ColorizerController => InnerControl.ColorizerControllerCore;

		public IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestableElements => View.HitTestableItems;

		public ViewController(IRenderer renderer, InnerHierarchicalControlBase innerControl)
		{
			this.renderer = renderer;
			InnerControl = innerControl;
			InnerControl.Owner = this;
		}

		private void ProcessData()
		{
			InnerControl.ClearGroups();
			View.UpdateItems(InnerControl);
			View.ColorizeItems(ColorizerController);
			InvalidateLayout();
		}

		private void ApplyAppearance()
		{
			View.ApplyAppearance();
			View.ColorizeItems(ColorizerController);
			InvalidateLayout();
		}

		private void Invalidate()
		{
			Container.Invalidate();
		}

		private void RegisterUpdate(UpdateType updateType)
		{
			updateActions |= updateType;
		}

		private bool ApplyUpdates()
		{
			if (updateLocked)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((updateActions & UpdateType.Data) != UpdateType.None)
			{
				ProcessData();
			}
			if ((updateActions & UpdateType.Appearance) != UpdateType.None)
			{
				ApplyAppearance();
			}
			if ((updateActions & UpdateType.Layout) != UpdateType.None)
			{
				InvalidateLayout();
			}
			if ((updateActions & UpdateType.NonSpecific) != UpdateType.None)
			{
				Invalidate();
			}
			updateActions = UpdateType.None;
			return true;
		}

		private bool CheckAndUpdateCache(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			if (cacheBitmap == null || cacheBitmap.Bounds != bounds)
			{
				cacheBitmap?.Dispose();
				cacheBitmap = new BitmapCache(bounds);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void UpdateRenderContext(IDXGraphics graphics, bool useAntiAliasing)
		{
			renderer.UpdateContext(graphics, useAntiAliasing);
		}

		protected override void ApplyPropertyChanging()
		{
			Container.Changing();
		}

		protected override void ApplyPropertyChanged()
		{
			Container.Changed();
		}

		protected override bool ProcessChanged(UpdateType updateType)
		{
			RegisterUpdate(updateType);
			return ApplyUpdates();
		}

		protected override HierarchicalElement CreateObjectForCloneCore()
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		protected override void DisposeOverride()
		{
			base.DisposeOverride();
			if (cacheBitmap != null)
			{
				cacheBitmap.Dispose();
				cacheBitmap = null;
			}
		}

		internal void LockUpdate()
		{
			updateLocked = true;
		}

		internal void UnLockUpdate()
		{
			updateLocked = false;
			ApplyUpdates();
		}

		public void Render(Rectangle bounds, IDXGraphics graphics, bool drawToMetaFile)
		{
			object state = graphics.Save();
			try
			{
				RenderCore(bounds, graphics, drawToMetaFile);
			}
			finally
			{
				graphics.Restore(state);
			}
		}

		private void RenderCore(Rectangle bounds, IDXGraphics graphics, bool drawToMetaFile)
		{
			IHierarchicalView view = View;
			view.CalculateLayout(InnerControl, bounds);
			view.UpdateStrategy(drawToMetaFile);
			bool allowAntiAliasing = view.AllowAntiAliasing;
			UpdateRenderContext(graphics, allowAntiAliasing);
			if (DesignMode && !TreeMapHasData)
			{
				view.RenderDesignModeNoDataPresentation(bounds);
			}
			else if (drawToMetaFile)
			{
				view.Render(bounds);
			}
			else
			{
				if (CheckAndUpdateCache(bounds) || needRedraw)
				{
					UpdateRenderContext(cacheBitmap.Graphics, allowAntiAliasing);
					cacheBitmap.Graphics.Clear(Color.Transparent);
					view.Render(bounds);
					cacheBitmap.DisposeGraphics();
					needRedraw = false;
					UpdateRenderContext(graphics, allowAntiAliasing);
				}
				cacheBitmap.Render(renderer);
				view.RenderHighlightedItems();
			}
			UpdateRenderContext(null, allowAntiAliasing);
		}

		public void InvalidateLayout()
		{
			needRedraw = true;
			View.ResetInnerBounds();
			Invalidate();
		}
	}
	public class BitmapCache : IDisposable
	{
		private readonly Rectangle bounds;

		private readonly Rectangle renderBounds;

		private IDXGraphics graphics;

		private DXBitmap image;

		public Rectangle Bounds => bounds;

		public IDXGraphics Graphics
		{
			get
			{
				if (graphics == null)
				{
					graphics = image.CreateGraphics();
				}
				return graphics;
			}
		}

		public BitmapCache(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			this.bounds = bounds;
			image = new DXBitmap(bounds.Width + bounds.Left, bounds.Height + bounds.Top);
			renderBounds = new Rectangle(Point.Empty, image.Size);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			DisposeGraphics();
			if (image != null)
			{
				image.Dispose();
				image = null;
			}
		}

		public void Render(IRenderer renderer)
		{
			renderer.SetSmoothingMode(DXSmoothingMode.None);
			renderer.DrawImage(image, renderBounds);
			renderer.RestoreSmoothingMode();
		}

		public void DisposeGraphics()
		{
			if (graphics != null)
			{
				graphics.Dispose();
			}
			graphics = null;
		}
	}
	public class SunburstCenterLabelViewData : IHitTestableElement, IPatternHolder
	{
		private readonly ISunburstAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider;

		private int radius;

		private Rectangle labelBounds;

		private string labelText;

		private Color backgroundColor;

		private Color foregroundColor;

		private DXFont textFont;

		private Point center;

		public Rectangle LabelBounds => labelBounds;

		public Color BackgroundColor => backgroundColor;

		public Color ForegroundColor => foregroundColor;

		public DXFont TextFont => textFont;

		public string LabelText
		{
			get
			{
				return labelText;
			}
			set
			{
				labelText = value;
			}
		}

		public bool CanRender
		{
			get
			{
				if (labelBounds.Width > 0)
				{
					return labelBounds.Height > 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		object IHitTestableElement.Object => appearanceProvider.CenterLabel;

		bool IHitTestableElement.IsHitTestVisible => true;

		IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> IHitTestableElement.Children => new IHitTestableElement[0];

		public SunburstCenterLabelViewData(ISunburstAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider)
		{
			this.appearanceProvider = appearanceProvider;
		}

		bool IHitTestableElement.HitTest(Point point, out bool isInElementDiapason)
		{
			isInElementDiapason = false;
			double num = point.X - center.X;
			double num2 = point.Y - center.Y;
			return num * num + num2 * num2 < (double)(radius * radius);
		}

		PatternDataProvider IPatternHolder.GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new CenterLabelPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		private Rectangle CalculateLabelBounds()
		{
			int x = center.X - radius;
			int y = center.Y - radius;
			return new Rectangle(x, y, 2 * radius, 2 * radius);
		}

		public void UpdateLayout(double centerLabelRadius, Point point)
		{
			center = point;
			radius = (int)Math.Ceiling(centerLabelRadius);
			labelBounds = CalculateLabelBounds();
		}

		public void CalculateLabelText(ICenterLabelPatternValueHolder patternValueHolder, string textPattern)
		{
			PatternParser patternParser = new PatternParser(textPattern, this);
			patternParser.SetContext(patternValueHolder);
			labelText = patternParser.GetText();
		}

		public void ApplyStyle(SunburstDefaultStyleProvider sunburstStyleProvider)
		{
			ICenterLabelAppearanceProvider centerLabel = appearanceProvider.CenterLabel;
			HierarchicalItemStyle defaultCenterLabelStyle = sunburstStyleProvider.DefaultCenterLabelStyle;
			if (defaultCenterLabelStyle != null)
			{
				backgroundColor = ((centerLabel.BackgroundColor == TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupFill) ? defaultCenterLabelStyle.Fill : centerLabel.BackgroundColor);
				foregroundColor = ((centerLabel.ForegroundColor == TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultRootGroupTextColor) ? defaultCenterLabelStyle.TextColor : centerLabel.ForegroundColor);
				textFont = ((centerLabel.DXTextFont == TreeMapItemStyleProvider.DefaultSunburstCenterLabelFont) ? defaultCenterLabelStyle.DXFont : centerLabel.DXTextFont);
			}
			else
			{
				backgroundColor = centerLabel.BackgroundColor;
				foregroundColor = centerLabel.ForegroundColor;
				textFont = centerLabel.DXTextFont;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SunburstItemViewData : HierarchicalItemViewDataBase
	{
		private const int DoublePi = 360;

		private const int halfPi = 90;

		private float labelAngle;

		private float startAngle;

		private float sweepAngle;

		private float radius;

		private float holeRadius;

		private Point center;

		public bool CanRender
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.ActualFill.ToArgb() != 0)
				{
					return base.Item.Value > 0.0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public float StartAngle => startAngle;

		public float SweepAngle => sweepAngle;

		public float Radius => radius;

		public float HoleRadius => holeRadius;

		public Point Center => center;

		public float LabelAngle => labelAngle;

		public new SunburstViewDataCollection Children => (SunburstViewDataCollection)base.Children;

		public bool CanRenderLabel => !base.LabelBounds.IsEmpty;

		public SunburstItemViewData(ISunburstItem item)
			: base(item)
		{
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<HierarchicalItemViewDataBase> CreateChildrenCollection()
		{
			return new SunburstViewDataCollection(this);
		}

		protected override bool HitTest(Point point, out bool isInElementDiapason)
		{
			isInElementDiapason = false;
			double num = point.X - Center.X;
			double num2 = point.Y - Center.Y;
			double num3 = Math.Sqrt(num * num + num2 * num2);
			if (num3 < (double)HoleRadius)
			{
				return false;
			}
			double num4 = MathUtils.Radian2Degree(Math.Acos(num / num3));
			if (num2 < 0.0)
			{
				num4 = 360.0 - num4;
			}
			if (IsInAngleRange(num4, startAngle, startAngle + sweepAngle))
			{
				isInElementDiapason = base.IsGroup;
				return num3 < (double)Radius;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override Color GetLegendColor()
		{
			return base.ActualFill;
		}

		private bool IsInAngleRange(double pointAngle, double startAngle, double finishAngle)
		{
			if (!(finishAngle <= 360.0))
			{
				if (!(pointAngle >= startAngle))
				{
					return pointAngle <= finishAngle % 360.0;
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (pointAngle >= startAngle)
			{
				return pointAngle <= finishAngle;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private RectangleF CalculateLabelLayout(float characterWidth, float fontHeight, ILabelAppearanceProvider labelOptions)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Label) || !labelOptions.Visible || !base.ShowLabel)
			{
				return RectangleF.Empty;
			}
			float num = StartAngle + SweepAngle / 2f;
			float num2 = (float)MathUtils.Degree2Radian(num);
			float num3 = radius - holeRadius;
			float num4 = holeRadius + num3 / 2f;
			if (labelOptions.DisplayMode == SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Horizontal)
			{
				double num5 = (double)num4 * Math.Sin(num2);
				double num6 = (double)num4 * Math.Cos(num2);
				Tuple<TreeMapRect, TreeMapRect> tuple = LabelBoundsCalculator.CalcHorizontalLabelBounds(MathUtils.Degree2Radian(startAngle + sweepAngle), MathUtils.Degree2Radian(startAngle), center.X, center.Y, holeRadius, radius, fontHeight);
				RectangleF rectangleF = tuple.Item1.ConvertToRectangleF();
				RectangleF rectangleF2 = tuple.Item2.ConvertToRectangleF();
				RectangleF result = ((rectangleF.Width > characterWidth && rectangleF.Width * rectangleF.Height > rectangleF2.Width * rectangleF2.Height) ? rectangleF : rectangleF2);
				if (!labelOptions.AutoLayout || result.Width > num3)
				{
					labelAngle = 0f;
					if (!(result.Width < characterWidth) && !double.IsNaN(result.Width) && !double.IsNaN(result.Height))
					{
						return result;
					}
					return RectangleF.Empty;
				}
			}
			else if (labelOptions.DisplayMode == SunburstLabelDisplayMode.Tangent)
			{
				double val = Math.Acos(1f - (num3 - fontHeight / 2f) / radius / 2f) * 2.0;
				double val2 = Math.Acos(1f - (num3 + fontHeight / 2f) / radius / 2f) * 2.0;
				double num7 = Math.Min(val, val2);
				double num8 = MathUtils.Degree2Radian(sweepAngle);
				float num9 = ((num7 < num8) ? (2f * radius * (float)Math.Sin(num7 / 2.0)) : Math.Abs(num4 * (float)Math.Tan(num8 / 2.0) * 2f));
				if (!labelOptions.AutoLayout || num9 > num3)
				{
					if (fontHeight > num3 || Math.Ceiling(num9 - characterWidth) < 0.0)
					{
						return RectangleF.Empty;
					}
					labelAngle = num + 90f;
					return CalculateLabelBounds(num4, num9, fontHeight, num2);
				}
			}
			if (sweepAngle - startAngle < 90f)
			{
				float num10 = Math.Max(num4 - characterWidth * (float)base.Label.Length * 0.8f / 2f, HoleRadius) * Math.Abs((float)Math.Tan((double)num2 - MathUtils.Degree2Radian(startAngle))) * 2f;
				if (num10 < fontHeight * 0.8f)
				{
					return RectangleF.Empty;
				}
			}
			labelAngle = num;
			return CalculateLabelBounds(num4, num3, fontHeight, num2);
		}

		private RectangleF CalculateLabelBounds(float distance, float width, float fontHeight, double angleRad)
		{
			float num = distance * (float)Math.Cos(angleRad);
			float x = (float)Center.X + num - width / 2f;
			float num2 = distance * (float)Math.Sin(angleRad);
			float y = (float)Center.Y + num2 - fontHeight / 2f;
			return new RectangleF(x, y, width, fontHeight);
		}

		public void SetStyle(SunburstItemStyle style)
		{
			base.Fill = style.Fill;
			base.BorderColor = style.BorderColor;
			base.DXFont = style.DXFont;
			base.TextColor = style.TextColor;
			base.GlowTextColor = style.TextGlowColor;
			base.ShowLabel = style.ShowLabel;
			base.BorderBrightnessCoeff = style.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
		}

		public void UpdateLayout(Point center, float holeRadius, float groupWidth, float startAngle, float sweepAngle, ILabelAppearanceProvider label)
		{
			this.center = center;
			this.holeRadius = holeRadius;
			radius = holeRadius + groupWidth;
			this.startAngle = startAngle;
			this.sweepAngle = sweepAngle;
			base.LabelBounds = CalculateLabelLayout(TextMeasurer.Default.GetSymbolWidth(base.DXFont, DXStringAlignment.Center, DXStringAlignment.Near), TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(base.DXFont).Height, label);
		}
	}
	public class SunburstViewDataCollection : IEnumerable<SunburstItemViewData>, IEnumerable
	{
		private class DescendingComparer : IComparer<SunburstItemViewData>
		{
			public int Compare(SunburstItemViewData x, SunburstItemViewData y)
			{
				return y.Item.Value.CompareTo(x.Item.Value);
			}
		}

		private readonly DescendingComparer descendingComparer = new DescendingComparer();

		private readonly List<SunburstItemViewData> viewDataCollection = new List<SunburstItemViewData>();

		private readonly SunburstItemViewData parent;

		private int selectedNodeCount;

		public int Count => viewDataCollection.Count;

		public SunburstItemViewData this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return viewDataCollection[index];
			}
			set
			{
				viewDataCollection[index] = value;
			}
		}

		public SunburstViewDataCollection(SunburstItemViewData parent)
		{
			this.parent = parent;
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return viewDataCollection.GetEnumerator();
		}

		private void OnSelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			selectedNodeCount += (((SunburstItemViewData)sender).IsSelected ? 1 : (-1));
			if (parent != null)
			{
				parent.IsSelected = Count == selectedNodeCount;
			}
		}

		public void Add(SunburstItemViewData viewData)
		{
			viewDataCollection.Add(viewData);
			if (viewData.IsSelected)
			{
				selectedNodeCount++;
			}
			else if (parent != null)
			{
				parent.IsSelected = false;
			}
			viewData.SelectionChanged += OnSelectionChanged;
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			foreach (SunburstItemViewData item in viewDataCollection)
			{
				item.SelectionChanged -= OnSelectionChanged;
			}
			viewDataCollection.Clear();
		}

		public IEnumerator<SunburstItemViewData> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return viewDataCollection.GetEnumerator();
		}

		public void OrderByDescending()
		{
			viewDataCollection.Sort(descendingComparer);
		}
	}
	public abstract class HierarchicalItemViewDataBase : IColorizerElement, IHitTestableElement, IGroupValueProvider, IInteractiveElement, ILegendSourceItem
	{
		private Color colorizerColor;

		private bool isSelected;

		private bool isHighlighted;

		protected bool ShowLabel { get; set; }

		protected float BorderBrightnessCoeff { get; set; }

		public IEnumerable<HierarchicalItemViewDataBase> Children { get; }

		public IHierarchicalItem Item { get; }

		public bool IsGroup => Children.Any();

		public Color Fill { get; protected set; }

		public Color ActualFill
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(colorizerColor != Color.Empty))
				{
					return Fill;
				}
				return colorizerColor;
			}
		}

		public Color BorderColor { get; protected set; }

		public bool StateChanged { get; protected set; }

		public string Label { get; private set; }

		public RectangleF LabelBounds { get; set; }

		public DXFont DXFont { get; protected set; }

		public Color GlowTextColor { get; protected set; }

		public Color TextColor { get; protected set; }

		public bool IsHighlighted
		{
			get
			{
				return isHighlighted;
			}
			set
			{
				if (isHighlighted != value)
				{
					isHighlighted = value;
					StateChanged = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected
		{
			get
			{
				return isSelected;
			}
			set
			{
				if (isSelected != value)
				{
					isSelected = value;
					StateChanged = true;
					RaiseIsSelectedChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		IHierarchicalItem IColorizerElement.Item => Item;

		bool IColorizerElement.IsGroup => IsGroup;

		Color IColorizerElement.Color
		{
			get
			{
				return colorizerColor;
			}
			set
			{
				colorizerColor = value;
			}
		}

		IEnumerable<IColorizerElement> IColorizerElement.Children => Children;

		object IHitTestableElement.Object => Item;

		bool IHitTestableElement.IsHitTestVisible => true;

		IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> IHitTestableElement.Children => Children;

		object IGroupValueProvider.GroupValue
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(Item is IGroupValueProvider groupValueProvider))
				{
					return Item;
				}
				return groupValueProvider.GroupValue;
			}
		}

		IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> IInteractiveElement.Children => Children;

		bool IInteractiveElement.IsGroup => IsGroup;

		object IInteractiveElement.Source => Item.Tag ?? Item;

		string ILegendSourceItem.Text => Label;

		object ILegendSourceItem.Brush => GetLegendColor();

		object ILegendSourceItem.SourceObject => Item;

		public event EventHandler SelectionChanged;

		bool IHitTestableElement.HitTest(Point point, out bool isInElementDiapason)
		{
			return HitTest(point, out isInElementDiapason);
		}

		protected HierarchicalItemViewDataBase(IHierarchicalItem item)
		{
			Item = item;
			Children = CreateChildrenCollection();
		}

		private void RaiseIsSelectedChanged()
		{
			this.SelectionChanged?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
		}

		protected abstract bool HitTest(Point point, out bool isInElementDiapason);

		protected abstract Color GetLegendColor();

		protected abstract IEnumerable<HierarchicalItemViewDataBase> CreateChildrenCollection();

		public void UpdateLabel(PatternParser patternParser)
		{
			patternParser.SetContext(Item);
			Label = patternParser.GetText();
		}

		public void UpdateLabel(ILabelFormatter formatter)
		{
			Label = formatter.GetDataLabelText(Item.Tag, Item.Label);
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapItemViewData : HierarchicalItemViewDataBase, ITreeMapItemLayout
	{
		public const float DefaultStrokeSize = 2.8f;

		private readonly bool isRootItem;

		private Rectangle headerBounds;

		private Rectangle boundsForChildren;

		private Rectangle roundedBounds;

		private Rectangle borderBounds;

		private TreeMapRect bounds;

		private bool borderVisibility;

		private TreeMapPadding borderThickness = new TreeMapPadding(1);

		private TreeMapPadding padding = new TreeMapPadding(4);

		private DXStringAlignment labelAlignment;

		private DXStringAlignment labelVerticalAlignment;

		private TreeMapPadding headerPadding = new TreeMapPadding(4);

		internal Rectangle HeaderBounds => headerBounds;

		public new ViewDataCollection Children => (ViewDataCollection)base.Children;

		public Rectangle BoundsForChildren => boundsForChildren;

		public Rectangle RoundedBounds => roundedBounds;

		public bool BorderVisibility => borderVisibility;

		public bool CanRenderLabel
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.LabelBounds.Width == 0f || base.LabelBounds.Height == 0f || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(base.Label))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (base.IsGroup)
				{
					if (base.LabelBounds.Width + (float)headerPadding.Left + (float)headerPadding.Right <= (float)roundedBounds.Width)
					{
						return base.LabelBounds.Height + (float)headerPadding.Top + (float)headerPadding.Bottom <= (float)roundedBounds.Height;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public TreeMapPadding BorderThickness => borderThickness;

		public TreeMapPadding Padding => padding;

		public DXStringAlignment LabelAlignment => labelAlignment;

		public DXStringAlignment LabelVerticalAlignment => labelVerticalAlignment;

		public TreeMapPadding HeaderPadding => headerPadding;

		public TreeMapRect Layout
		{
			get
			{
				return bounds;
			}
			set
			{
				bounds = value;
			}
		}

		double ITreeMapItemLayout.Weight => base.Item.Value;

		public TreeMapItemViewData(ITreeMapItem item, bool isRootItem)
			: base(item)
		{
			this.isRootItem = isRootItem;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<HierarchicalItemViewDataBase> CreateChildrenCollection()
		{
			return new ViewDataCollection(this);
		}

		protected override bool HitTest(Point point, out bool isInElementDiapason)
		{
			isInElementDiapason = RoundedBounds.Contains(point);
			return isInElementDiapason;
		}

		protected override Color GetLegendColor()
		{
			if (Children.Count <= 0)
			{
				return base.ActualFill;
			}
			return Children.OrderByDescending((TreeMapItemViewData a) => a.Item.Value).First().ActualFill;
		}

		private Rectangle DecreaseRectangle(Rectangle rect, TreeMapPadding correction)
		{
			int num = rect.Width - correction.Horizontal;
			int num2 = rect.Height - correction.Vertical;
			if (num <= 0 || num2 <= 0)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			return new Rectangle(rect.X + correction.Left, rect.Y + correction.Top, num, num2);
		}

		private void CalculateHeaderLayout(SizeF dpiScaleFactor)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (base.ShowLabel)
			{
				num = MathUtils.ScaleVertically(dpiScaleFactor, HeaderPadding.Vertical);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Label))
				{
					num += TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(base.DXFont).Height;
				}
			}
			headerBounds = new Rectangle(RoundedBounds.X, RoundedBounds.Y, RoundedBounds.Width, num);
		}

		private void CalculateLabelBounds(SizeF dpiScaleFactor)
		{
			if (!base.ShowLabel)
			{
				base.LabelBounds = Rectangle.Empty;
				return;
			}
			if (base.IsGroup)
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = DecreaseRectangle(headerBounds, MathUtils.ScalePadding(dpiScaleFactor, HeaderPadding));
				base.LabelBounds = TextPaintHelper.CorrectLabelBounds(rectangle, base.DXFont);
				return;
			}
			Rectangle rectangle2 = DecreaseRectangle(roundedBounds, MathUtils.ScalePadding(dpiScaleFactor, Padding));
			if ((float)rectangle2.Width < TextMeasurer.Default.GetSymbolWidth(base.DXFont, labelAlignment, labelVerticalAlignment))
			{
				rectangle2 = Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			base.LabelBounds = rectangle2;
		}

		private void RoundLayout(RectangleF availableSize)
		{
			roundedBounds = TreeMapLayoutHelper.RoundChildLayoutInternal(bounds, availableSize);
		}

		private void SetGroupBorderVisibility(DefaultBoolean borderVisible, TreeMapItemGroupStyle style)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0004: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			bool flag = (((int)borderVisible == 2) ? style.BorderVisible : ((int)borderVisible == 0));
			borderVisibility = flag && IsChildrenContainsGroup();
		}

		private bool IsChildrenContainsGroup()
		{
			foreach (ITreeMapItem child in base.Item.Children)
			{
				if (TreeMapItemHelper.IsGroupItem(child))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void CorrectBounds(PointF offset)
		{
			bounds.X += offset.X;
			bounds.Y += offset.Y;
		}

		private void SetLeafBorderVisibility(DefaultBoolean leafBorderVisible, TreeMapItemLeafStyle style)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0002: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if ((int)leafBorderVisible == 2)
			{
				borderVisibility = style.BorderVisible;
			}
			else
			{
				borderVisibility = (int)leafBorderVisible == 0;
			}
		}

		private void SetStyle(TreeMapItemLeafStyle style)
		{
			base.StateChanged = false;
			labelVerticalAlignment = (DXStringAlignment)style.LabelVerticalAlignment;
			base.GlowTextColor = style.TextGlowColor;
			padding = style.Padding;
			labelAlignment = (DXStringAlignment)style.LabelAlignment;
			base.ShowLabel = style.ShowLabel;
			base.BorderBrightnessCoeff = style.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
		}

		public void SetLeafStyle(ITreeMapAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, TreeMapItemLeafStyle style)
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			SetStyle(style);
			SetLeafBorderVisibility(appearanceProvider.LeafBorderVisible, style);
			base.Fill = style.Fill;
			base.TextColor = style.TextColor;
			base.DXFont = style.DXFont;
			padding = style.Padding;
			base.BorderColor = style.BorderColor;
		}

		public void SetGroupStyle(ITreeMapAppearanceProvider appearanceProvider, TreeMapItemGroupStyle style)
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			SetStyle(style);
			SetGroupBorderVisibility(appearanceProvider.GroupBorderVisible, style);
			base.Fill = (isRootItem ? style.Fill : style.SubGroupFill);
			base.BorderColor = (isRootItem ? style.BorderColor : style.SubGroupBorderColor);
			headerPadding = (isRootItem ? style.HeaderPadding : style.SubGroupHeaderPadding);
			base.TextColor = (isRootItem ? style.TextColor : style.SubGroupTextColor);
			base.DXFont = (isRootItem ? style.DXFont : style.DXSubGroupFont);
		}

		public Rectangle GetBorderBounds()
		{
			if (!base.IsGroup)
			{
				return roundedBounds;
			}
			return borderBounds;
		}

		private void CalculateGroupItemBounds(SizeF dpiScaleFactor)
		{
			int num = RoundedBounds.Height - headerBounds.Height;
			if (num > 0)
			{
				Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(RoundedBounds.X, headerBounds.Bottom, RoundedBounds.Width, num);
				if (borderVisibility)
				{
					borderBounds = DecreaseRectangle(rect, MathUtils.ScalePadding(dpiScaleFactor, Padding));
					boundsForChildren = DecreaseRectangle(borderBounds, MathUtils.ScalePadding(dpiScaleFactor, borderThickness));
				}
				else
				{
					boundsForChildren = DecreaseRectangle(rect, MathUtils.ScalePadding(dpiScaleFactor, Padding));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				boundsForChildren = Rectangle.Empty;
			}
		}

		public void CalculateElementsLayout(RectangleF bounds, SizeF dpiScaleFactor)
		{
			CorrectBounds(bounds.Location);
			RoundLayout(bounds);
			if (base.IsGroup)
			{
				CalculateHeaderLayout(dpiScaleFactor);
				CalculateGroupItemBounds(dpiScaleFactor);
			}
			CalculateLabelBounds(dpiScaleFactor);
		}

		public Color ApplyTransparency(BaseRenderStrategy renderStrategy, Color hatchColor)
		{
			if (base.Fill.IsEmpty)
			{
				return renderStrategy.GetColorWithTransparency(base.ActualFill, hatchColor);
			}
			return base.Fill;
		}
	}
	public class ViewDataCollection : IEnumerable<TreeMapItemViewData>, IEnumerable
	{
		private readonly List<TreeMapItemViewData> viewDataCollection = new List<TreeMapItemViewData>();

		private readonly TreeMapItemViewData parent;

		private int selectedNodeCount;

		public int Count => viewDataCollection.Count;

		public TreeMapItemViewData this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return viewDataCollection[index];
			}
			set
			{
				viewDataCollection[index] = value;
			}
		}

		public ViewDataCollection(TreeMapItemViewData parent)
		{
			this.parent = parent;
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return viewDataCollection.GetEnumerator();
		}

		private void OnSelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			selectedNodeCount += (((TreeMapItemViewData)sender).IsSelected ? 1 : (-1));
			if (parent != null)
			{
				parent.IsSelected = Count == selectedNodeCount;
			}
		}

		public void Add(TreeMapItemViewData viewData)
		{
			viewDataCollection.Add(viewData);
			if (viewData.IsSelected)
			{
				selectedNodeCount++;
			}
			else if (parent != null)
			{
				parent.IsSelected = false;
			}
			viewData.SelectionChanged += OnSelectionChanged;
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData item in viewDataCollection)
			{
				item.SelectionChanged -= OnSelectionChanged;
			}
			viewDataCollection.Clear();
		}

		public IEnumerator<TreeMapItemViewData> GetEnumerator()
		{
			return viewDataCollection.GetEnumerator();
		}
	}
	public class SunburstView : ControlViewBase, ISunBurstView, IHierarchicalView, ICenterLabelPatternValueHolder
	{
		private const string EmptySunburstMessage = "There is no data to display in a sunburst.\nSpecify a new data adapter or make sure\nthat the data adapter is configured correctly,\nand data is loaded.";

		private const int DoublePi = 360;

		private const int StartNestingIndex = 1;

		private int maxGroupLevel = 1;

		private readonly SunburstCenterLabelViewData centerLabelViewData;

		private readonly SunburstViewDataCollection itemsViewData;

		private readonly ISunburstAppearanceProvider appearance;

		private Point center;

		private double totalValue;

		private float groupWidth;

		private bool CanRenderCenterLabel
		{
			get
			{
				if (appearance.ShowCenterLabel)
				{
					return centerLabelViewData.CanRender;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsClockwiseDirection => appearance.SweepDirection == SunburstSweepDirection.Clockwise;

		protected override IAppearanceProvider AppearanceProvider => appearance;

		protected override string EmptyDataMessage => "There is no data to display in a sunburst.\nSpecify a new data adapter or make sure\nthat the data adapter is configured correctly,\nand data is loaded.";

		internal SunburstCenterLabelViewData CenterLabelViewData => centerLabelViewData;

		protected override IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> InteractiveItems => itemsViewData;

		protected override IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestableItems
		{
			get
			{
				yield return centerLabelViewData;
				foreach (SunburstItemViewData itemsViewDatum in itemsViewData)
				{
					yield return itemsViewDatum;
				}
			}
		}

		public SunburstViewDataCollection Items => itemsViewData;

		double ICenterLabelPatternValueHolder.TotalValue => totalValue;

		public SunburstView(IRenderer renderer, ISunburstAppearanceProvider appearance)
			: base(renderer)
		{
			this.appearance = appearance;
			itemsViewData = new SunburstViewDataCollection(null);
			centerLabelViewData = new SunburstCenterLabelViewData(appearance);
		}

		private void RenderItems(SunburstViewDataCollection items, RectangleF bounds)
		{
			foreach (SunburstItemViewData item in items)
			{
				RenderItem(item, useHighlighting: false);
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					RenderItems(item.Children, bounds);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RenderItem(SunburstItemViewData item, bool useHighlighting)
		{
			if (!item.CanRender)
			{
				return;
			}
			DXGraphicsPath path = GetPath(item);
			if (useHighlighting)
			{
				if (item.IsSelected)
				{
					base.Renderer.FillPath(path, appearance.SelectedItemHatch.HatchStyle, item.ActualFill, appearance.SelectedItemHatch.HatchColor);
				}
				else if (item.IsHighlighted)
				{
					base.Renderer.FillPath(path, appearance.HighlightedItemHatch.HatchStyle, item.ActualFill, appearance.HighlightedItemHatch.HatchColor);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Renderer.FillPath(item.ActualFill, path);
			}
			using (DXPen pen = new DXPen(ObtainBorderColor(item)))
			{
				base.Renderer.DrawPath(pen, path);
			}
			if (item.CanRenderLabel)
			{
				if (item.LabelAngle != 0f)
				{
					base.Renderer.DrawGlowTextRotated(item.Label, item.TextColor, item.GlowTextColor, item.DXFont, item.LabelBounds, DXStringAlignment.Center, 2.8f, item.LabelAngle);
				}
				else
				{
					RenderHorizontalLabel(item, DXStringAlignment.Center, DXStringAlignment.Center, useEllipsis: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private DXGraphicsPath GetPath(SunburstItemViewData item)
		{
			DXGraphicsPath dXGraphicsPath = new DXGraphicsPath();
			Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(center.X, center.Y, 0, 0);
			Rectangle rectangle = Rectangle.Inflate(rect, Convert.ToInt32(item.Radius), Convert.ToInt32(item.Radius));
			if (rectangle.Width <= 0 || rectangle.Height <= 0)
			{
				return dXGraphicsPath;
			}
			if (item.HoleRadius >= 1f)
			{
				Rectangle rectangle2 = Rectangle.Inflate(rect, Convert.ToInt32(item.HoleRadius), Convert.ToInt32(item.HoleRadius));
				dXGraphicsPath.AddArc(rectangle, item.StartAngle, item.SweepAngle);
				dXGraphicsPath.AddArc(rectangle2, item.StartAngle + item.SweepAngle, 0f - item.SweepAngle);
			}
			else
			{
				dXGraphicsPath.AddPie(rectangle, item.StartAngle, item.SweepAngle);
			}
			dXGraphicsPath.CloseFigure();
			return dXGraphicsPath;
		}

		private void ApplyItemAppearance(SunburstItemViewData itemSunburst)
		{
			SunburstItem sunburstItem = itemSunburst.Item as SunburstItem;
			sunburstItem?.ApplyStyle(appearance.DefaultStyleProvider.DefaultStyle, appearance.ItemStyle);
			itemSunburst.SetStyle(appearance.ItemStyle);
			ILabelFormatter labelFormatter = ((InnerSunburst)sunburstItem.Owner)?.LabelFormatter;
			if (labelFormatter == null)
			{
				itemSunburst.UpdateLabel(GetPatternParser(appearance.LeafTextPattern));
			}
			else
			{
				itemSunburst.UpdateLabel(labelFormatter);
			}
		}

		private void FillItemsViewData(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> source, SunburstViewDataCollection target, int groupLevel)
		{
			if (appearance.MaxVisibleLevel > 0 && groupLevel > appearance.MaxVisibleLevel)
			{
				return;
			}
			maxGroupLevel = Math.Max(maxGroupLevel, groupLevel);
			foreach (ISunburstItem item in source)
			{
				SunburstItemViewData sunburstItemViewData = new SunburstItemViewData(item);
				ApplyItemAppearance(sunburstItemViewData);
				if (item.Children.Count > 0)
				{
					FillItemsViewData(item.Children, sunburstItemViewData.Children, groupLevel + 1);
				}
				target.Add(sunburstItemViewData);
				if (groupLevel == 1)
				{
					base.ItemsForLegend.Add(sunburstItemViewData);
				}
			}
			target.OrderByDescending();
		}

		private void ApplyAppearanceInternal(SunburstViewDataCollection items)
		{
			foreach (SunburstItemViewData item in items)
			{
				ApplyItemAppearance(item);
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					ApplyAppearanceInternal(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RenderHighlightedItems(SunburstViewDataCollection items)
		{
			foreach (SunburstItemViewData item in items)
			{
				if (item.IsHighlighted || item.IsSelected)
				{
					RenderItem(item, useHighlighting: true);
				}
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					RenderHighlightedItems(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		private void CalculateItemsLayout(SunburstViewDataCollection items, double itemsSum, double parentValue, float startAngle, float finishAngle, float holeRadius)
		{
			if (itemsSum <= 0.0)
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = (double)(finishAngle - startAngle) * Math.Min(itemsSum / parentValue, 1.0);
			float num2 = (IsClockwiseDirection ? startAngle : 0f);
			foreach (SunburstItemViewData item in items)
			{
				float num3 = (float)Math.Abs(item.Item.Value / itemsSum * num);
				if (num3 > 360f)
				{
					num3 %= 360f;
				}
				float num4 = (IsClockwiseDirection ? (num2 % 360f) : ((finishAngle - num3 - num2) % 360f));
				item.UpdateLayout(center, holeRadius, groupWidth, num4, num3, appearance.Label);
				num2 += num3;
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					CalculateItemsLayout(item.Children, item.Item.Children.Sum((IHierarchicalItem i) => i.Value), item.Item.Value, num4, num4 + num3, item.Radius);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ApplyCenterLabelAppearance(SunburstDefaultStyleProvider styleProvider)
		{
			centerLabelViewData.ApplyStyle(styleProvider);
			centerLabelViewData.CalculateLabelText(this, appearance.CenterLabel.TextPattern);
		}

		private void RenderCenterLabel()
		{
			base.Renderer.FillEllipse(centerLabelViewData.LabelBounds, centerLabelViewData.BackgroundColor);
			DXTextRenderingHint textRenderingHint = CalcTextRenderingHint(null, centerLabelViewData.BackgroundColor);
			base.Renderer.DrawBoundedText(centerLabelViewData.LabelText, centerLabelViewData.TextFont, centerLabelViewData.ForegroundColor, centerLabelViewData.LabelBounds, DXStringAlignment.Center, DXStringAlignment.Center, textRenderingHint);
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateItems(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> items)
		{
			maxGroupLevel = 1;
			itemsViewData.Clear();
			base.ItemsForLegend.Clear();
			if (items != null)
			{
				totalValue = items.Sum((IHierarchicalItem i) => i.Value);
				FillItemsViewData(items, itemsViewData, 1);
			}
			centerLabelViewData.CalculateLabelText(this, appearance.CenterLabel.TextPattern);
		}

		protected override void RenderContent()
		{
			if (itemsViewData.Count > 0)
			{
				if (CanRenderCenterLabel)
				{
					RenderCenterLabel();
				}
				RenderItems(itemsViewData, base.ContentRect);
			}
		}

		protected override void RenderHighlightedItems()
		{
			RenderHighlightedItems(itemsViewData);
		}

		protected override Rectangle CalculateLayoutCore(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			bounds = base.CalculateLayoutCore(dataProvider, bounds);
			if (base.ContentRect != bounds)
			{
				center = new Point(bounds.X + bounds.Width / 2, bounds.Y + bounds.Height / 2);
				int num = ((bounds.Height > bounds.Width) ? (bounds.Width / 2) : (bounds.Height / 2));
				float num2 = (float)((double)(num * appearance.HoleRadiusPercent) / 100.0);
				centerLabelViewData.UpdateLayout(num2, center);
				groupWidth = ((float)num - num2) / (float)maxGroupLevel;
				float num3;
				for (num3 = (float)appearance.StartAngle; num3 < 0f; num3 += 360f)
				{
				}
				CalculateItemsLayout(itemsViewData, totalValue, totalValue, num3, num3 + 360f, num2);
			}
			return bounds;
		}

		protected override void ApplyAppearance()
		{
			ApplyCenterLabelAppearance(appearance.DefaultStyleProvider);
			ApplyAppearanceInternal(itemsViewData);
		}

		protected override void ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController)
		{
			colorizerController.ColorizeItems(itemsViewData, maxGroupLevel);
		}

		internal Color ObtainBorderColor(HierarchicalItemViewDataBase item)
		{
			if (appearance.ItemStyle.BorderBrightnessCoeff == 1f)
			{
				return GraphicUtils.ApplyTransparency(item.ActualFill, item.BorderColor);
			}
			Color actualFill = item.ActualFill;
			float borderBrightnessCoeff = appearance.ItemStyle.BorderBrightnessCoeff;
			return Color.FromArgb(actualFill.A, (int)Math.Min(255f, (float)(int)actualFill.R * borderBrightnessCoeff), (int)Math.Min(255f, (float)(int)actualFill.G * borderBrightnessCoeff), (int)Math.Min(255f, (float)(int)actualFill.B * borderBrightnessCoeff));
		}
	}
	public class SunburstDefaultStyleProvider : ISunburstDefaultStyleProvider
	{
		private readonly SunburstItemStyle defaultStyle;

		private readonly HierarchicalItemStyle defaultCenterLabelStyle;

		public SunburstItemStyle DefaultStyle => defaultStyle;

		public HierarchicalItemStyle DefaultCenterLabelStyle => defaultCenterLabelStyle;

		public SunburstDefaultStyleProvider()
		{
			defaultStyle = new SunburstItemStyle();
			defaultCenterLabelStyle = new SunburstItemStyle();
		}
	}
	public interface ILayoutAlgorithmProvider
	{
		ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm LayoutAlgorithm { get; set; }
	}

		IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestableItems { get; }

		IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> LegendItemsSourceHolder { get; }

		bool AllowAntiAliasing { get; }

		void ResetInnerBounds();

		void UpdateItems(IViewDataProvider dataProvider);

		void Render(Rectangle bounds);

		void RenderHighlightedItems();

		void CalculateLayout(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds);

		void ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController);

		void RenderDesignModeNoDataPresentation(Rectangle bounds);

		void UpdateStrategy(bool drawToMetaFile);
	}
	public interface IViewDataProvider
	{
		IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> HierarchicalItems { get; }

		TitleCollection Titles { get; }

		LegendCollection Legends { get; }

		IList<ILegendItem> ObtainLegendItems(LegendBase legend);
	}
	public interface ITreeMapView : IHierarchicalView
	{
		ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm LayoutAlgorithm { get; set; }

		ViewDataCollection Items { get; }
	}
	public interface ISunBurstView : IHierarchicalView
	{
