	}
	public abstract class ControlViewBase : IHierarchicalView, IPatternHolder
	{
		private const int minFontSize = 2;

		private const int maxFontSize = 11;

		private readonly Dictionary<string, PatternParser> patternParserCache = new Dictionary<string, PatternParser>();

		private BaseRenderStrategy strategy;

		internal List<ViewDataBase> OuterLayoutElementViewData { get; private set; }

		internal List<ViewDataBase> OverlayLayoutElementViewData { get; private set; }

		protected List<ILegendSourceItem> ItemsForLegend { get; } = new List<ILegendSourceItem>();

		protected abstract IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestableItems { get; }

		protected abstract IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> InteractiveItems { get; }

		protected abstract string EmptyDataMessage { get; }

		protected abstract IAppearanceProvider AppearanceProvider { get; }

		protected virtual bool AllowAntiAliasing => true;

		protected BaseRenderStrategy RenderStrategy => strategy;

		protected IRenderer Renderer { get; }

		protected Rectangle ContentRect { get; set; }

		IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> IHierarchicalView.HitTestableItems => HitTestableItems;

		IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> IHierarchicalView.InteractiveItems => InteractiveItems;

		bool IHierarchicalView.AllowAntiAliasing => AllowAntiAliasing;

		IEnumerable<ILegendSourceItem> IHierarchicalView.LegendItemsSourceHolder => ItemsForLegend;

		protected ControlViewBase(IRenderer renderer)
		{
			Renderer = renderer;
			strategy = new StandardRenderStrategy();
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.Render(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			if (bounds.Width > 0 && bounds.Height > 0)
			{
				RenderCore(bounds);
			}
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.ResetInnerBounds()
		{
			ContentRect = Rectangle.Empty;
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.ApplyAppearance()
		{
			ApplyAppearance();
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.CalculateLayout(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			CalculateLayout(dataProvider, bounds);
		}

		private void CalculateLayout(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			ContentRect = CalculateLayoutCore(dataProvider, bounds);
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.UpdateItems(IViewDataProvider dataProvider)
		{
			UpdateItems(dataProvider.HierarchicalItems);
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.RenderHighlightedItems()
		{
			RenderHighlightedItems();
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController)
		{
			ColorizeItems(colorizerController);
		}

		void IHierarchicalView.RenderDesignModeNoDataPresentation(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			RenderDesignModeNoDataPresentation(EmptyDataMessage, bounds, AppearanceProvider.BackColor, AppearanceProvider.BorderOptions);
		}

		PatternDataProvider IPatternHolder.GetDataProvider(PatternConstants patternConstant)
		{
			return new TreeMapItemPatternProvider(patternConstant);
		}

		protected void RenderHorizontalLabel(HierarchicalItemViewDataBase itemViewData, DXStringAlignment horizontalAlignment, DXStringAlignment verticalAlignment, bool useEllipsis)
		{
			RectangleF labelBounds = itemViewData.LabelBounds;
			DXFont dXFont = TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(itemViewData.DXFont);
			List<string> list = TextPaintHelper.FormatMultilineText(labelBounds, dXFont, itemViewData.Label, useEllipsis);
			float firstLineOffset = GetFirstLineOffset(labelBounds, dXFont.Height, list.Count, verticalAlignment);
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				float num = firstLineOffset + (float)(i * dXFont.Height);
				RectangleF bounds = new RectangleF(labelBounds.X, labelBounds.Y + num, labelBounds.Width, dXFont.Height);
				Renderer.DrawGlowText(list[i], itemViewData.TextColor, itemViewData.GlowTextColor, itemViewData.DXFont, bounds, horizontalAlignment, verticalAlignment, 2.8f);
			}
		}

		private float GetFirstLineOffset(RectangleF labelBounds, int fontHeight, int lineCount, DXStringAlignment verticalAlignment)
		{
			if (verticalAlignment == DXStringAlignment.Near)
			{
				return 0f;
			}
			int num = fontHeight * lineCount;
			float num2 = labelBounds.Height - (float)num;
			if (num2 <= 0f)
			{
				return 0f;
			}
			if (verticalAlignment != DXStringAlignment.Center)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			return num2 / 2f;
		}

		private void RenderDesignModeNoDataPresentation(string message, Rectangle bounds, Color backColor, RectangularBorder borderOptions)
		{
			FillBackgroundAndRenderBorder(bounds, backColor, borderOptions);
			float num = GraphicUtils.CalcFontEmSize(TextMeasurer.Default, bounds, message, DefaultFonts.FontName, DXStringAlignment.Center, DXStringAlignment.Near);
			if (!(num < 2f))
			{
				if (num > 11f)
				{
					num = 11f;
				}
				DXFont font = new DXFont(DefaultFonts.FontName, num);
				DXStringAlignment dXStringAlignment = DXStringAlignment.Center;
				SizeF sizeF = TextMeasurer.Default.MeasureString(message, font, dXStringAlignment, dXStringAlignment);
				RectangleF bounds2 = new RectangleF(bounds.X, (float)(bounds.Y + bounds.Height / 2) - sizeF.Height / 2f, bounds.Width, sizeF.Height);
				Renderer.DrawBoundedText(message, font, GraphicUtils.XorColor(backColor), bounds2, dXStringAlignment, dXStringAlignment, DXTextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit);
			}
		}

		private void DrawOuterLayoutElements()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < OuterLayoutElementViewData?.Count; i++)
			{
				OuterLayoutElementViewData[i].Render(Renderer);
			}
		}

		private void DrawOverlayLayoutElements()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < OverlayLayoutElementViewData?.Count; i++)
			{
				OverlayLayoutElementViewData[i].Render(Renderer);
			}
		}

		private void RenderCore(Rectangle bounds)
		{
			FillBackgroundAndRenderBorder(bounds, AppearanceProvider.BackColor, AppearanceProvider.BorderOptions);
			DrawOuterLayoutElements();
			RenderContent();
			DrawOverlayLayoutElements();
		}

		private void FillBackgroundAndRenderBorder(Rectangle bounds, Color backColor, RectangularBorder border)
		{
			Renderer.SetSmoothingMode(DXSmoothingMode.None);
			if (backColor != Color.Empty)
			{
				Renderer.FillRectangle(bounds, backColor);
			}
			BorderHelper.RenderBorder(Renderer, border, bounds);
			Renderer.RestoreSmoothingMode();
		}

		protected Rectangle DecreaseRectangle(Rectangle bounds, TreeMapPadding padding)
		{
			int width = Math.Max(bounds.Width - padding.Left - padding.Right, 0);
			int height = Math.Max(bounds.Height - padding.Top - padding.Bottom, 0);
			return new Rectangle(bounds.X + padding.Left, bounds.Y + padding.Top, width, height);
		}

		protected DXTextRenderingHint CalcTextRenderingHint(FillStyleBase fillStyle, Color backColor)
		{
			if (fillStyle is HatchFillStyle hatchFillStyle)
			{
				if (backColor.A >= byte.MaxValue && hatchFillStyle.Color2.A >= byte.MaxValue)
				{
					return DXTextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;
				}
				return DXTextRenderingHint.AntiAlias;
			}
			if (backColor.A >= byte.MaxValue)
			{
				return DXTextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;
			}
			return DXTextRenderingHint.AntiAlias;
		}

		protected PatternParser GetPatternParser(string textPattern)
		{
			if (!patternParserCache.TryGetValue(textPattern, out var value))
			{
				value = new PatternParser(textPattern, this);
				patternParserCache[textPattern] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected abstract void ApplyAppearance();

		protected internal abstract void UpdateItems(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> items);

		protected abstract void RenderHighlightedItems();

		protected abstract void ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController);

		protected abstract void RenderContent();

		protected virtual Rectangle CalculateLayoutCore(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			bounds = DecreaseRectangle(AppearanceProvider.BorderOptions.CalcBorderedRect(bounds), MathUtils.ScalePadding(AppearanceProvider.DpiScaleFactor, AppearanceProvider.Padding));
			bounds = CalculateLayoutElementsViewDataAndBounds(dataProvider, bounds);
			return bounds;
		}

		internal Rectangle CalculateLayoutElementsViewDataAndBounds(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			IList<Title> list = new List<Title>();
			IList<Title> list2 = new List<Title>();
			for (int i = 0; i < ((DXCollectionBase<Title>)(object)dataProvider.Titles).Count; i++)
			{
				Title title = ((DXCollection<Title>)(object)dataProvider.Titles)[i];
				if (title.Visible)
				{
					if (title.IsOverlay)
					{
						list2.Add(title);
					}
					else
					{
						list.Add(title);
					}
				}
			}
			Dictionary<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>> dictionary = new Dictionary<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>>();
			Dictionary<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<LegendBase, IList<ILegendItem>>();
			for (int j = 0; j < ((DXCollectionBase<LegendBase>)(object)dataProvider.Legends).Count; j++)
			{
				LegendBase legendBase = ((DXCollection<LegendBase>)(object)dataProvider.Legends)[j];
				if (legendBase.Visible)
				{
					if (legendBase.IsOutside)
					{
						dictionary.Add(legendBase, dataProvider.ObtainLegendItems(legendBase));
					}
					else
					{
						dictionary2.Add(legendBase, dataProvider.ObtainLegendItems(legendBase));
					}
				}
			}
			Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle> tuple = TitleViewDataCalculator.CalculateViewData(list, AppearanceProvider, bounds);
			OuterLayoutElementViewData = tuple.Item1;
			Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle> tuple2 = LegendViewDataCalculator.CalculateViewData(dictionary, AppearanceProvider, tuple.Item2);
			OuterLayoutElementViewData.AddRange(tuple2.Item1);
			Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle> tuple3 = TitleViewDataCalculator.CalculateViewData(list2, AppearanceProvider, tuple2.Item2);
			OverlayLayoutElementViewData = tuple3.Item1;
			Tuple<List<ViewDataBase>, Rectangle> tuple4 = LegendViewDataCalculator.CalculateViewData(dictionary2, AppearanceProvider, tuple2.Item2);
			OverlayLayoutElementViewData.AddRange(tuple4.Item1);
			return tuple2.Item2;
		}

		public void UpdateStrategy(bool drawToMetaFile)
		{
			if (drawToMetaFile)
			{
				strategy = new MetaFileRenderStrategy();
			}
			else
			{
				strategy = new StandardRenderStrategy();
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapView : ControlViewBase, ITreeMapView, IHierarchicalView
	{
		internal const string EmptyTreeMapMessage = "There is no data to display in a tree map.\nSpecify a new data adapter or make sure\nthat the data adapter is configured correctly,\nand data is loaded.";

		private readonly ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm DefaultLayoutAlgorithm = new TreeMapSquarifiedLayoutAlgorithm();

		private readonly ITreeMapAppearanceProvider appearance;

		private readonly ViewDataCollection itemsViewData;

		private ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm ActualLayoutAlgorithm => LayoutAlgorithm ?? DefaultLayoutAlgorithm;

		protected override string EmptyDataMessage => "There is no data to display in a tree map.\nSpecify a new data adapter or make sure\nthat the data adapter is configured correctly,\nand data is loaded.";

		protected override IAppearanceProvider AppearanceProvider => appearance;

		protected override bool AllowAntiAliasing => false;

		protected override IEnumerable<IHitTestableElement> HitTestableItems => itemsViewData;

		protected override IEnumerable<IInteractiveElement> InteractiveItems => itemsViewData;

		public ViewDataCollection Items => itemsViewData;

		public ITreeMapLayoutAlgorithm LayoutAlgorithm { get; set; }

		public TreeMapView(IRenderer renderer, ITreeMapAppearanceProvider appearance)
			: base(renderer)
		{
			this.appearance = appearance;
			itemsViewData = new ViewDataCollection(null);
		}

		private void RenderItemBorder(TreeMapItemViewData viewData)
		{
			base.Renderer.DrawRectangle(viewData.GetBorderBounds(), base.RenderStrategy.GetColorWithTransparency(viewData.ActualFill, viewData.BorderColor), viewData.BorderThickness.Top);
		}

		private void RenderItems(ViewDataCollection items)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData item in items)
			{
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					RenderGroup(item);
				}
				else
				{
					RenderLeaf(item, base.RenderStrategy.GetUseHighlighting(useHighlighting: false));
				}
			}
		}

		private void RenderGroup(TreeMapItemViewData itemViewData)
		{
			appearance.GroupStyle.FillStyle.Render(base.Renderer, itemViewData.RoundedBounds, itemViewData.ActualFill);
			if (itemViewData.BorderVisibility)
			{
				RenderItemBorder(itemViewData);
			}
			RenderGroupHeader(itemViewData);
			RenderItems(itemViewData.Children);
		}

		private void RenderGroupHeader(TreeMapItemViewData itemViewData)
		{
			if (itemViewData.CanRenderLabel)
			{
				DXFont font = TextMeasurer.Default.ScaleFont(itemViewData.DXFont);
				string text = TextPaintHelper.FormatSinglelineText(itemViewData.LabelBounds, font, itemViewData.Label);
				DXTextRenderingHint textRenderingHint = CalcTextRenderingHint(appearance.GroupStyle.FillStyle, itemViewData.ActualFill);
				base.Renderer.DrawBoundedText(text, itemViewData.DXFont, itemViewData.TextColor, itemViewData.LabelBounds, itemViewData.LabelAlignment, DXStringAlignment.Center, textRenderingHint);
			}
		}

		private void RenderLeaf(TreeMapItemViewData itemViewData, bool useHighlighting)
		{
			if (!(itemViewData.Layout.Width > 0.0) || !(itemViewData.Layout.Height > 0.0))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (itemViewData.StateChanged)
			{
				ApplyItemAppearance(itemViewData);
			}
			appearance.LeafStyle.FillStyle.Render(base.Renderer, itemViewData.RoundedBounds, itemViewData.ActualFill);
			if (useHighlighting)
			{
				if (itemViewData.IsSelected)
				{
					appearance.SelectedLeafStyle.FillStyle.Render(base.Renderer, itemViewData.RoundedBounds, itemViewData.ApplyTransparency(base.RenderStrategy, appearance.SelectedItemHatch.HatchColor));
				}
				else if (itemViewData.IsHighlighted)
				{
					appearance.HighlightedLeafStyle.FillStyle.Render(base.Renderer, itemViewData.RoundedBounds, itemViewData.ApplyTransparency(base.RenderStrategy, appearance.HighlightedItemHatch.HatchColor));
				}
			}
			if (itemViewData.BorderVisibility)
			{
				RenderItemBorder(itemViewData);
			}
			if (itemViewData.CanRenderLabel)
			{
				RenderHorizontalLabel(itemViewData, itemViewData.LabelAlignment, itemViewData.LabelVerticalAlignment, useEllipsis: true);
			}
		}

		private void ApplyItemAppearance(TreeMapItemViewData itemViewData)
		{
			TreeMapItemStyleProvider defaultStyleProvider = appearance.DefaultStyleProvider;
			TreeMapItem treeMapItem = itemViewData.Item as TreeMapItem;
			if (itemViewData.IsGroup)
			{
				TreeMapItemGroupStyle defaultGroupStyle = defaultStyleProvider.DefaultGroupStyle;
				treeMapItem?.ApplyStyle(defaultGroupStyle, appearance.GroupStyle);
				itemViewData.SetGroupStyle(appearance, defaultGroupStyle);
				return;
			}
			TreeMapItemLeafStyle defaultLeafStyle = defaultStyleProvider.DefaultLeafStyle;
			if (treeMapItem != null)
			{
				treeMapItem.ApplyStyle(defaultLeafStyle, appearance.LeafStyle);
				if (itemViewData.IsSelected)
				{
					treeMapItem.ApplyStyle(defaultLeafStyle, defaultStyleProvider.DefaultSelectedLeafStyle);
					treeMapItem.ApplyStyle(defaultLeafStyle, appearance.SelectedLeafStyle);
				}
				else if (itemViewData.IsHighlighted)
				{
					treeMapItem.ApplyStyle(defaultLeafStyle, defaultStyleProvider.DefaultHighlightedLeafStyle);
					treeMapItem.ApplyStyle(defaultLeafStyle, appearance.HighlightedLeafStyle);
				}
			}
			itemViewData.SetLeafStyle(appearance, defaultLeafStyle);
		}

		private void FillItemsViewData(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> source, ViewDataCollection target, int groupLevel, List<KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem>> legendItems)
		{
			int num = groupLevel + 1;
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (TreeMapItem item in source)
			{
				TreeMapItemViewData treeMapItemViewData = new TreeMapItemViewData(item, groupLevel == 0);
				if (TreeMapItemHelper.IsGroupItem(item) && (appearance.MaxVisibleLevel == 0 || num < appearance.MaxVisibleLevel))
				{
					flag = ((DXCollectionBase<TreeMapItem>)(object)item.Children).Find((Predicate<TreeMapItem>)((TreeMapItem m) => ((DXCollectionBase<TreeMapItem>)(object)m.Children).Count > 0)) == null;
					FillItemsViewData(item.Children, treeMapItemViewData.Children, num, legendItems);
				}
				ApplyItemAppearance(treeMapItemViewData);
				ILabelFormatter labelFormatter = ((InnerTreeMap)item.Owner)?.LabelFormatter;
				if (labelFormatter != null)
				{
					treeMapItemViewData.UpdateLabel(labelFormatter);
				}
				else
				{
					treeMapItemViewData.UpdateLabel(GetPatternParser(item.IsGroup ? appearance.GroupTextPattern : appearance.LeafTextPattern));
				}
				target.Add(treeMapItemViewData);
				if (flag)
				{
					int indexInSource = ((TreeMapItem)treeMapItemViewData.Item).IndexInSource;
					legendItems.Add(new KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem>(indexInSource, treeMapItemViewData));
					flag = false;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ResetItemsLayout(ViewDataCollection itemsViewData)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData itemsViewDatum in itemsViewData)
			{
				itemsViewDatum.Layout = TreeMapRect.Empty;
				if (itemsViewDatum.IsGroup)
				{
					ResetItemsLayout(itemsViewDatum.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		private void CalculateLayout(RectangleF bounds, ViewDataCollection itemsViewData, int correction, int groupLevel)
		{
			if (bounds.Width == 0f || bounds.Height == 0f || itemsViewData.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			ActualLayoutAlgorithm.Calculate(itemsViewData, bounds.Width - (float)correction, bounds.Height - (float)correction, groupLevel);
			groupLevel++;
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData itemsViewDatum in itemsViewData)
			{
				itemsViewDatum.CalculateElementsLayout(bounds, appearance.DpiScaleFactor);
				if (itemsViewDatum.IsGroup)
				{
					int availableSizeCorrection = GetAvailableSizeCorrection(itemsViewDatum.Children);
					if (itemsViewDatum.BoundsForChildren != RectangleF.Empty)
					{
						CalculateLayout(itemsViewDatum.BoundsForChildren, itemsViewDatum.Children, availableSizeCorrection, groupLevel);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private int GetAvailableSizeCorrection(ViewDataCollection itemsViewData)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData itemsViewDatum in itemsViewData)
			{
				if (!itemsViewDatum.IsGroup && itemsViewDatum.BorderVisibility)
				{
					return 1;
				}
			}
			return 0;
		}

		private void ApplyAppearanceInternal(ViewDataCollection items)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData item in items)
			{
				ApplyItemAppearance(item);
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					ApplyAppearanceInternal(item.Children);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RenderHighlightedItems(ViewDataCollection items)
		{
			foreach (TreeMapItemViewData item in items)
			{
				if (item.IsGroup)
				{
					RenderHighlightedItems(item.Children);
				}
				else if (item.IsHighlighted || item.IsSelected)
				{
					RenderLeaf(item, base.RenderStrategy.GetUseHighlighting(useHighlighting: true));
				}
			}
		}

		private void PrepareLegendItemsSource(List<KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem>> legendItems)
		{
			legendItems.Sort((KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem> a, KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem> b) => a.Key.CompareTo(b.Key));
			base.ItemsForLegend.Clear();
			for (int num = 0; num < legendItems.Count; num++)
			{
				base.ItemsForLegend.Add(legendItems[num].Value);
			}
		}

		protected override void RenderContent()
		{
			RenderItems(itemsViewData);
		}

		protected override void RenderHighlightedItems()
		{
			RenderHighlightedItems(itemsViewData);
		}

		protected override Rectangle CalculateLayoutCore(IViewDataProvider dataProvider, Rectangle bounds)
		{
			bounds = base.CalculateLayoutCore(dataProvider, bounds);
			if (base.ContentRect != bounds)
			{
				ResetItemsLayout(itemsViewData);
				int availableSizeCorrection = GetAvailableSizeCorrection(itemsViewData);
				CalculateLayout(bounds, itemsViewData, availableSizeCorrection, 0);
			}
			return bounds;
		}

		protected override void ColorizeItems(IColorizerController colorizerController)
		{
			colorizerController.ColorizeItems(itemsViewData, 0);
		}

		protected override void ApplyAppearance()
		{
			ApplyAppearanceInternal(itemsViewData);
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateItems(IEnumerable<IHierarchicalItem> items)
		{
			itemsViewData.Clear();
			List<KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem>> legendItems = new List<KeyValuePair<int, ILegendSourceItem>>();
			if (items != null)
			{
				FillItemsViewData(items, itemsViewData, 0, legendItems);
			}
			PrepareLegendItemsSource(legendItems);
		}
	}
	public abstract class BaseRenderStrategy
	{
		public abstract bool GetUseHighlighting(bool useHighlighting);

		public abstract Color GetColorWithTransparency(Color backColor, Color forwardColor);
	}
	public class StandardRenderStrategy : BaseRenderStrategy
	{
		public override bool GetUseHighlighting(bool useHighlighting)
		{
			return useHighlighting;
		}

		public override Color GetColorWithTransparency(Color backColor, Color forwardColor)
		{
			return forwardColor;
		}
	}
	public class MetaFileRenderStrategy : BaseRenderStrategy
	{
		public override bool GetUseHighlighting(bool useHighlighting)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public override Color GetColorWithTransparency(Color backColor, Color forwardColor)
		{
			return GraphicUtils.ApplyTransparency(backColor, forwardColor);
		}
	}
	public static class XtraSerializingUtils
	{
		private const string typeNamePropertyName = "TypeNameSerializable";

		public static object GetKnownContentPropertyInstance(XtraPropertyInfo propertyInfo, Assembly sourceAssembly, string typeNamespace)
		{
			string text = propertyInfo.ChildProperties["TypeNameSerializable"].Value.ToString();
			Type knownType = SafeTypeResolver.GetKnownType(sourceAssembly, typeNamespace + text, false, (bool?)null);
			return Activator.CreateInstance(knownType);
		}

		public static object GetKnownContentPropertyInstance(XtraItemEventArgs e, Assembly sourceAssembly, string typeNamespace)
		{
			return GetKnownContentPropertyInstance(e.Item, sourceAssembly, typeNamespace);
		}
	}
	public static class SerializationUtils
	{
		public static Assembly TreeMapAssembly => typeof(SerializationUtils).Assembly;

		public static string PublicNamespace => "DevExpress.XtraTreeMap.";

		public static void ConvertFontToDXFont(IXtraPropertyCollection properties, string fontPropertyName)
		{
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			XtraPropertyInfo val = properties[fontPropertyName];
			if (val != null)
			{
				properties.Add(new XtraPropertyInfo("DX" + fontPropertyName, val.PropertyType, val.Value));
			}
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapXmlSerializer : XmlXtraSerializer
	{
		private sealed class ImageConverter : IOneTypeObjectConverter
		{
			public static readonly ImageConverter Instance = new ImageConverter();

			public Type Type => typeof(Image);

			private ImageConverter()
			{
			}

			public string ToString(object obj)
			{
				return null;
			}

			public object FromString(string str)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		private sealed class PaletteConverter : IOneTypeObjectConverter
		{
			private const char splitter = ',';

			public static readonly PaletteConverter Instance = new PaletteConverter();

			public Type Type => typeof(Palette);

			private PaletteConverter()
			{
			}

			public string ToString(object obj)
			{
				Palette palette = (Palette)obj;
				string text = ((object)palette).ToString();
				if (!palette.IsPredefined)
				{
					foreach (Color item in (DXCollectionBase<Color>)(object)palette)
					{
						text = text + "," + ColorTranslator.ToHtml(item);
					}
				}
				return text;
			}

			public object FromString(string str)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(str))
				{
					string[] array = str.Split(',');
					if (array.Length != 0)
					{
						Palette palette = Palettes.GetPalette(array[0]);
						if (palette == null)
						{
							palette = new Palette();
							for (int i = 1; i < array.Length; i++)
							{
								palette.Add(ColorTranslator.FromHtml(array[i]));
							}
						}
						return palette;
					}
				}
				return new Palette();
			}
		}

		private readonly string serializerName;

		private readonly bool serializeVersion = true;

		private const string collectionItemNamePrefix = "Item";

		private const string serializer = "XmlSerializer";

		protected override string SerializerName => serializerName;

		protected override string Version
		{
			get
			{
				if (!serializeVersion)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return "23.2.4.0";
			}
		}

		public TreeMapXmlSerializer(string controlName)
			: this(serializeVersion: true, controlName)
		{
		}

		public TreeMapXmlSerializer(bool serializeVersion, string controlName)
		{
			this.serializeVersion = serializeVersion;
			serializerName = controlName + "XmlSerializer";
		}

		protected override ObjectConverterImplementation GetDefaultObjectConverterImplementation()
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ObjectConverterImplementation val = new ObjectConverterImplementation();
			val.RegisterConverter((IOneTypeObjectConverter)(object)ImageConverter.Instance);
			val.RegisterConverter((IOneTypeObjectConverter)(object)PaletteConverter.Instance);
			ObjectConverter.Instance.CopyConvertersTo(val);
			return val;
		}

		private static bool IsCollectionItem(string name)
		{
			if (!name.StartsWith("Item"))
			{
				return false;
			}
			int result;
			return int.TryParse(name.Substring("Item".Length), out result);
		}

		private XtraPropertyInfo CreateXmlPropertyInfo(string name, Type propertyType, object val, bool isKey)
		{
			XtraPropertyInfo val2 = ((XmlXtraSerializer)this).CreateXtraPropertyInfo(name, propertyType, isKey, (Dictionary<string, string>)null);
			val2.Value = val;
			return val2;
		}

		private XtraPropertyInfo ReadInfo(XmlReader reader, bool skipZeroDepth)
		{
			reader.Read();
			if (reader.IsStartElement())
			{
				XtraPropertyInfo val = CreateXmlPropertyInfo(reader.Name, null, null, isKey: true);
				bool isEmptyElement = reader.IsEmptyElement;
				if (!skipZeroDepth || reader.Depth != 0)
				{
					while (reader.MoveToNextAttribute())
					{
						Type propertyType = null;
						if (reader.Name.EndsWith("_type", StringComparison.Ordinal))
						{
							propertyType = ((XmlXtraSerializer)this).ResolveType(reader.Value, ((XtraSerializer)this).ObjectConverterImpl);
							reader.MoveToNextAttribute();
						}
						XtraPropertyInfo val2 = CreateXmlPropertyInfo(reader.Name, propertyType, reader.Value, isKey: false);
						val.ChildProperties.Add(val2);
					}
				}
				if (isEmptyElement)
				{
					return val;
				}
				do
				{
					XtraPropertyInfo val3 = ReadInfo(reader, skipZeroDepth);
					if (val3 != null)
					{
						val.ChildProperties.Add(val3);
					}
				}
				while (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement);
				reader.Read();
				return val;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override Type ResolveType(string typeName, ObjectConverterImplementation converter)
		{
			Type type = converter.ResolveType(typeName);
			if (type != null)
			{
				return type;
			}
			return SafeTypeResolver.GetPrimitiveOrTrustedType(typeName, (IDictionary<string, Type>)null, (bool?)false);
		}

		protected override void WriteApplicationAttribute(string appName, XmlWriter tw)
		{
		}

		protected override void WriteStartDocument(XmlWriter tw)
		{
			tw.WriteStartDocument();
		}

		protected override void SerializeLevelCore(XmlWriter tw, IXtraPropertyCollection props)
		{
			//IL_0057: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (!props.IsSinglePass)
			{
				foreach (XtraPropertyInfo item in (IEnumerable)props)
				{
					XtraPropertyInfo val = item;
					if (!val.IsKey)
					{
						SerializeAttributeProperty(tw, val);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (XtraPropertyInfo item2 in (IEnumerable)props)
			{
				XtraPropertyInfo val2 = item2;
				if (val2.IsKey)
				{
					SerializeContentProperty(tw, val2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SerializeAttributeProperty(XmlWriter writer, XtraPropertyInfo p)
		{
			object obj = p.Value;
			if (obj != null)
			{
				Type type = obj.GetType();
				if (!type.IsPrimitive)
				{
					obj = ((XtraSerializer)this).ObjectConverterImpl.ObjectToString(obj);
				}
			}
			if (p.Value != null)
			{
				string value = default(string);
				if (((XmlXtraSerializer)this).ShouldSerializePropertyType(p, ref value))
				{
					writer.WriteAttributeString(p.Name + "_type", value);
				}
				writer.WriteAttributeString(p.Name, XmlXtraSerializer.XmlObjectToString(obj));
			}
		}

		private void SerializeContentProperty(XmlWriter writer, XtraPropertyInfo p)
		{
			if (((ICollection)p.ChildProperties).Count > 0 || IsCollectionItem(p.Name))
			{
				writer.WriteStartElement(p.Name.Replace("$", string.Empty));
				((XmlXtraSerializer)this).SerializeLevel(writer, p.ChildProperties);
				writer.WriteEndElement();
			}
		}

		protected override IXtraPropertyCollection DeserializeCore(Stream stream, string appName, IList objects)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IXtraPropertyCollection val = (IXtraPropertyCollection)new XtraPropertyCollection();
			XmlReader reader = ((XmlXtraSerializer)this).CreateReader(stream);
			XtraPropertyInfo val2 = ReadInfo(reader, skipZeroDepth: true);
			val.Add(val2);
			return val;
		}

		protected override XmlWriterSettings CreateXmlWriterSettings()
		{
			return new XmlWriterSettings
			{
				Indent = true,
				CheckCharacters = false,
				OmitXmlDeclaration = false,
				Encoding = Encoding.UTF8
			};
		}
	}
	public class TreeMapSerializationContext : SerializationContext
	{
		private static XtraPropertyInfo GetRootPropertyInfo(IXtraPropertyCollection store)
		{
			if (((ICollection)store).Count == 1)
			{
				IXtraPropertyCollection childProperties = store[0].ChildProperties;
				if (((ICollection)childProperties).Count != 1)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return childProperties[0];
			}
			throw new XmlException();
		}

		protected override int GetCollectionItemsCount(XtraPropertyInfo root)
		{
			return ((ICollection)root.ChildProperties).Count;
		}

		protected override object InvokeCreateCollectionItem(DeserializeHelper helper, string propertyName, XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			object owner = e.Owner;
			IXtraSupportDeserializeCollectionItem val = (IXtraSupportDeserializeCollectionItem)((owner is IXtraSupportDeserializeCollectionItem) ? owner : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val.CreateCollectionItem(propertyName, e);
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override object InvokeCreateContentPropertyValueMethod(DeserializeHelper helper, XtraItemEventArgs e, PropertyDescriptor prop)
		{
			object owner = e.Owner;
			IXtraSupportCreateContentPropertyValue val = (IXtraSupportCreateContentPropertyValue)((owner is IXtraSupportCreateContentPropertyValue) ? owner : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val.Create(e);
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override void InvokeAfterDeserialize(DeserializeHelper helper, object obj, XtraPropertyInfo bp, object value)
		{
			//IL_0017: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0021: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IXtraSupportAfterDeserialize val = (IXtraSupportAfterDeserialize)((obj is IXtraSupportAfterDeserialize) ? obj : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				bp.Value = value;
				val.AfterDeserialize(new XtraItemEventArgs((object)null, obj, (object)null, bp));
			}
		}

		protected override void DeserializeObjectsCore(DeserializeHelper helper, IList objects, IXtraPropertyCollection store, OptionsLayoutBase options)
		{
			if (objects.Count == 1)
			{
				object obj = objects[0];
				XtraObjectInfo val = (XtraObjectInfo)((obj is XtraObjectInfo) ? obj : null);
				if (val != null)
				{
					XtraPropertyInfo rootPropertyInfo = GetRootPropertyInfo(store);
					if (rootPropertyInfo == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					if (val.Name == rootPropertyInfo.Name)
					{
						helper.DeserializeObject(val.Instance, rootPropertyInfo.ChildProperties, options);
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			throw new XmlException();
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeCollectionItem(SerializeHelper helper, object owner, MethodInfo mi, XtraPropertyInfo itemProperty, object item, XtraItemEventArgs e)
		{
			IXtraSupportShouldSerializeCollectionItem val = (IXtraSupportShouldSerializeCollectionItem)((owner is IXtraSupportShouldSerializeCollectionItem) ? owner : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				itemProperty.Value = item;
				try
				{
					return val.ShouldSerializeCollectionItem(e);
				}
				finally
				{
					itemProperty.Value = null;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	public static class XtraSerializingHelper
	{
		[ThreadStatic]
		public static bool XtraSerializing;

		private static List<XtraObjectInfo> CreateSerializeInfo(InnerHierarchicalControlBase hierarchicalControl, string controlName)
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<XtraObjectInfo> list = new List<XtraObjectInfo>();
			list.Add(new XtraObjectInfo(controlName, (object)hierarchicalControl));
			return list;
		}

		private static bool TryReadXml(XmlReader reader)
		{
			try
			{
				return reader.Read();
			}
			catch (XmlException)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		private static bool TryReadAndOmitWhitespace(XmlReader reader)
		{
			do
			{
				if (!TryReadXml(reader))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			while (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Whitespace);
			return true;
		}

		public static void LoadLayoutFromStream(InnerHierarchicalControlBase hierarchicalControl, Stream stream, string controlName)
		{
			List<XtraObjectInfo> list = CreateSerializeInfo(hierarchicalControl, controlName);
			XtraSerializer val = (XtraSerializer)(object)new TreeMapXmlSerializer(controlName);
			val.DeserializeObjects((object)hierarchicalControl, (IList)list.ToArray(), stream, "", (OptionsLayoutBase)null);
		}

		public static void SaveLayoutToStream(InnerHierarchicalControlBase hierarchicalControl, Stream stream, string controlName)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Expected O, but got Unknown
			List<XtraObjectInfo> list = CreateSerializeInfo(hierarchicalControl, controlName);
			list.Add(new XtraObjectInfo("", (object)null));
			XtraSerializer val = (XtraSerializer)(object)new TreeMapXmlSerializer(controlName);
			val.SerializeObjects((object)hierarchicalControl, (IList)list.ToArray(), stream, "", (OptionsLayoutBase)null);
		}

		public static bool IsValidXml(Stream stream)
		{
			using XmlReader xmlReader = SafeXml.CreateReader(stream);
			if (!TryReadAndOmitWhitespace(xmlReader))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (xmlReader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.XmlDeclaration)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!TryReadAndOmitWhitespace(xmlReader))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (xmlReader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element || (xmlReader.Name != "TreeMapXmlSerializer" && xmlReader.Name != "SunburstXmlSerializer"))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
}
